### [1] [A Dynamical-Photometric Phase Space for Spiral Galaxies: Probing the Local Coupling Between Light and Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11264)
*Aritra Sanyal,Farook Rahaman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动力学—光度相空间（dynamical–photometric phase space），将盘星系（disc galaxies）中局域运动学量 X(R)=V(R)/R 与光度量 Y(R)=dlnI/dlnR 逐点对应。对SPARC数据库中的136个晚型星系测试，中位决定系数R²=0.930，并通过八项独立检验及5折交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盘星系中发光物质分布与局域引力场之间的相互作用蕴含超越全局标度关系（global scaling relations）的物理信息。现有框架难以在逐点（pointwise）层面同时刻画运动学与光度特征，需要建立新的表征工具。

Method: 定义以角频率 X=V/R=ω 和表面亮度对数斜率 Y=dlnI/dlnR 为轴的动力学—光度相空间；用平滑sigmoid关系式 Y=[a lnX+b]/(1+exp[k(X-X_trans)]) 统一描述重子主导内区与暗物质主导外区的双区行为。将该框架应用于SPARC数据库中136个晚型星系（倾角20°–89°，距离1–130 Mpc，恒星质量覆盖五个数量级）。

Result: 中位决定系数 R²=0.930；通过八项独立统计检验与5折交叉验证；转换参数 X_trans 可识别暗物质主导起始位置，样本中位转换半径 R_trans=5.40 kpc。

Conclusion: 所提出的相空间框架能够以统一解析形式描述从重子主导内盘到暗物质主导外盘的平滑过渡，并可靠提取暗物质主导起始半径，为盘星系动力学结构研究提供新工具。

Abstract: The interplay between luminous matter distribution and the local gravitational field within disc galaxies encodes physical information beyond that captured by global scaling relations. We introduce a \emph{dynamical--photometric phase space} defined by the kinematic variable $X(R)=V(R)/R$ and the photometric variable $Y(R)=\mathrm{d}\ln I/\mathrm{d}\ln R$, placing the local gravitational scale and the logarithmic surface brightness gradient into direct pointwise correspondence at each galactocentric radius $R$. The quantity $X=V/R=ω$ represents the angular frequency of circular motion and acts as a probe of the local mean mass density, while $Y$ measures the radial steepness of the stellar light distribution. The baryon-dominated inner disc is characterized by large negative $Y$, whereas the dark-matter-dominated outer region approaches $Y\rightarrow0$. This two-regime behaviour is described by the smooth sigmoid relation $Y=[a\ln X+b]/(1+\exp[k(X-X_{\rm trans})])$, which reduces to the logarithmic coupling $Y=a\ln X+b$ in the baryonic zone. We apply this framework to 136 late-type galaxies from the SPARC database, spanning inclinations $20^{\circ}$--$89^{\circ}$, distances $1$--$130$\,Mpc, and five decades in stellar mass. The median coefficient of determination is $R^{2}=0.930$. Statistical validation includes eight independent tests together with 5-fold cross-validation. The transition parameter $X_{\rm trans}$ identifies the onset of dark-matter dominance, corresponding to a median transition radius $R_{\rm trans}=5.40$\,kpc across the sample.

</details>


### [2] [Analysis of the equations of motion of fictitious matter in embedding theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11379)
*A. J. Ziyatdinov,S. A. Paston*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入理论（embedding theory）作为一种广义相对论的修正方案，通过将时空视为十维平坦背景空间中的四维曲面来替代暗物质（dark matter）解释，并分析了其静态解中壁（wall）、弦（string）和球（ball）三类虚构物质构型，证明了弦情形下Liouville方程解的旋转对称性，以及球情形下球对称假设下中心光滑解的唯一单参数族存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入理论为解释通常归因于暗物质的观测效应提供了广义相对论的一种可能修正框架。通过将嵌入理论中的运动方程改写为爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）加上额外虚构物质（fictitious matter）贡献的形式，作者旨在分析该理论的静态解，从而理解其物理意义与数学结构。

Method: 将嵌入理论中的运动方程重新表述为爱因斯坦方程与描述虚构物质的方程的组合，并将静态解约化为三种类型的虚构物质构型：壁型、弦型和球型。其中弦型情形最终归结为Liouville方程（Liouville equation），球型情形归结为其三维类比形式。随后分别分析这两种方程解的结构与性质。

Result: 对于弦型情形，证明了当密度贡献在无穷远处减小时，Liouville方程的所有解均具有旋转对称性。对于球型情形，证明了在球对称假设下，存在唯一的单参数解族，且该族解在中心处光滑。

Conclusion: 嵌入理论的静态解在弦和球两种构型下分别由Liouville方程及其三维类比方程描述，并获得了关于解对称性与唯一性的严格数学结果，为后续研究嵌入理论的物理应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Embedding theory is a possible modification of general relativity that provides a framework for explaining the observed effects typically attributed to dark matter. The idea of this modification is to consider our spacetime as a four-dimensional surface in a ten-dimensional flat ambient space. The equations of motion in embedding theory can be reformulated as a set of Einstein equations with the contribution of some additional fictitious matter and of equations describing this matter. We analyze static solutions of these equations, which are reduced to fictitious-matter configurations of the wall, string, and ball types. The string case is ultimately described by the Liouville equation, and the ball case is described by its three dimensional analogue. For the string case, we show that as the density contribution decreases at infinity, all solutions to the Liouville equation are rotationally symmetric. For the case of the ball, we show that under the assumption of spherical symmetry, there exists a unique one-parameter family of solutions that are smooth at the center.

</details>


### [3] [Imprints of core/cusp dark matter distributions on black hole signatures in galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11430)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Che-Yu Chen,Soroush Zare,Volker Perlick*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了由通用核心/尖点暗物质晕（core/cusp dark matter halo）驱动的静态球对称黑洞时空精确解族，并在弱引力场近似下推导了光环、ISCO等强场可观测量的解析修正，揭示了其由无量纲紧致度参数控制的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系环境中的宿主暗物质晕可以对黑洞强场可观测信号施加微弱但相干的修正，理解这些环境效应对于精确引力波天文学具有重要意义。

Method: 通过各向异性应力-能量张量（含非零径向压力）描述通用核心/尖点暗物质晕，从爱因斯坦方程自洽构造精确的静态球对称黑洞时空解族；随后对特征圆轨道进行微扰解析估计，并推导弱/强偏转角及环下沉物理相关的解析表达式。

Result: 领先阶强场修正由无量纲紧致度 $q_\gamma$ 控制而非总晕质量；对于尖点轮廓，光环和ISCO向外位移且轨道频率红移；对于核心轮廓，光环半径不变但频率和俘获碰撞参数仍携带有限环境修正。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕的内对数斜率 $\gamma$ 决定相对论区域探测到的暗物质量，环境修正对环下沉物理（准正则模红移和晚期波传播）产生可观测的微扰印记。

Abstract: In galactic environments, a host dark matter (DM) halo can imprint weak but coherent corrections on black hole (BH) strong-field observables. We construct an exact family of static and spherically symmetric BH spacetimes sourced by a generic core/cusp DM halo, described by an anisotropic stress-energy tensor with nonvanishing radial pressure. The resulting geometry is determined self-consistently from the Einstein equations for a broad $\{α,β,γ\}$ density profile, including the NFW, Moore, Hernquist, Jaffe, and core/cusp Dehnen models as special cases. We discuss the asymptotic structure, horizon location, curvature scale, and energy conditions of the corresponding geometries, emphasizing that the inner logarithmic slope $γ$ controls the amount of DM probed by the relativistic region. We then obtain perturbative analytic estimates for the characteristic circular geodesics, demonstrating that the leading strong-field corrections are controlled by the dimensionless compactness $q_γ$, rather than by the total halo mass alone. To leading order in $q_γ$, we derive analytic expressions for the light ring radius, angular frequency, critical impact parameter, Lyapunov exponent, innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) radius, and ISCO frequency. For cuspy profiles, the light ring and ISCO are displaced outward, while the corresponding orbital frequencies are redshifted; for cored profiles, the light ring radius is unchanged at this order, although its frequency and capture impact parameter still carry finite environmental corrections. We also investigate the weak- and strong-deflection angles and their dependence on the halo's inner structure. We further discuss how these environmental corrections may affect ringdown physics, in particular through the perturbative imprint of the halo on quasinormal-mode redshifts and late-time wave propagation.

</details>


### [4] [Torsional pseudo-inflation beyond Einstein-Cartan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11453)
*Fernando Izaurieta,Samuel Lepe,Cristian Quinzacara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了扭转（torsion）在早期宇宙论中的第二个角色——在爱因斯坦-卡特（Einstein–Cartan）反弹后接管暴胀（inflation）的职责。作者首先计算了这种机制的预算，发现其最多只能提供约0.46个e-folds，远低于暴胀所需。随后在具有非最小耦合的黎曼-卡特宇宙学中构造了一个由扭转凝聚体维持的精确de Sitter解，该解是动力学吸引子，其相位结束后整个图像接近普朗克尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转最早通过爱因斯坦-卡特反弹进入早期宇宙学（消除了奇点），随后有提议认为反弹后的膨胀可以承担暴胀的职责。本文以建设性的视角重新审视这一想法，旨在评估其可行性并探索扭转维持准de Sitter相位的可能性。

Method: 首先对Weyssenhoff自旋流体（spin fluid）在等熵源（barotropic source）w∈[0,1]下的加速窗口进行定量计算；然后在具有非最小耦合的黎曼-卡特宇宙学中，通过矢量和轴向两个分支研究扭转由耦合而非自旋供给的机制，并分析de Sitter解的稳定性与相位终止条件。

Result: 反弹后的加速窗口最多提供N_e≈0.46个e-folds，低于暴胀需求两个数量级。在轴向分支上构造了精确de Sitter解：恒定轴向扭转f₀、线性高斯-博内（Gauss–Bonnet）耦合及由Friedmann约束固定的势能V₀=6f₀²+5/2 v²。该解是同质动力学吸引子（特征值恰为-3H），相位在斜率U′漂移到5/16 V₀地板时终止，整个阶段发生在约H≳0.3（简约普朗克单位）的普朗克尺度附近。

Conclusion: 由自旋驱动的扭转暴胀机制原理上有效，但燃料按a⁻⁶稀释，远不足以满足暴胀需求。在其非最小耦合的替代方案中，矢量分支复现了观察上可行的Palatini暴胀，轴向分支则提供一个由扭转凝聚体维持的精确de Sitter吸引子，但该相位接近普朗克尺度，其微扰谱仍有待研究。

Abstract: Torsion entered early-universe cosmology twice: through the nonsingular Einstein--Cartan bounce, where the quantum spin of fermions halts the contraction, and through the proposal that the expansion following that bounce could take over the duties of cosmic inflation. We revisit the second idea in a constructive spirit. First we compute its budget: for a Weyssenhoff spin fluid with barotropic source, $w\in\left[0,1\right]$, the accelerated window that follows the bounce spans $N_e=\ln\left[4/\left(1+3w\right)\right]/3\left(1-w\right)\leq\tfrac{1}{3}\ln4\simeq0.46$ e-folds, and positive spatial curvature only shrinks it. The mechanism is sound; its torsional ``fuel'' dilutes as $a^{-6}$, and the engine stops two orders of magnitude short of inflationary needs. We then ask what torsion would need in order to sustain a quasi-de Sitter phase, and give a two-branch answer in Riemann--Cartan cosmology with nonminimal couplings, where torsion is fed by the couplings rather than by spin. The vectorial branch reproduces Palatini inflation, observationally alive. On the axial branch we exhibit an exact de Sitter solution sustained by a torsion condensate: constant axial torsion $f_{0}$, a linear Gauss--Bonnet coupling, and a potential fixed to $V_{0}=6f_{0}^{2}+\tfrac{5}{2}v^{2}$ by the Friedmann constraint. The solution is an attractor of the homogeneous dynamics, with eigenvalue exactly $-3H$. The phase ends when the slope $U^{\prime}$, a decreasing function of the roll speed at fixed flat potential, drifts down to the floor $5/16V_{0}$ and the condensate switches itself off; consistency of the effective theory pins the whole episode near the Planck scale, $H\gtrsim0.3$ in reduced Planck units. We studied this branch at background level; its perturbation spectrum remains open.

</details>


### [5] [Investigation of Circular Photon Orbits in Naked Singularity Spacetimes from a Geometric Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11456)
*Qing-Rong Xie,Chen-Kai Qiao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了裸奇点时空（naked singularity spacetime）中圆形光子轨道（circular photon orbit）的性质，采用光学几何（optical geometry）框架及其内蕴曲率方法，证明了任意球对称裸奇点时空中圆形光子轨道的总数必为偶数（N=2k），其中稳定与不稳定轨道各占k条，并给出了数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆形光子轨道在引力理论和天文观测中均扮演关键角色，但裸奇点时空中此类轨道的性质尚未被充分探索，值得深入研究。

Method: 采用几何方法研究圆形光子轨道，核心框架为光学几何及其内蕴曲率（intrinsic curvature）。通过内蕴测地曲率（intrinsic geodesic curvature）分析轨道的存在性，进而考察稳定与不稳定轨道的数量及其拓扑不变量（topological invariant）。

Result: 对于任意球对称裸奇点时空，圆形光子轨道的总数为偶数整数（N=2k），其中k条为稳定轨道、k条为不稳定轨道，二者比例相等。该结论获得了严格的数学证明。

Conclusion: 将裸奇点时空与无裸奇点的紧凑天体时空（compact object spacetime）进行对比发现，二者关于圆形光子轨道数量的结论一致，表明圆形光子测地线的数量主要由事件视界（event horizon）的存在与否所决定，而非时空奇异性本身。

Abstract: Circular photon orbits play a pivotal role in both gravitational theories and astronomical observations. However, the properties of circular photon orbits in naked singularity spacetimes remain insufficiently explored and deserve in-depth investigation. The present work is dedicated to a comprehensive study on the features of circular photon orbits in naked singularity spacetimes. Notably, a geometric approach is employed to investigate these orbits, in which the framework of optical geometry together with its intrinsic curvatures plays crucial roles. We analyze the existence of circular photon orbits through the intrinsic geodesic curvature, and we then investigate the number of stable and unstable circular orbits, as well as the topological invariant associated with circular photon orbits. By examining various classes of naked singularity spacetimes, we obtain a general conclusion regarding circular photon orbits that holds for arbitrary spherically symmetric naked singularity spacetimes: the total number of circular photon orbits is an even integer ($N = 2k$), consisting of $k$ stable orbits and $k$ unstable orbits in equal proportion. A mathematical proof of this conclusion is also provided in the present work. Furthermore, a comparison with other categories of spacetimes reveals that the conclusions regarding the count of circular photon orbits in naked singularity spacetimes agree with those obtained for compact object spacetimes without naked singularities, indicating that the number of circular photon geodesics is primarily governed by the presence of event horizons rather than spacetime singularities.
  Keywords: Circular Photon Orbit, Naked Singularity Spacetime, Optical Geometry, Geometric Curvatures

</details>


### [6] [Pressure profile bounds from relaxing the TOV equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11619)
*Christopher Simmonds,Matt Visser*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文针对广义相对论完美流体球（general relativistic perfect fluid spheres）的内部压强剖面（internal pressure profiles）推导了多个新颖且相当一般的界，这些界通过对TOV常微分方程组（system of ODEs）进行多种方式松弛，得到若干不同的微分不等式。文中基于不同强度与普适性的输入假设（如密度为正、密度有界、密度单调或平均密度单调）建立了多个直截了当但并非平凡的结果，并将其置于前人工作的历史框架中进行比较与互相关联分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为广义相对论完美流体球的内部压强分布建立更普适的理论界。作者希望探讨界（bound）强度、输入假设（input assumptions）的弱度以及分析可处理性（tractability）三者之间的权衡关系，从而完善关于此类流体球结构的已有数学约束。

Method: 方法上，作者通过多种不同方式对TOV（Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff）方程组这一常微分方程组进行松弛处理，将其转化为若干不同的微分不等式（differential inequalities），进而基于不同的密度性质假设（正性、有界性、单调性以及平均密度的单调性）分别推导出多个独立的压强界。

Result: 论文得到了一系列新的、一般化的内部压强上界或下界，每个界分别依赖不同的输入条件：仅依赖密度的正性、密度的有界性、密度的单调性或平均密度的单调性。这些界之间存在相互关联，并已被放置在历史研究框架中加以定位。

Conclusion: 作者指出，新得到的界在强度、输入假设的弱度与分析可处理性之间存在经典权衡，且与以往相关工作相互交织、互为补充，为该领域提供了一套系统化的比较框架。由于摘要未给出具体数值或定理形式，详细结论需见全文。

Abstract: We develop several new and quite general bounds on the internal pressure profiles of general relativistic perfect fluid spheres --- based on various ways of relaxing the TOV system of ODEs to obtain several distinct differential inequalities. There is, as usual, a trade-off between strength of the bound, weakness of the input assumptions, and tractability of the analysis. Specifically we shall develop several straightforward but nontrivial bounds that variously depend only on the positivity of density, the boundedness of density, the monotonicity of density, or the monotonicity of the average density. We shall carefully place these new bounds within the historical framework of previous efforts in this regard, and explore the ways in which they are inter-related.

</details>

*The LIGO Scientific Collaboration,the Virgo Collaboration,the KAGRA Collaboration,A. G. Abac,A. Abe,I. Abouelfettouh,F. Acernese,K. Ackley,A. Adam,S. Adhicary,D. Adhikari,R. X. Adhikari,V. K. Z. Zieba,A. B. Zimmerman,L. Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第四次观测运行（O4）第二和第三部分的数据，对超轻玻色子（ultralight bosons）施加约束：通过隐藏马尔可夫模型（HMM）追踪方案搜索双黑洞并合残余天体周围的超轻矢量玻色子云产生的长时瞬变引力波，未发现信号；同时基于三个双黑洞系统的高自旋推断，排除了特定质量范围的标量及矢量玻色子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻玻色子是暗物质（dark matter）和超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）物理的重要候选者，可通过黑洞超辐射（superradiance）效应形成玻色子云，产生可观测的引力波信号。本工作旨在利用最新的引力波观测数据对这些玻色子的质量范围施加更严格的约束。

Method: 采用两种互补的方法：（1）针对双黑洞并合残余体（来自事件GW250114和GW250207）实施定向搜索（directed search），利用隐藏马尔可夫模型（hidden Markov model, HMM）追踪方案探测长时瞬变引力波；（2）利用事件GW240515、GW241113和GW241225_08中三个双黑洞体系成分黑洞的推断高自旋值，推导超轻标量（scalar）和矢量（vector）玻色子的质量约束。（方法中）还包括对探测阈值对应的1%虚假警报概率（false alarm probability）设置搜索灵敏度。

Result: 两个定向搜索目标（GW250114和GW250207的残余黑洞）均未发现信号。在1%虚假警报概率阈值下，以超过90%置信度排除矢量玻色子质量范围[2.80, 3.95]×10^-13 eV。基于三个双黑洞事件的高自旋推断，在10^5年黑洞年龄假设下，以90%置信度排除标量玻色子质量范围[1.39, 6.94]×10^-13 eV和矢量玻色子质量范围[0.32, 14.4]×10^-13 eV。

Conclusion: 本工作显著扩展了对超轻玻色子质量参数的探测覆盖，综合引力波直接搜索与高自旋约束两种途径，为超轻玻色子参数空间提供了更严格的限制。约束范围对黑洞年龄假设具有依赖性，未来更精确的年龄估计可进一步收紧结论。

Abstract: We present constraints on ultralight bosons using binary black hole mergers observed in the second and third parts of the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run. Directed searches are conducted for long-transient gravitational waves from ultralight vector boson clouds around merger remnants, using a hidden-Markov-model (HMM) tracking scheme. We target the remnant black holes formed in the binary coalescences that produced GW250114 and GW250207. We find no evidence for such signals from either target. Estimating our search sensitivity at a threshold corresponding to a 1% false alarm probability, we thus disfavor vector boson masses in the range of $[2.80, 3.95]\times 10^{-13}$ eV with greater than 90% confidence. In addition, we derive constraints on ultralight scalar and vector bosons from the inferred high spins of the constituent black holes in three binaries, using events GW240515, GW241113, and GW241225_08. The excluded mass ranges in this approach depend on the assumed black-hole ages. At $10^5$ years, corresponding to typical dynamically formed binaries, we exclude scalar and vector bosons in the ranges $[1.39, 6.94]\times 10^{-13}$ eV and $[0.32, 14.4]\times 10^{-13}$ eV at 90% confidence, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Trapped surface formation via radial boosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11706)
*Nathan Thomas Carruth*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文通过称为“径向提升”（radial boost）的尺度变换，展示了可以从经典的局部小数据存在性结果推导出动量陷获面（trapped surface）的动力形成，推翻了此前认为必须依赖大数据存在性结果的假设，并阐明了短脉冲假设（short-pulse ansatz）本质上是一种几何光学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究（Christodoulou、Klainerman、Rodnianski、Luk、An 等人）已经得到了爱因斯坦真空方程中陷获面动力形成的若干结果。由于 Christodoulou 和 Klainerman 证明的闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）稳定性意味着“小”初始数据不会产生奇点或陷获面，学界一直假设动力陷获面的形成需要非线性双曲型爱因斯坦场方程的大数据存在性结果。本论文旨在挑战这一假设，证明小数据结果足以产生类似结论。

Method: 作者引入一种称为“径向提升”（radial boost）的尺度变换（scaling），利用经典的局部小数据存在性结果来构造动力陷获面形成的结果。该方法绕开了对大数据存在性结果的依赖，并在该缩放框架下进一步证明所谓的入射剪切（incoming shear）在最高阶满足线性波动方程（linear wave equation），从而完整地将短脉冲假设阐释为一种几何光学（geometric optics）假设。

Result: 论文成功地表明，通过径向提升缩放，可以从经典的局部小数据存在性结果获得与 Christodoulou 及 Klainerman-Rodnianski 在性质上类似的动力陷获面形成结果。同时，还阐明了短脉冲假设作为几何光学假设的完整机制，即入射剪切在最高阶服从线性波动方程。

Conclusion: 该工作推翻了大动量陷获面形成必然依赖大数据存在性结果的既有认识，表明小数据局部存在性结果经由径向提升缩放即可产生定性相似的陷获面形成结论，且这一方法为短脉冲假设提供了几何光学的严格诠释。

Abstract: Work of Christodoulou, Klainerman, Rodnianski, Luk, An, and others has provided a number of results on the dynamical formation of trapped surfaces in vacuum solutions to the Einstein field equations. Since the stability of Minkowski spacetime as proved by Christodoulou and Klainerman implies that `small' initial data to the Einstein vacuum equations must give rise to a solution with no singularities, and hence no trapped surfaces, it has been assumed that dynamical formation of trapped surfaces requires a (hard) large-data existence result for the nonlinear hyperbolic Einstein field equations. In this paper we show, to the contrary, that dynamical trapped surface formation results qualitatively similar to those of Christodoulou and Klainerman-Rodnianski can be obtained from (classical) local, small-data existence results via a scaling we term a radial boost. In the process we also fully elucidate the short-pulse ansatz as a geometric optics ansatz by showing that, in this scaled picture, the so-called incoming shear satisfies, at highest order, a linear wave equation.

</details>


### [9] [Vishveshwara's waveform revisited: insights from a Keldysh quasinormal mode expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11823)
*Jérémy Besson,José Luis Jaramillo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过双曲化（hyperboloidal）方法将史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）上的Vishveshwara线性散射问题重新表述为非自伴动力学框架，利用Keldysh共振展开在双正交的准正则模（quasinormal mode）系中分析全时域波形，揭示了第二峰值与基模及泛音的对应关系，以及第一峰值由代数学特殊模（algebraically special mode）和支割线（branch cut）控制的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正则模激活（quasinormal mode activation）的时间演化规律在非线性演化中已有广泛观察，但其潜在机制尚不清晰。作者希望通过线性散射框架重新审视这个问题，以揭示时域波形中峰值结构与准正则模激发之间的一致性。

Method: 采用双曲化方法（hyperboloidal approach）将散射问题置于非自伴动力学（non-selfadjoint dynamics）框架中，该框架允许在由准正则模构成的双正交（bi-orthogonal）系统内进行Keldysh共振展开，从而对全时域波形中的模态激活过程进行定量分析。

Result: 计算发现Vishveshwara波形中的第二个峰值与基模（fundamental mode）及第一泛音（first overtones）密切相关，与非线性演化中的振铃波形（ringdown waveform）模式一致。第一峰值对于偶宇称Zerilli扰动的控制完全来自代数学特殊模和邻近的支割线，而该现象在奇宇称Regge-Wheeler扰动中不存在。

Conclusion: 这些定性特征对初始数据的改变具有稳健性，可能为理解非线性演化中振铃激活时间（ringdown activation time）的潜在机制提供洞见。

Abstract: We revisit Vishveshwara's linear scattering on a Schwarzschild black hole to study the evolution of quasinormal mode activation along the full time-domain waveform. Specifically, by adopting a hyperboloidal approach we cast the scattering problem in a non-selfadjoint dynamics setting where a (Keldysh) resonant expansion in a bi-orthogonal system of quasinormal modes can be readily performed. The calculation reveals the neat correlation of the second peak in Vishveshwara's waveform to the fundamental mode and first overtones, closely following the ringdown waveform pattern of non-linear evolutions. The first peak is completely controlled, for even (Zerilli) perturbations, by the algebraically special mode and the nearby branch cut. This phenomenon is absent for odd (Regge-Wheeler) perturbations. These qualitative features are robust under change of initial data and, we argue, might provide insight into the mechanisms underlying the ringdown activation time in non-linear evolutions.

</details>


### [10] [A Nonsingular Logarithmic Bouncing Cosmology in $f(R,T)$ Gravity with Thermodynamic Viability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11824)
*Anjani,Pankaj Kumar,S. H. Shekh,Milan Srivastava*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在修改后的 $f(R,T)$ 引力框架内，通过假设对数时间依赖的尺度因子，提出了一个无奇点的反弹宇宙学模型，并研究了多种模型参数和物质—几何耦合参数下的动力学演化、能量条件违逆、经典稳定性及热力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造一个在修改引力框架下能够避免初始奇点的无奇异反弹宇宙学模型，以替代标准宇宙学中大爆炸奇点所引发的问题，并验证模型的可行性。

Method: 在空间平坦的Friedmann–Robertson–Walker时空背景下，采用修改后的 $f(R,T)$ 引力理论，假设对数时间依赖的尺度因子以实现从收缩相到膨胀相的平滑过渡；通过分析哈勃参数、减速参数、能量密度、压强、有效状态方程参数、声速平方及广义第二定律等来检验模型。

Result: 模型成功实现了反弹行为；能量条件违逆被证实发生；声速平方在允许参数空间内保持正值，表明经典稳定性；获得了物质—几何耦合参数的宇宙学约束，并与已有的致密天体约束兼容；广义第二定律显示总熵产生率在收缩相为负、膨胀相为正，但在反弹点出现奇异性。

Conclusion: 该反弹模型在修改引力框架下具有可行的动力学、稳定性及参数兼容性，但在反弹点热力学描述失效，表明标准热力学框架在该过渡阶段需要推广或修正。

Abstract: We present a nonsingular bouncing cosmological model in the framework of modified $f(R,T)$ gravity within a spatially flat Friedmann--Robertson--Walker universe. A logarithmic time-dependent scale factor is assumed to realize a smooth transition from a contracting phase to an expanding phase without encountering an initial singularity. Based on this assumption, the dynamical evolution of the Hubble parameter, deceleration parameter, energy density, and pressure is obtained for various choices of the model and the matter--geometry coupling parameter to confirm the occurrence of a successful bounce. The effective equation of state parameter is examined to characterize the cosmic fluid during different evolutionary phases. The violation of energy conditions, necessary for the realization of the bouncing behavior, is also discussed. The stability of the model is investigated using the squared speed of sound and is found to remain positive within the allowed parameter space, indicating classical stability. Furthermore, constraints on the matter--geometry coupling parameter are obtained by demanding positive energy density, negative pressure, and a viable cosmological evolution. The obtained cosmological constraint on the coupling parameter is also shown to be compatible with the currently available compact-object constraints. The thermodynamic behavior of the model is examined by testing the generalized second law of thermodynamics. The total entropy production rate remains negative during the contracting phase and changes its sign to positive during the expanding phase. However, it becomes singular at the bouncing point, reflecting the breakdown of the standard thermodynamic description during the transition phase.

</details>


### [11] [Taiji Resolving Power for the Transverse Scalar Mode of Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11852)
*Bo-Xuan Ge,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了太极（Taiji）引力波探测器在已识别的高信噪比张量啁啾信号中，能够分辨的最小共传播横标量分量。以一周年基准啁啾和张量信噪比ρ_T=1000为例，发现太极可在全天中位水平排除ε_b≳0.532%的横标量应变分数，且阈值按ρ_T^{-1}缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察引力波探测器太极对额外标量极化分量的分辨能力，特别是其在强大张量信号背景下所能探测的最小标量成分比例。

Given a specific theory of gravity, in particular the Einstein-Cartan-Sciama-Kibble one, we evaluate the conditions for obtaining a smooth junction. Abstract: We report a combined rheology and x-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) study of the structural and mechanical relaxation of a ductile, nanocolloidal glass following the cessation of shear flow. After the glass is sheared to 300% strain at various shear rates and then held at fixed strain, the stress undergoes a protracted, quasi-logarithmic decay with hold time that depends weakly on the initial strain rate. Recovery rheology measurements reveal that this stress relaxation is accompanied by a logarithmic decrease in the elastic component of the recoverable strain; hence, the rates of decrease of the stress and recoverable strain are proportional. XPCS measurements during the stress relaxation reveal dynamics dominated by a convection-like backflow that is divided into two dynamically distinct regions indicative of banded motion. In one region, the flow can be modeled by an affine strain, while in the other region the glass moves as a plug while undergoing slow, glassy relaxation. The rates of these dynamics approximately track the rate of loss of recoverable strain, indicating this motion is the predominant microscopic mechanism driving the conversion of recoverable to unrecoverable strain during stress relaxation. In contrast, XPCS measurements during strain recovery reveal purely affine flow with no evidence of heterogeneity and with strain rates that agree quantitatively with the rheometry measurements. Together, these results provide a unified microscopic picture connecting the evolving internal dynamics of a ductile glass to its macroscopic mechanical relaxation following flow cessation.

Abstract: An essential component of food items like mayonnaise and salad dressing is hen egg yolk. Phosvitin (PVT) is a phosphoprotein, which exists in the granules of this hen egg yolk which stabilizes the food emulsion by preventing phase separation. To understand, how this protein is adsorbed at the interface of water and edible oil in the presence and absence of lipids is essential for improved control in food production. To monitor the kinetics of this adsorption, the dynamic interfacial tension has been determined in the current investigation. The drop in interfacial tension over time indicates the adsorption of protein at interface which is enhanced on increasing the concentration of protein in water phase. However, at higher concentration, the positive activation energy hinders the adsorption process resulting a saturated interfacial tension. The dilation rheology of the macromolecular film at this oil-water interface shows the elastic nature of the film to be greater than the viscous nature, indicating the formation of a soft gel film. At low concentration, the zwitterionic lipid, 1-palmitoyl-2-oleoyl-sn-glycero 3-phosphocholine (POPC), promotes this protein adsorption at the interface. Interestingly, at high concentration, the lipid overtakes the interface removing the protein from there. The lipid-protein composite film again shows the nature of a soft gel. The non-monotonic effects of lipids on assembly of protein at the water-edible oil interface is an important observation to optimize the composition of relevant food products.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统如细菌和细胞会发生生长或降解，导致质量守恒（mass conservation）的微弱破缺，而传统相分离理论通常基于严格的质量守恒假设。作者旨在探究这种弱非保守效应对活性物质中相分离行为的影响，为理解自然界中普遍存在的非守恒生物体系提供理论框架。

Method: 作者将反应项纳入最小连续模型以描述弱非保守效应，并采用数值模拟与线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）系统研究相分离行为。同时通过关联函数（correlation function）与静态结构因子（static structure factor）的定量分析，以及对所得相态的定性分析来表征稳态特征。

Result: 研究发现甚至微弱的非保守反应即可使相分离停止粗化并稳定非平衡微相分离态；随活性增加，系统经历从连通迷宫状图案到蠕虫状结构再到孤立液滴的形态转变。定量分析揭示了具有明确稳态特征长度的存在，定性分析表明非保守反应主要促进微相分离并增强局部六方有序性（hexagonal ordering），而活性主要控制畴形态。

Conclusion: 弱质量守恒破缺会从根本上改变活性相分离的非线性粗化动力学，为理解相关系统中的图案形成（pattern formation）提供了最小化理论框架。

Abstract: Biological systems such as bacteria and cells undergo growth or degradation, resulting in weak violations of mass conservation. We investigate how such weak non-conservative dynamics affect phase separation in scalar active matter by incorporating a reaction term into a minimal continuum model. Through numerical simulations and linear stability analysis, we show that even weak non-conservative reactions arrest coarsening and stabilize nonequilibrium microphase-separated states. With increasing activity, the system undergoes a morphological transition from interconnected labyrinthine patterns to worm-like structures and eventually to isolated droplets. Quantitative analysis of the correlation function and static structure factor reveals a well-defined steady-state characteristic length. Qualitative analysis of the resulting phases shows that the non-conservative reaction primarily promotes microphase separation and enhances local hexagonal ordering, while activity predominantly controls the domain morphology. Our results demonstrate that weak violations of mass conservation fundamentally alter the nonlinear coarsening dynamics of active phase separation and provide a minimal framework for understanding pattern formation in related systems.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁相互作用为控制和组织主动物质（active matter）提供了多功能且强有力的工具，其自然存在于趋磁细菌等生物系统，也可工程化构建于合成平台。系统梳理偶极型主动粒子的实验与理论研究，有助于理解其自组织机制并推动相关应用发展。

Conclusion: 文章指出磁主动系统在可编程材料、生物医学驱动及非平衡物理等领域的设计、控制与应用方面既存在挑战也蕴含机遇。

Abstract: Magnetic interactions provide a versatile and powerful tool for controlling and organizing active matter, where individual units continuously consume energy to drive autonomous motion. These interactions arise naturally in biological systems, such as magnetotactic bacteria, and can be engineered into synthetic platforms, including colloidal microswimmers, magnetic nanoparticles, and macroscopic granular robots. This review focuses on active, self-propelled particles that carry an intrinsic magnetic dipole moment, powered by their own energy consumption rather than driven by external fields; here, the dipole moment mediates interactions and self-organization, not propulsion. We survey experimental and theoretical studies across all length scales, showing how dipolar interactions shape single-particle dynamics, collective behavior, and self-organization. We discuss models incorporating pairwise dipolar forces and confinement, and examine emergent phenomena such as chaining, swarming, and tunable pattern formation. We close by outlining challenges and opportunities in the design, control, and application of magnetic active systems, from programmable materials and biomedical actuation to nonequilibrium physics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究使用二维竞争相互作用模型，其低温相由单粒子厚度的条带组成。作者沿七条加热等容线（heating isochore）进行分子动力学模拟，并采用热力学、取向性、动力学以及基于图的（graph-based）可观测量进行系统分析。

Abstract: The melting of stripe-forming systems involves changes in connectivity that are not fully captured by conventional structural and orientational descriptors. We investigate a two-dimensional model with competing interactions whose low-temperature phase consists of one-particle-thick stripes. Molecular dynamics simulations along seven heating isochores are analyzed using thermodynamic, orientational, dynamical, and graph-based observables. Heating produces a multistage reconstruction in which the loss of stripe alignment and the reorganization of filament connectivity occur over distinct temperature ranges. Density controls whether the disordered filaments fragment into finite polymer-like clusters or remain joined in a dynamically fluid, system-spanning network. The topological observables distinguish these outcomes, which are not resolved by the thermodynamic and orientational responses alone. Thus, the same ordered stripe microphase can melt into topologically distinct fluids selected by density.

Abstract: We present \texttt{rcpgenerator}, an openly available code for generating $d$-dimensional dense, disordered, non-overlapping close packings from an arbitrary prescribed list of particle diameters. Typically, particle coordinates are advanced with a single, global step size, which must shrink as the size ratio $S\equiv D_{\max}/D_{\min}$ grows, generally stalling the optimization. Adam instead gives each coordinate its own adaptive step size, stabilizing the optimization time across a broader range of $S$. We demonstrate this in three-dimensional periodic tests that reach $S\sim5\times10^{5}$ for a continuous lognormal distribution ($N\sim10^{6}$ diameters) and particle numbers up to $N\approx5.6\times10^{6}$ for power-law distributions, with the densest packings reaching $φ\simeq0.87$, each completed in minutes to hours on a multicore machine. Additionally, results are commensurate with multimodal packing densities measured in vibrated-bed experiments. Abstract: Accelerated aging is widely used to study the long-term behavior of materials within laboratory time scales, particularly for materials exposed to solvent environments over extended periods. This is especially important for additively manufactured (AM) polymers, whose increasing use in naval and commercial undersea applications requires reliable methodologies for assessing durability under in-service conditions. A common approach relies on elevating the temperature below the glass transition or melting temperature to accelerate degradation. However, the temperature limits for accelerated aging of AM polymers remain poorly understood, particularly because temperatures beyond a threshold may activate deformation and degradation mechanisms that are absent under service conditions. To address this gap, this paper investigates fused deposition modeling (FDM) Acrylonitrile Butadiene Styrene (ABS) exposed to saltwater and deionized (DI) water to establish a temperature threshold for accelerated aging in aqueous environments and propose a methodology for determining such thresholds. Controlled geometries and varying print directions were employed to explicitly probe the underlying mechanisms. We show that samples exposed to temperatures above the threshold exhibit pronounced shrinkage and warping along the printing direction due to the relaxation of process-induced internal stresses. These observations establish an accelerated-aging temperature threshold of 50$^\circ$C for ABS, beyond which additional mechanisms absent under service conditions become active. Additionally, the resulting geometric distortions are masked in thick geometries but become highly pronounced in thin structures. Moreover, solvent ionic content strongly influences water uptake, with saltwater reaching saturation in 1 day, whereas DI water did not reach saturation even after 30 days and exhibited greater mass uptake.

Abstract: The Tsallis $q$-sum is one of the fundamental nonlinear composition laws of nonextensive statistical mechanics. Although it has been extensively investigated in continuous settings, its implications for finite mathematical structures remain less explored. Here we investigate this question by replacing ordinary additive laws with the Tsallis $q$-sum. We first introduce an axiomatic $q$-cardinality of finite sets satisfying a nonlinear additivity principle. Existence and uniqueness are established, leading to an explicit expression that continuously recovers the classical cardinality as $q\to1$. We then propose a nonlinear matrix composition induced by the same deformation and establish its principal algebraic properties, including associativity, the neutral element, and a characterization of its noncommutativity in terms of the ordinary matrix commutator. An illustrative example over $M_2(\mathbb Z_6)$ shows how the deformation can modify the center of a finite matrix algebra when $1-q$ is a zero divisor. These results indicate that the Tsallis $q$-sum provides a natural mechanism for constructing nonlinear finite mathematical structures and connects nonextensive statistical mechanics with finite algebra and nonlinear mathematical physics.

Abstract: The configurational partition function is a central object in classical equilibrium statistical mechanics, yet its inverse characterization remains largely unexplored. In this letter, we study the inverse problem of identifying smooth one-dimensional potentials that produce the Gaussian configurational partition function of the harmonic oscillator. Under suitable admissibility conditions, we prove that the partition function uniquely determines the pushforward measure generated by the potential and derive an equivalent characterization through a simple relation between its inverse branches. We establish a rigidity result showing that the harmonic oscillator is the only smooth symmetric single-well potential compatible with the Gaussian configurational partition function. We also demonstrate that this uniqueness is lost for multi-well potentials due to the possible rearrangement of inverse branches producing identical measures. Our results reveal a geometric structure underlying configurational statistics and provide a new perspective on inverse problems in statistical mechanics.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对ω光致产生反应的自旋密度矩阵元测量在较大的-t区域以及高能区间存在空白，尤其缺乏极化矩阵元的提取；同时现有数据主要停留在核子共振区，尚不足以对Regge理论模型的预测施加严格约束。

Method: 使用杰斐逊实验室的CLAS谱仪测量γp→pω反应，通过ω介子主导的带电衰变道ω→π⁺π⁻π⁰进行探测；在不同入射光子能量区间内给出t依赖结果的精细分箱（fine binning）测量，并分别在Adair坐标系中呈现角度依赖的微分截面与自旋密度矩阵元，再将新数据与基于Regge理论（Regge theory）的模型预测进行比较。

Result: 所有自旋密度矩阵元在-t>0.6 GeV²区域内均为首次测量；新数据与此前CLAS结果一致，但将能量覆盖范围从核子共振区（W∈[2.45,3.25] GeV及W∈[1.95,2.83] GeV）扩展至Regge区间；与Regge理论模型的比较表明，新数据对这些模型施加了更为严格的约束。

Conclusion: 该测量填补了ω光致产生自旋观测量的高动量转移区域空白，为评估和改善Regge理论框架下ω介子产生机制的理解提供了更高精度的实验基准，暗示现有模型需进一步调整以适应新的约束条件。

Abstract: We report on the differential cross sections d$σ$/d$t$, the unpolarized spin-density matrix elements $ρ^0_{00}$, $ρ^0_{1-1}$, Re\,$ρ^0_{10}$, and the first extraction of the polarized elements Im\,$ρ^3_{10}$, Im\,$ρ^3_{1-1}$ for the reaction $γp\to pω$ using the CLAS spectrometer at Jefferson Laboratory. The $ω$~mesons were detected in their dominant charged decay mode, $ω\to π^+π^-π^0$, and all $t$-dependent results are presented in a fine binning for incident photon energies between 2.73 and 5.16~GeV (corresponding to the center-of-mass energy range $W \in [\,2.45,3.25\,]$~GeV). All matrix elements are first measurements for $-t > 0.6$~GeV$^2$. Moreover, differential cross sections d$σ$/d(cos\,$Θ_{\rm \,c.m.}^{\,ω}$) and the corresponding angle-dependent unpolarized spin-density matrix elements in the Adair frame are presented for the incident photon energy range 1.56--3.80~GeV (corresponding to $W \in [\,1.95,2.83\,]$~GeV). These new $ω$~photoproduction data are consistent with earlier CLAS results but extend the energy range well beyond the nucleon resonance region into the Regge regime. The comparison with Regge-theory-based model predictions shows that the new data impose more stringent constraints on our understanding of $ω$~photoproduction.

</details>

### [42] [Unifying Physical Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11585)
*Cyrill Bösch,Yigithan Gediz,Hakan Türeci*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文基于伴随方法（adjoint method）提出了一个统一理论框架，阐明了物理计算系统在何种条件下可以在自身硬件上精确计算其性能的梯度，从而避免了通过数字孪生（digital twin）进行反向传播时面临的模型-现实差距（model-reality gap）问题。该框架覆盖线性和非线性系统，并推广到非厄米、非互易系统，为多种已有的物理学习算法提供了统一的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理计算系统利用器件动力学进行计算，但其基于梯度的优化面临挑战：通过数字孪生进行反向传播存在模型-现实差距。尽管已有少量理论和实验研究提出了设备上梯度计算（on-device gradient computation）的方案，但缺乏一个统一理论来识别物理系统何时能够计算自身性能的梯度。

Method: 作者基于伴随方法建立统一框架，推导了物理系统在同一硬件上生成精确梯度所需的伴随场（adjoint field）的充分条件。对于线性系统，在保持互易性（reciprocity）的前提下允许耗散或增益；对于非线性轨迹系统，充分条件是线性化系统的互易性以及时间反转镜（time-reversal mirror）的存在。此外，作者提出互易性仅是更一般的交织条件（intertwining condition）的最简实例，该条件将精确梯度计算扩展到非厄米、非互易系统。

Result: 该框架统一了平衡传播（Equilibrium Propagation）、哈密顿回波反向传播（Hamiltonian echo backpropagation）、完全前向模式训练（fully forward mode training）以及集成光子与自由空间光学系统中的原位梯度方法（in situ gradient methods）。算法上，非线性情形需要无穷小微扰（infinitesimal nudging），而线性系统允许有限振幅实验。进一步推广包括时变参数、Onsager互易动力学以及非线性PT对称薛定谔方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为精确的物理学习算法（physical learning algorithms）提供了统一的理论基础，并为在各类物理系统中构建此类算法提供了模板，解决了物理计算系统中梯度计算缺乏统一理论的问题。

Abstract: Physical computing systems exploit device dynamics for computation, but their gradient-based optimization is challenging: backpropagation through a digital twin suffers from model-reality gap. On-device gradient computation could resolve this issue, and a handful of theoretical and experimental studies have proposed ways to achieve it. Yet a unifying theory identifying when a physical system can compute the gradient of its own performance has been missing. Here we develop such a unification, based on the adjoint method: we identify sufficient conditions under which the adjoint field required for formally exact gradients can be generated on the same hardware that performs the computation. Linear and nonlinear systems obey fundamentally different conditions: for linear systems damping or gain is admissible provided reciprocity is preserved. For nonlinear trajectory systems the sufficient conditions are reciprocity of the linearized system and the existence of a time-reversal mirror. Algorithmically, the nonlinear case requires infinitesimal nudging, whereas linear systems admit a finite-amplitude experiment. We recover Equilibrium Propagation, Hamiltonian echo backpropagation, fully forward mode training and in situ gradient methods in integrated-photonic and free-space-optical systems. We further show that reciprocity is only the simplest instance of a more general intertwining condition, which extends exact on-device gradient computation to a class of non-Hermitian, non-reciprocal systems. Further generalizations include time-dependent parameters, Onsager-reciprocal dynamics and nonlinear, PT-symmetric Schrödinger equations. Our work provides a unified theoretical basis for formally exact physical learning algorithms and a template for constructing them across a range of physical systems.

</details>


### [43] [Logarithmically Correlated Landscapes and Localization in Non-Hermitian Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11700)
*Xianqi Tong,Qifeng Ding,Xiaosen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究一维Hatano-Nelson环中的非互易跳跃被准周期调制穿越零点时的局域化问题。规范变换将每个本征态映射为单一空间包络，其对数成为确定性的对数相关场；作者推导出精确傅里叶表示，证明景观方差随系统尺寸对数增长，并确定了扩展相在Jensen公式给出的代数边界处终止。奇异区内的局域化需要刚度超过临界阈值，其极值统计呈现异常行为，区别于Anderson、Aubry-André和随机规范普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期调制的非互易跳跃（nonreciprocal hopping）能够同时引入非厄米性与准周期无序，从而创造出既不同于传统Anderson局域化也不同于Aubry-André模型的新的局域化物理，需要系统分析其本征态结构与临界行为。

Method: 利用规范变换（gauge transformation）将每个本征态映射为单一空间包络，当跳跃振幅沿准周期轨道穿越零时，包络对数成为确定性的对数相关场；以此为基础推导精确傅里叶表示，并借助Jensen公式确定扩展相的代数边界；通过极值统计（extreme-value statistics）和刚度（stiffness）阈值分析表征局域化与临界多分形（multifractal）态的相变。

Result: 景观方差随系统尺寸对数增长，刚度由调制功率与不可通约频率的算术性质共同决定；扩展相在Jensen公式给出的代数边界处终止。奇异区内存在临界刚度阈值：高于阈值时波函数权重集中于少数最高景观峰，态为局域化；低于阈值时权重分散于过多竞争峰，态为临界多分形。峰间隙按排名对数的幂次增长，指数编码了频率的连分数类型，区别于已有普适类。刚度在多带晶格中跨通道叠加，且对百分之一量级的组分无序保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该机制建立了非互易跳跃准周期调制下局域化转变的精确描述，其特征普适类独立于已知的Anderson、Aubry-André和随机规范模型；由于所有特征在百分之一量级无机无序下仍然存活，该机制可以通过非互易拓扑电路（topolectrical circuits）在实际实验中实现和验证。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional Hatano-Nelson ring whose nonreciprocal hopping is quasiperiodically modulated through zero. A gauge transformation maps every eigenstate onto a single spatial envelope, whose logarithm becomes a deterministic, logarithmically correlated field once the hopping vanishes along the quasiperiodic orbit. We derive an exact Fourier representation and prove that the landscape variance grows logarithmically with system size, with a stiffness set by the modulation power and the arithmetic of the incommensurate frequency. The extended phase terminates at an algebraic boundary given by Jensen's formula. Inside the singular regime, localization requires the stiffness to exceed a critical threshold: above it, the wavefunction weight concentrates on the few highest landscape peaks and the state is localized; below it, the weight spreads over too many competing peaks and the state is a critical multifractal. The extreme-value statistics are anomalous: peak gaps grow as a power of the logarithm of rank, with an exponent that encodes the continued-fraction type of the frequency, distinct from the Anderson, Aubry-André, and random-gauge universality classes. The stiffness adds across channels in multiband lattices, and all signatures survive percent-level component disorder, placing the mechanism within reach of nonreciprocal topolectrical circuits.

</details>

*Bibo Zhong,Jinying Zhu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过热调制实验测定各向同性材料三阶弹性常数（third-order elastic constants, TOEC）的新方法，推导了均匀温度变化和单轴应力引起的波速变化方程，并通过铝样品的超声实验验证了该方法的可行性和与单轴加载法的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三阶弹性常数在非线性材料表征中具有重要作用，但传统测量方法操作繁琐且误差较大，需要寻求更简便、灵敏的替代测量方案。

Method: 推导了各向同性介质中因均匀温度变化和单轴应力引起的波速变化方程，建立了以热致波速变化和热应变表达TOEC的解析式；通过单轴加载实验和热调制实验测量铝样品中的超声波速变化来确定TOEC，并对两种方法的结果进行对比。

Result: 铝样品的TOEC通过单轴加载测试和热调制测试两种方法实验测定，结果表明两种方法得到了良好一致的实验结果。

Conclusion: 热调制测试因实验装置简单、测量灵敏度高，有望成为测定TOEC和绝对声学非线性参数（absolute acoustic nonlinearity parameters）的潜在实验方法。

Abstract: Third-order elastic constants (TOEC) play an important role in nonlinear material characterization, but measurements of TOEC are laborious with large error margins. This Letter presents the equations of wave velocity changes caused by homogeneous temperature variation and uniaxial stress in isotropic media and the expression of TOEC in terms of thermally induced velocity change and thermal strain. TOEC of an aluminum sample were experimentally determined by measuring ultrasonic wave velocity changes in the uniaxial loading test and the thermal modulation test. Experimental results showed good agreement between the two test methods. Owing to the simple test setup and high measurement sensitivity, the thermal modulation test is a potential experimental method to determine TOEC and absolute acoustic nonlinearity parameters.

</details>

### [46] [The $L^2$ metric for hyperbolic 2-monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11416)
*Guido Franchetti,Derek Harland*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文计算了质量参数为1/2的双磁单极子（2-monopoles）在引入修正规范固定条件后的修正$L^2$度量，得到的度量是一个复非退化双线性形式，可限制为4维反演对称双磁单极子子空间上的黎曼度量，并首次以初等函数和椭圆积分显式给出其表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲磁单极子模空间上的$L^2$度量已知存在发散问题，近期研究表明可通过引入修正的规范固定条件（gauge fixing condition）使其变为有限。本文旨在将这一方法应用于质量参数为1/2的双磁单极子，显式计算修正后的$L^2$度量。

Method: 采用近期提出的修正规范固定条件来消除$L^2$度量的发散性，对质量$\tfrac{1}{2}$的双磁单极子模空间进行度量的显式计算，并将结果以初等函数和椭圆积分的形式表达。

Result: 得到的修正$L^2$度量是一个复非退化双线性形式（complex non-degenerate bilinear form），它限制在4维反演对称双磁单极子（inversion symmetric 2-monopoles）子空间上时成为一个黎曼度量。该度量能够显式地用初等函数和椭圆积分给出，其渐近形式与已有文献结果一致。

Conclusion: 通过修正规范固定条件，成功解决了双曲磁单极子模空间上$L^2$度量的发散问题，并在质量1/2的双磁单极子情形下获得显式的闭合表达式，验证了该方法的有效性与一致性。

Abstract: It has been recently shown that the notoriously divergent $L ^2 $ metric on the moduli space of hyperbolic monopoles can be made finite by the introduction of a modified gauge fixing condition \cite{franchetti:2024}. In this paper we compute this modified $L ^2 $ metric for the mass $\tfrac{1}{2}$ 2-monopoles. The resulting metric is actually a complex non-degenerate bilinear form, which restricts to a Riemannian metric on the 4-dimensional subspace of inversion symmetric 2-monopoles. Remarkably, we have been able to compute the $L ^2 $ form explicitly in terms of elementary functions and elliptic integrals. Its asymptotic form matches what was expected on the basis of previous results in the literature.

</details>

*Jacob Emil Mencke,Thomas Stucker,Paolo Ricci*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导并数值实现了碰撞托克马克预鞘（collisional presheath）入口处、磁场垂直入射壁面情形下的漂移动力学（drift-kinetic）模型边界条件。与先前使用的临时（ad hoc）边界条件相比，新边界条件显著增加了等离子体向壁面的流出，导致装置整体等离子体密度明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移动力学模型在等离子体-壁面相互作用模拟中需要准确的边界条件，而此前使用的临时边界条件可能无法正确刻画碰撞预鞘入口处的物理过程，因此需要推导更为严格的边界条件。

Method: 基于离子分布函数在Hermite-Laguerre基上的展开及相应回转矩（gyromoments）的演化构建漂移动力学模型，在线性等离子体装置几何中推导碰撞预鞘入口处、磁场垂直入射壁面的边界条件，并将其数值实现，同时与采用先前临时边界条件的简化模拟进行比较。

Result: 采用新边界条件后，等离子体向壁面的流出量显著增加，从而使得整个装置体积内的等离子体密度明显减小。

Conclusion: 新推导的边界条件比先前使用的临时边界条件更能准确反映碰撞预鞘入口处的物理行为，对等离子体输运和密度分布具有重要影响。

Abstract: Boundary conditions for a drift-kinetic model at the collisional presheath entrance with perpendicular incidence of the magnetic field to the wall are derived and numerically implemented. The drift-kinetic model for the plasma is based on the expansion of the ion distribution function on a Hermite-Laguerre basis, and the evolution of the resulting gyromoments. A linear-plasma-device geometry is considered. Comparison with simpler simulations with previously used ad hoc boundary conditions is presented. For the new set of boundary conditions, a significant increase of the plasma outflow to the wall is observed, leading to a significantly smaller plasma density in the whole volume of the device.

</details>


### [48] [The Ambipolar electric field in multispecies plasma atmospheres: effects of alpha particles and stochastic heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12014)
*Luca Barbieri,Pascal Démoulin,Daniel Verscharen*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将潘内库克-罗塞兰（Pannekoek–Rosseland）理论推广到多物种、多温度等离子体，研究了由电子、质子和α粒子组成的无碰撞、引力分层等离子体大气中的稳态，导出了自洽的安培电场（ambipolar electric field），分析了引力过滤效应对密度剖面和温度梯度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate stationary states of a collisionless, gravitationally stratified plasma atmosphere composed of electrons, protons, and alpha particles by extending Pannekoek--Rosseland theory to multispecies and multi-temperature plasmas. Starting from Liouville's theorem, we derive the self-consistent ambipolar electric field from kinetic equilibrium and charge neutrality. For a single-temperature atmosphere, we obtain analytical expressions for the ambipolar field, show its dependence on alpha-particle abundance, and determine the relative stratification of the three species. A first-order analytical approximation to the electrostatic potential accurately reproduces the numerical solution. We then generalize the formalism to multi-temperature plasmas generated by stochastic boundary heating, representing the stationary distribution as a superposition of Maxwellian populations. Gravitational filtering produces non-exponential density profiles and increasing temperatures with altitude, while preserving the relative species stratification, with alpha particles most strongly stratified and protons least. The ambipolar electric field contains a dominant gravitational contribution, corresponding to the generalized Pannekoek--Rosseland field, and a thermoelectric contribution arising from species-dependent temperature gradients, which accounts for its non-monotonic structure. These results provide a framework for studying the combined effects of plasma composition and stochastic heating in gravitationally stratified astrophysical plasmas.

Abstract: Gravitational wave observations have found over 300 merging binary black holes, yet their origins remain uncertain. Recent work showed that many may come from isolated stellar binaries whose orbits shrink through stable mass transfer. If true, their spins may help to distinguish this channel from other formation pathways. We investigate the tidal spin up of black hole progenitor stars with detailed modeling of binaries undergoing stable mass transfer. We calculate the tidal torques by solving tidally excited oscillation modes and predict the resulting black hole spins. We find a diverse spin distribution strongly affected by the mass transfer histories of the progenitors. Binaries can form black holes with moderate spins ($0.1\lesssimχ_\mathrm{eff}\lesssim0.3$) if they only go through case A or case B mass transfer. In the former case, they can become super-synchronized upon detachment, while in the latter case, the donor is usually only partially stripped, leaving a puffy envelope where strong tides are excited. If both case A and case AB mass transfer occur, the resulting black hole spins are almost always negligible. As the mass transfer history is jointly determined by mass ratio and initial binary period, our results predict an anti-correlation between black hole spins and mass ratio, consistent with limited evidence from data. Our results can also potentially explain the case of GW190412, a moderately-spinning binary with a high mass ratio. We discuss the limitations of our methods and additional physics (e.g., nonlinear tides, case C, and L2 mass transfer) that need to be incorporated in future work.

</details>

Abstract: On 2025 August 18, the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration reported a sub-threshold gravitational-wave (GW) candidate, S250818k, consistent with a binary neutron star (NS) merger potentially involving a sub-solar-mass compact object. Follow-up electromagnetic (EM) observations identified a Type IIb supernova, SN 2025ulz within the broad localization area of the GW signal. This potential link between a sub-solar GW event candidate and a Type IIb SN, while not confirmed given the low statistical significance of S250818k, has nonetheless sparked renewed interest in the "superkilonova" scenario, where sub-solar-mass NSs form through processes like the fragmentation of an accretion disk or core fission in a collapsing star. In this picture, the in-spiral and merger of a sub-solar NS-NS binary is followed by the merger of the NS-NS remnant with the central black hole (BH), producing chirp-like GW signals. For sub-solar NSs with masses in the (0.1-1)M_sun range and BH masses in the so-called lower mass gap range of ~(3-5) M_sun, these signals can persist in the 20 - 1024 Hz frequency band of ground-based GW detectors for (10^2-10^3)s, potentially offering an opportunity to probe the superkilonova scenario, as well as the lower mass gap between NSs and stellar-mass BHs. However, the complexity of the underlying astrophysics may yield waveforms that deviate from standard templates, limiting the use of matched filtering in real GW searches. We therefore explore the detectability of such signals using the Cross-Correlation Algorithm (CoCoA), a more robust though less sensitive cross-correlation method. We discuss general strategies for implementing CoCoA superkilonova searches via either targeted follow-up of candidate chirps identified in matched-filter searches, or EM-triggered searches of stripped-envelope core-collapse SNe.

Abstract: Physics-based machine learning blends traditional science with modern data-driven techniques. Our method introduces a novel loss function and an output variability-based scaling mechanism, which enhance the model's capability to accurately represent the spatial distribution and morphology of particle showers in detector outputs while mitigating the influence of rare artefacts on the training. Leveraging Normalizing Flows (NFs) in a teacher-student generative framework, we demonstrate that our approach not only outperforms classic data-driven model assimilation but also yields models that are 421 times faster than existing NF implementations in ZDC simulation literature.

Abstract: We construct a novel family of non-supersymmetric, asymptotically locally AdS$_5$ solutions of five-dimensional minimal gauged supergravity. In Lorentzian signature the solutions describe finite temperature rotating electro-magnetically charged black strings with $S^1\times Σ_{\mathfrak{g}}$ horizon topology. In Euclidean signature the solutions are completely smooth and are asymptotic to $T^2\times Σ_{\mathfrak{g}}$. We present a detailed analysis of the thermodynamic properties of these gravitational backgrounds, compute their regularized on-shell action and analyze the extremal and supersymmetric limits. The supersymmetric, but non-extremal, limit of the Euclidean backgrounds is a family of complex black saddles and it provides the holographic dual description of the topologically twisted index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ SCFTs placed on $T^2\times Σ_{\mathfrak{g}}$. We show that the supergravity regularized on-shell action in this limit is in agreement with the large $N$ limit of the topologically twisted index in the dual SCFT.

### [55] [Universal quadratic field equations via homotopy algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11307)
*Christoph Chiaffrino,Raji Ashenafi Mamade,Barton Zwiebach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解释了同伦代数（homotopy algebra）的 bar-cobar 构造如何将任意规范理论（gauge theory）的运动方程重构为扩展场集合上规范协变的二次方程，其中线性项编码原理论相互作用，二次项是普遍的。新方程是一微分分次结合代数或李代数上的 Maurer-Cartan 方程，且所有新方程解均规范等价于仅含 I 型场的解，后者对应原始运动方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意规范理论提供统一的运动方程重构框架，使得相互作用信息与场结构分别编码于线性项与二次项中，从而简化理论研究；同时为弦理论中场论描述提供几何直观。

Method: 利用同伦代数的 bar-cobar 构造，将原理论的运动方程转化为扩展场上的规范协变二次方程。扩展场分为 I 型多局域场（type-I multilocal fields，依赖一组坐标）与 II 型多局域场（type-II multilocal fields，依赖若干组坐标）。新方程被表述为微分分次（differential graded）结合代数或李代数上的 Maurer-Cartan 方程。

Result: 证明了原理论的每个解都可通过仅含 I 型场的解表示，且两者规范等价。在弦理论中，I 型场对应相关共形场论（CFT）的纠缠态，插入黎曼曲面（Riemann surface）的多个穿刺点（punctures）；II 型态可表示不连通的黎曼曲面。

Conclusion: 该构造为任意规范理论提供了一种统一且规范协变的运动方程形式，并在弦理论背景下揭示了多局域场与黎曼曲面几何结构之间的对应关系，为理解纠缠结构与弦相互作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: We explain how the 'bar-cobar' construction for homotopy algebras reformulates the equations of motion of arbitrary gauge theories as gauge-covariant quadratic equations for an extended set of fields. The linear term of the new equations encodes the interactions of the original theory, while the quadratic term is universal. The extended fields include type-I multilocal fields, which depend on a set of coordinates and type-II multilocal fields, which depend on several sets of coordinates.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为Kontsevich模型的超对称扩展建立具体的Ward恒等式（Ward identities）候选系统，从而刻画超对称矩阵模型（supersymmetric matrix model）中测度与费米核所需的代数与分析性质，为潜在的矩阵模型实现奠定理论基础。

Method: 作者采用超流（super-current）公式化方法处理N=1超Virasoro约束。具体步骤包括：独立指派玻色与费米幺正性（monodromies）以统一四个扇区；对每个扇区实施超Miwa变换（super-Miwa transformation），将投影超能动张量转化为谱变量中的微分算子；利用膨胀位移（dilaton shifts）构造超量子Airy结构（Super Quantum Airy Structures）。核心方法是通过建立NS-NS与R-R约束之间的精确相似变换（similarity transformation），其交织前因子组合了玻色行列式、三次Airy权重以及由Neveu-Schwarz与Ramond费米传播子之差定义的Grassmann指数核。

Result: 研究结果表明：在NS-NS与R-NS扇区，膨胀位移可产生超量子Airy结构，而R-R与NS-R扇区则需要额外的奇坐标。主要结果为NS-NS与R-R微分约束之间的精确相似变换，其交织因子由玻色行列式、三次Airy权重与Grassmann指数核共同构成。

Conclusion: 该工作为Kontsevich模型的超对称扩展确定了一个具体的Ward恒等式候选系统，并精确刻画了实现该超对称矩阵模型所需的测度与费米核的代数与分析性质，为后续的矩阵模型构建提供了明确的方向。

Abstract: We develop a super-current formulation of $\mathcal{N}=1$ super-Virasoro constraints for external-source models. By assigning bosonic and fermionic monodromies independently, we unify the NS-NS, NS-R, R-NS, and R-R sectors. For each, a super-Miwa transformation represents the projected super energy-momentum tensor as a differential operator in spectral variables. Dilaton shifts yield Super Quantum Airy Structures in the NS-NS and R-NS sectors, whereas the R-R and NS-R sectors require an additional odd coordinate. Our principal result is an exact similarity transformation relating the NS-NS and R-R differential constraints. The intertwining prefactor combines a bosonic determinant, a cubic Airy weight, and a Grassmann exponential kernel defined by the difference of Neveu--Schwarz and Ramond fermionic propagators. These results establish a concrete candidate system of Ward identities for a supersymmetric extension of the Kontsevich model, delineating the precise algebraic and analytic properties of the measure and fermionic kernel required for its potential matrix-model realization.

</details>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Effects of Tool Wear on the Surface Texture in Turning: A Feature Characterization Approach Based on ISO 21920-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12163)
*Alexander Müller,Maximilian Berndt,Hagen Schmidt,Lars Müller,Matthias Eifler,Eberhard Kerscher,Benjamin Kirsch,Jörg Seewig*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究通过ISO 21920-2标准化的特征参数表征来量化车削刀具磨损。研究发现一个新开发的均值特征方法可将轮廓分解为确定性成分和随机性成分，其中磨损信息几乎全部由确定性成分携带；一个单一参数——槽级平均最大绝对梯度（$\overline{R_\mathrm{dt}}_\mathrm{groove}$）即可解释刀具磨损指标83-90%的方差，为稳健的磨损估计提供了物理动机明确的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表面参数$R_\mathrm{a}$或$R_\mathrm{q}$仅能全局描述形貌，无法对工艺引起的确定性结构进行空间分辨评估。本研究旨在探究ISO 21920-2标准化的特征表征能否将刀具磨损信息变得可获取且具有物理可解释性。

Method: 研究基于12个AlTiN涂层硬质合金刀片（CNMG120408）车削正火AISI 1045钢的粗糙度轮廓数据库，在刀具全寿命周期内测量九个磨损状态、每状态三个重复轮廓。首先检验标准化的场参数和特征参数与月牙洼磨损、后刀面磨损及切削时间的相关性；随后采用分水岭分割提取旋转刀具沟槽并统计其几何特征；最后开发均值特征方法将轮廓分解为确定性成分和随机性成分，并通过共聚焦测量验证对切削刃几何的重构。

Result: 磨损引起的变化几乎完全由确定性成分携带，其中又以切削沟槽的后沿面为主。均值特征方法成功重构了参与切削的刀刃几何，后沿面陡峭化可归因于副切削刃上的缺口磨损。对超过92万种特征参数组合和多变量模型的穷尽评估表明，单一参数$\overline{R_\mathrm{dt}}_\mathrm{groove}$即可解释磨损指标83-90%的方差。

Conclusion: 一个基于物理动机的单一参数即可实现稳健的刀具磨损估计，无需依赖多变量复杂模型。后续研究将探索利用散射光传感器进行在线监测。

Abstract: The surface texture of a turned component acts as a fingerprint of both the process parameters and the tool wear condition, imaging the cutting edge. Classical surface parameters such as $R_\mathrm{a}$ or $R_\mathrm{q}$ describe the topography only globally and allow no spatially resolved evaluation of the process-induced deterministic structures. This work investigates how far the feature characterization standardized in ISO 21920-2 makes this wear information accessible and physically interpretable. The database comprises roughness profiles of twelve AlTiN-coated carbide inserts (CNMG120408) machining normalized AISI 1045 steel, measured at nine wear states over the entire tool life, with three replicate profiles per state. The correlation of standardized field and feature parameters with crater wear, flank wear, and cutting time is first examined. Watershed segmentation is then adapted to extract the rotational tool grooves and evaluate their geometry statistically. A newly developed mean-feature approach decomposes the profile into a deterministic and a stochastic component. Wear-induced changes are almost entirely carried by the deterministic component, and within it by the trailing flank of the cutting groove. A comparison with confocal measurements confirms that the mean feature reconstructs the engaged cutting edge geometry, with the trailing-flank steepening attributable to notch wear on the secondary cutting edge. An exhaustive evaluation of more than 920,000 feature characterization combinations and multivariate models reveals that the groove-level mean maximum absolute gradient $\overline{R_\mathrm{dt}}_\mathrm{groove}$ alone explains 83-90% of the variance of the wear indicators, so that a single, physically motivated parameter suffices for robust wear estimation. A follow-up study will investigate inline monitoring using scattered light sensors.

</details>

### [73] [Full-Covariance Bayesian Inference of Stochastic Gravitational Wave Background with Time-Domain Simulations for Taiji-like Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11276)
*Qingyuan Liang,Ju Chen,Minghui Du,Huai-Ke Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文针对太极（Taiji）类引力波探测任务，实现了一个贝叶斯谱推断框架，该框架将二阶时域（TD）时延干涉（TDI）模拟与频域（FD）谱似然相结合，用于随机引力波背景（SGWB）分析，并通过全XYZ协方差矩阵纳入不等臂长和时变效应。受控模拟验证了在保留频段内计算函数与实现平均谱在几个百分点内一致；静态等臂FD、等臂TD和不等臂TD三种配置在参数估计中给出一致的贝叶斯证据诊断。论文还加入了一级相变声波谱进行扩展分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太极类激光干涉空间任务需要准确处理SGWB分析中由不等臂长和轨道时变效应引起的复杂噪声与信号耦合；传统静态等臂FD近似可能不足以刻画这些效应，因而需要一种在时域模拟与频域谱似然之间自洽衔接的推断方法。

Method: 采用Triangle-Simulator生成X、Y、Z Michelson流，将其分割为有限段并做傅里叶变换，对每段构建依赖于轨道的复数3×3协方差矩阵，同时评估轨道相关响应函数（response function）与噪声传递函数（noise transfer function），使不等臂和时变效应可通过全XYZ协方差进入似然；随后对静态等臂FD、等臂TD和不等臂TD三种配置进行参数估计比较。

Result: 受控模拟显示，在远离TDI零点（TDI nulls）的保留频段内，计算函数能以几个百分点的精度再现实现平均谱。三种配置在边际化仪器噪声和有效银河双白矮星前景后，对天体物理背景恢复给出一致的参数不确定性趋势和贝叶斯证据诊断。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，基于全XYZ协方差的频域似然与时域TDI模拟相结合可在多配置下自洽且稳健地实现SGWB参数推断，并具备扩展至包含一级相变声波谱等额外信号源的能力。

Abstract: For Taiji-like missions, we implement a Bayesian spectral inference framework that combines second-generation time-domain (TD) simulations of time-delay interferometry (TDI) with a frequency-domain (FD) spectral likelihood for stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) analyses. The X, Y, Z Michelson streams generated with Triangle-Simulator are divided into finite segments, Fourier transformed, and modeled with a segment-dependent complex 3 x 3 covariance matrix. For each segment we evaluate the orbit-dependent response functions and noise transfer functions, allowing unequal-arm and time-evolving effects to enter through the full XYZ covariance. Controlled simulations performed with Triangle-Simulator show that the calculated functions reproduce the realization-averaged spectra at the few-percent level over the retained frequency band away from TDI nulls. We then compare parameter-estimation results for static equal-arm FD, equal-arm TD, and unequal-arm TD configurations, using in each case a full XYZ-covariance likelihood matched to the corresponding detector configuration. All three yield consistent uncertainty trends and Bayesian-evidence diagnostics for astrophysical-background recovery after marginalizing over instrumental noise and an effective Galactic double-white-dwarf foreground. Finally, we add a sound-wave spectrum from a first-order phase transition.

</details>

McKenzie,E. R. Rees,B. Shapiro*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出LIGO A♯探测器概念，作为LIGO天文台在第五次观测运行（O5）之后基于室温干涉仪的升级方案，通过更重的测试质量、改进的悬挂与隔振系统、更高的臂腔功率、增强的频率依赖压缩、降低镀膜热噪声等手段实现宽带灵敏度提升，并显著增强天体物理学与基础物理研究的科学产出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在A+灵敏度基础上，现有LIGO探测器在灵敏度与科学产出方面存在提升空间，需要为2030年代规划下一阶段重大科学升级方案，同时为下一代引力波探测器（如Cosmic Explorer）提供技术铺垫。

Method: 通过综合采用多项关键技术方案实现宽带灵敏度改进，包括更重的测试质量（test masses）、改进的悬挂与地震隔离系统、增加臂腔（arm-cavity）功率、增强频率依赖压缩（frequency-dependent squeezing）、在两种场景下降低镀膜热噪声（coating thermal noise），以及改进对机械运动与光学模式的主动控制。

Result: A♯方案大幅提高致密双星合并的探测率，增强天体物理种群推断能力，并改善双中子星并合事件的早期预警时间与定位精度。灵敏度提升还支持对高阶多极（higher-order multipoles）、中等质量黑洞（intermediate-mass black holes）、残余黑洞铃宕（remnant black hole ringdown）及中子星物态方程（equation of state）的精细研究，并扩展连续波（continuous waves）与爆发（bursts）等新引力波源的发现潜力，有望探测O5后仍未发现的致密双星合并随机背景（stochastic background）。

Conclusion: A♯既作为2030年代LIGO的重大科学升级方向，也作为下一代引力波天文台（如Cosmic Explorer）的技术原理验证平台（technology pathfinder），其关键技术挑战与实现路径值得进一步研究与持续推进。

Abstract: We present the LIGO A$^\sharp$ detector concept, an upgrade for the LIGO observatories based on room-temperature interferometers beyond the fifth observing run (O5). Building on the A+ sensitivity, A$^\sharp$ targets broadband sensitivity improvements through heavier test masses, improved suspensions and seismic isolation, increased arm-cavity power, enhanced frequency-dependent squeezing, reduced coating thermal noise considering two scenarios, and improved control of mechanical motion and optical modes. We describe the principal design choices, projected noise performance, and corresponding astrophysical prospects. LIGO A$^\sharp$ substantially increases compact-binary detection rates, strengthens population inference, and improves both early-warning times and localization for binary neutron star mergers. The improved sensitivity enables more detailed studies of compact-binary coalescences, including higher-order multipoles, intermediate-mass black holes, remnant black hole ringdown, and the neutron star equation of state. It also broadens the discovery potential for new gravitational-wave sources such as continuous waves and bursts, should enable detection of the stochastic background from compact binary mergers if it remains undetected after O5, and strengthens the role of gravitational-wave detectors as probes of fundamental physics. We discuss key technical challenges and the role of A$^\sharp$ as both a major scientific upgrade for the 2030s and a technology pathfinder for next-generation gravitational-wave observatories, such as Cosmic Explorer.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文概述LIGO探测器表征（detector characterization）团队在第四次观测运行（O4）第二、第三阶段（O4b与O4c）期间的活动，涵盖探测器配置与性能变化、噪声缓解措施、引力波候选体验证工具及数据质量产品，并对未来观测运行的探测器表征工作进行展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着观测运行时间延长与探测事件数量增多，确保LIGO探测器数据的质量与可靠性对提取引力波源信息至关重要。作者旨在系统总结O4b与O4c阶段探测器表征工作的进展，以持续提升探测器灵敏度并支撑各类引力波搜索。

Method: 本文首先总结LIGO Hanford与LIGO Livingston两个台站在O4a结束至O4c结束期间的探测器配置与性能变化，包括O4b前的停机升级与O4c期间的维修工作。随后描述两个台站开展的仪器调查，用于理解并缓解瞬态毛刺（glitches）、窄带谱线（narrowband spectral lines）及振动引起的噪声等问题，并回顾用于验证引力波候选体的工具、流程及其提供的数据质量产品。

Result: 探测器表征活动使LIGO在O4运行期间能够可靠探测数百次致密双星并合（compact binary coalescences）产生的引力波事件。相关数据质量产品支撑了致密双星并合、未建模瞬态（unmodeled transients）、连续引力波（continuous gravitational waves）以及随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）等多类搜索。

Conclusion: 探测器表征工作对维持和提升LIGO探测器的灵敏度与可靠性必不可少，尤其随着观测运行时间延长和探测事件增多其重要性更加凸显。文章最后对未来观测运行中探测器表征活动的发展方向进行了展望。

Abstract: LIGO detector characterization efforts enabled the confident detection of gravitational waves from hundreds of compact binary coalescences during the fourth observing run. Reliable production of high quality detector data and rapid noise mitigation efforts allow the extraction of the most in-depth knowledge of gravitational wave sources and their progenitors. In this paper we describe LIGO detector characterization activities during the second and third parts of O4-O4b and O4c. We summarize changes in detector configuration and performance at the LIGO Hanford and LIGO Livingston Observatories between the end of the first part of O4a and the end of O4c, including upgrades made during the commissioning break preceding O4b and during repairs performed in O4c. We describe instrumental investigations carried out at both sites designed to understand and subsequently mitigate the effect on detector sensitivity of transient glitches, narrowband spectral lines, and vibration-driven noise, among other data quality concerns. We then review the tools and procedures used to validate gravitational wave candidates and the data quality products thus supplied to searches for gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences and unmodeled transients, continuous gravitational waves, and the stochastic gravitational wave background. The efforts of the detector characterization group are essential for maintaining and improving the sensitivity and reliability of the LIGO detectors especially as observing runs lengthen and more events are detected. We conclude with prospects for LIGO detector characterization activities in future observing runs.

</details>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [On Constrained Riesz Minimum Energy Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11987)
*Dan Coroian,Peter Dragnev,Alan Legg,Ramon Orive*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Riesz框架下的约束平衡问题（constrained equilibrium problems），即在超平面$\mathbb{R}^d$中考虑$s$-Riesz势，给出了若干一般性结果，并详细求解了单位球内的一个约束平衡问题作为示例，最后通过数值实验展示了如何利用约束Leja点（constrained Leja points）对约束平衡测度进行离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rakhmanov（1996）首先引入对数势的约束平衡问题，用于描述离散正交多项式零点的渐近分布，Dragnev、Saff、Kuijlaars和Van Assche等人将此方法推广到更一般的设置。本文旨在将该理论扩展到Riesz势框架下，并探讨其求解与数值离散化方法。

Method: 论文在Riesz框架下定义并研究约束平衡问题，对应的势能为$s$-Riesz势，参数满足$\max(0,d-2)<s<d$，空间维数为$d\geq 1$。作者给出一般性理论结果，并以单位球内的约束平衡问题为具体示例进行详细求解，最后采用Coroian和Dragnev（2001）提出的约束Leja点对约束平衡测度进行数值离散化。

Result: 论文获得了Riesz框架下约束平衡问题的一般性结果，完整求解了单位球内的一个具体约束平衡问题作为示例，并通过数值实验展示了约束Leja点能够有效离散化约束平衡测度。

Conclusion: Riesz设置下的约束平衡问题不仅具有理论意义，还可通过约束Leja点数值求解相应平衡测度，对描述一般势场下的渐近分布问题具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The constrained equilibrium problem for the logarithmic potential was introduced by Rakhmanov (1996) as he realized that the asymptotic distribution of the zeros of discrete orthogonal polynomials in a compact interval could be described in terms of the equilibrium measure of this interval in a class of measures subject to a certain constraint. Other authors such as Dragnev and Saff (1997), and Kuijlaars and Van Assche (1999), extended this approach to more general settings. These problems have proven to be useful for describing asymptotic distributions in different settings.
  In the current paper, we consider constrained equilibrium problems in the Riesz setting, that is, for $s$-Riesz potentials in the hyperplane $\mathbb{R}^d,$ with $d\geq 1$ and $\max (0,d-2) < s < d$. Along with some general results, an illustrative example consisting of the solution of a constrained equilibrium problem in the unit ball is studied in detail. This is the main part of the paper. Finally, a number of numerical experiments show how the constrained equilibrium measure, the solution of the problem, may be discretized using the so-called 'constrained Leja points' introduced by Coroian and Dragnev (2001).

</details>

### [77] [Analytically Consistent Reconstruction of Finite Data Using Padé Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11262)
*Emerson Díaz,Balma Duch,Pere Masjuan*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文重新诠释Padé近似中Froissart双峰（Froissart doublets）的作用，将其视为携带输入数据解析一致性信息的“诊断对象”（diagnostic objects），并据此开发一种基于Padé序列的通用算法，用于从有限数据集（含统计与系统不确定度）中迭代重构最兼容的解析结构（analytic structure）。该方法无需对不一致来源建模，且经Stieltjes函数、拟实验数据和一般全纯函数验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限数据集重建函数的解析结构是理论、数值和实验物理中的基本问题，但有限信息效应以及统计与系统不确定度可能掩盖真实解析结构，降低Padé逼近的可靠性。

Method: 将Froissart双峰的出现重新解释为诊断性对象而非纯数值伪影，开发一种基于Padé序列的通用算法，利用Froissart双峰在Padé序列中的动态行为来识别局部不一致，并迭代重建与数据最兼容的解析结构。方法无需建立不一致来源的模型，可区分真实解析特征与有限信息效应导致的虚假结构。

Result: 算法通过Stieltjes函数、含统计与系统不确定度的拟实验（pseudo-experimental）数据集以及一般全纯函数进行了验证，全部获得成功；完整算法以Mathematica笔记本形式在公开GitLab仓库中提供。

Conclusion: 本文提供的通用Padé基算法能够有效从含噪有限数据中重建解析结构，其关键洞察在于Froissart双峰不仅可作为伪影判别标志，更能充当数据解析一致性的指示器。

Abstract: Reconstructing the analytic structure of a function from finite datasets is a fundamental problem across theoretical, numerical, and experimental physics. While Padé approximants provide a natural framework, finite-information effects, as well as statistical and systematic uncertainties, may obscure the underlying analytic structure and limit reconstruction reliability. In this work, we reinterpret the appearance of Froissart doublets not merely as numerical artifacts but as \textit{diagnostic objects} carrying information about the analytic consistency of the input data. Accordingly, we develop a general Padé-based algorithm that exploits the dynamics of Froissart doublets along Padé sequences to identify localized inconsistencies and iteratively reconstruct the analytic structure most compatible with the data. The method requires no model for the origin of the inconsistencies and distinguishes genuine analytic features from spurious structures induced by finite-information effects. We validate it using Stieltjes functions, realistic pseudo-experimental datasets with statistical and systematic uncertainties, and general holomorphic functions. The complete algorithm is provided as a supplementary Mathematica notebook in an open GitLab repository.

</details>

### [78] [Discriminating superconducting fluctuations from the pseudogap in Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_{n-1}$Cu$_n$O$_{2n+4+δ} (n = 2,3)$: A magnetotransport study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11284)
*Shunpei Yamaguchi,Nae Sasaki,Shintaro Adachi,Keiichi Harada,Yuki Teramoto,Shintaro Matsuda,Tomohiro Usui,Takenori Fujii,Takashi Noji,Itsuhiro Kakeya,Haruka Taniguchi,Michiaki Matsukawa,Atsushi Miyake,Hajime Ishikawa,Koichi Kindo,Takao Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过对Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ和Bi2Sr2CaCu3O10+δ单晶在不同掺杂范围内的磁输运测量，发现虽然赝能隙（pseudogap）引起面内电阻率和霍尔系数的强温度依赖，但T²霍尔角行为和修正Kohler法则在所有掺杂下保持稳健。赝能隙起始温度与超导涨落（superconducting fluctuations）明显不同，阐明了赝能隙并非来源于超导涨落，而可能源于BCS-BEC交叉区域的预成库珀对（preformed Cooper pairing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解正常态性质对于揭示高温超导（high-Tc superconductivity）的微观机制至关重要，而赝能隙是高温超导体正常态中最为神秘的现象之一，其起源尚无定论。

Method: 对Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ（Bi-2212，铜氧双层）和Bi2Sr2CaCu3O10+δ（Bi-2223，铜氧三层）单晶在宽的掺杂范围内进行系统的磁输运（magnetotransport）测量，研究电阻率、霍尔系数、霍尔角及Kohler法则等输运性质。

Result: 面内电阻率和霍尔系数表现出强烈的赝能隙诱导的温度依赖，但T²霍尔角行为与修正Kohler法则在所有掺杂下均保持稳健；赝能隙的起始温度与超导涨落的特征温度明显不同，且与赝能隙幅度之比与d波超导体的预期因子一致。

Conclusion: 赝能隙并非由超导涨落引起，其物理起源可能来自BCS-BEC交叉（BCS-BEC crossover）区域的预成库珀对凝聚，这一观点为理解高温超导机制提供了新的线索。

Abstract: Understanding the normal state is essential for uncovering the mechanism of high-$T_c$ superconductivity. We investigate magnetotransport in Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$ and Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{10+δ}$ single crystals over a wide doping range. While the in-plane resistivity and Hall coefficient show strong pseudogap-induced temperature dependence, the $T^2$ Hall-angle behavior and the modified Kohler's rule remain robust across all dopings. The onset temperatures of the pseudogap are clearly distinct from superconducting fluctuations, although they scale with the pseudogap magnitudes with a factor consistent with a $d$-wave superconductor. These results demonstrate that the pseudogap does not arise from superconducting fluctuations and instead suggest that it may originate from preformed Cooper pairing in the BCS-BEC crossover regime.

</details>


### [79] [Light induced superconducting diode effect in patterned films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11331)
*Evan M. Wilson,Hou-Tong Chen,Alexander V. Balatsky*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过广义含时金兹堡-朗道（generalized time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau）模拟，证明在具有不对称孔洞的图案化超导薄膜中，结构化光驱动能够产生超导二极管（superconducting diode）响应，实现无结几何构型下的整流直流光电压和零偏压方向性超电流不平衡，连续驱动二极管效率约为10^-3，脉冲驱动效率可达10^-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化光为在不永久修改材料或施加静态偏压的情况下控制超导输运提供了新途径，本研究旨在探索无需结几何构型即可实现超导二极管响应的光学驱动方案。

Method: 采用广义含时金兹堡-朗道模拟方法，在具有不对称孔洞的图案化超导薄膜上施加结构化光驱动，系统地分析孔洞阵列构型、光模式、圆偏振手性与偏振状态对超导整流响应的影响，并考察连续驱动与脉冲驱动两种激发方式下的二极管效率。

Result: 研究发现连续驱动二极管效率约为10^-3，脉冲驱动效率可提升至10^-2；增加不对称孔洞数量增强整流效应，反转圆偏振手性可翻转二极管极性，光学空间模式显著改变方向性响应的幅度与极性。在线性偏振照射下，不对称超构晶体将驱动转化为局域手性超电流运动，诱发类似逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）的动力学时间反演对称性破缺机制。

Conclusion: 研究确立了图案化超导薄膜作为光可调谐超导二极管行为可行平台的潜力，为零偏压方向性超电流和光控超导输运提供了一种无需静态偏压或结几何的新型调控方案。

Abstract: Structured light offers a route to control superconducting transport without permanently modifying the material or applying a static bias. Here we show that structured optical driving can generate a superconducting diode response in patterned superconducting films with asymmetric holes. Using generalized time-dependent Ginzburg Landau simulations, we find that optical driving produces rectified dc photovoltages and zero bias directional supercurrent imbalance in a junction free geometry, with continuous-drive diode efficiencies of order $10^{-3}$ and pulsed efficiencies reaching $10^{-2}$. The response is controlled by both the hole array and the optical mode. Increasing the number of asymmetric holes enhances rectification, reversing circular helicity reverses the diode polarity, and the optical spatial mode strongly modifies the magnitude and polarity of the directional response. Pulsed excitation enhances the zero bias line cut current imbalance to the percent level. For linearly polarized illumination, the asymmetric metacrystal converts the drive into local chiral supercurrent motion, inducing an inverse Faraday effect like mechanism for dynamical time reversal symmetry breaking. These results establish patterned superconducting films as a viable platform for light-tunable superconducting diode behavior.

</details>

*Dmitrii V. Semenok,Pedro N. Ferreira,Di Zhou,Fabian Jőbstl,Andrey V. Sadakov,Kirill S. Pervakov,Burkhan I. Massalimov,Toni Helm,Ryosuke Akashi,Vladimir M. Pudalov,Viktor V. Struzhkin,Christoph Heil,Ivan A. Troyan*
