<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钇多氢化物是高压超导领域中的基准材料，但Y-H体系中的若干关键性质尚未得到充分表征，需要对其超导性能进行更系统和精确的重新考察。

Method: 该研究采用多种实验与理论方法：接触输运测量、无接触式的射频（radio-frequency）交流磁化率测量、脉冲场实验（最高至60 T），并辅以第一性原理计算，包括密度泛函理论、随机自洽谐波近似（stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation）、超导密度泛函理论以及全带宽Migdal-Eliashberg计算。

Result: YH₆和YH₉分别表现出218-221 K和235-237 K的超导转变温度，且转变宽度仅2-5 K；YH₆在60 T磁场下展现出线性上临界场斜率d B_c2/dT = -0.52 T/K，并在30 T以上出现显著转变展宽和可忽略的正常态磁阻。射频交流磁化率测量通过无接触磁场屏蔽为YH₆的超导性提供了证据。钯掺入、钯薄膜溅射及铝合金化实验均强烈抑制高温超导性。非谐效应将立方YH₁₀的预测T_c显著降低至约260-270 K。

Conclusion: 综合实验结果与理论计算，二元钇超氢化物中不存在室温超导性，相关高温超导表现主要集中在亚室温区间。

Abstract: Yttrium polyhydrides are benchmark materials in high-pressure superconductivity, yet several key properties of the Y-H system remain insufficiently characterized. Here we combine contact transport, contactless radio-frequency measurements, pulsed-field experiments, and first-principles calculations to reinvestigate YH$_6$, YH$_9$, and YH$_{10}$ in the pressure range 140-213 GPa. Yttrium hydrides YH$_6$ ($\textit{$T_c$}$ = 218-221 K) and YH$_9$ ($\textit{$T_c$}$ = 235-237 K) demonstrate narrow superconducting transitions ($\textit{$Δ$T$_c$}$ = 2-5 K), approaching the limit imposed by thermal fluctuations. Pulsed-field measurements on YH$_6$ up to 60 T establish an extended superconducting phase diagram with a linear slope $\textit{dB$_{c2}$/dT}$ = -0.52 T/K, pronounced transition broadening above 30 T, and negligible normal-state magnetoresistance. We report the radio-frequency AC susceptibility study of YH$_6$, providing evidence for superconductivity via high-frequency field screening in a contactless geometry. Experiments involving Pd incorporation, Pd thin-film sputtering, and Al alloying show strong suppression of high-temperature superconductivity, with no transitions detected above 78-120 K. Finally, using density-functional theory with the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation, superconducting density-functional theory, and full-bandwidth Migdal-Eliashberg calculations, we show that anharmonic effects substantially reduce the predicted $\textit{$T_c$}$ of cubic YH$_{10}$ to approximately 260-270 K. These results strongly disfavor room-temperature superconductivity in binary yttrium superhydrides.

</details>

*V. M. Kovalev,A. V. Chaplik*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微观理论框架，解释超导薄膜中声学等离子体激元（acoustic plasmon）模式的起源，阐明有限厚度薄膜中安德森-希格斯约束（Anderson-Higgs constraints）被绕过所需的物理条件，其理论预测与近期实验观测高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期超导薄膜实验揭示了与超导转变密切相关的声学等离子体模式，这直接挑战了长期以来关于超导体中等离子体谱刚性的安德森-希格斯范式（Anderson-Higgs paradigm）。作者旨在建立微观理论框架，解释这一行为并给出经典约束被绕过的物理条件。

Method: 作者构建了适用于有限厚度超导薄膜的微观理论框架，考察法向（normal）与超流体（superfluid）电荷密度的横向重新分布，并分析其与电磁辐射之间独特的耦合机制——该机制在常规卡尔森-戈德曼（Carlson-Goldman）情景中根本不存在。

Result: 该理论预测了声学模式，其色散关系（dispersion）、温度标度（temperature scaling）以及对薄膜厚度的依赖性与近期实验观测结果显著吻合。

Conclusion: 通过界定这一声学响应的参数区间，作者将集体激发的电磁活性观测与超导基本原理相协调，揭示了有限厚度下安德森-希格斯约束被绕过的可行途径。

Abstract: Recent experiments on superconducting films have revealed an acoustic plasmon mode that depends critically on the superconducting transition, directly challenging the long-standing Anderson-Higgs paradigm regarding the stiffness of the plasma spectrum in superconductors. In this Letter, we provide a microscopic theoretical framework that explains this behavior and establishes the physical conditions under which classical Anderson-Higgs constraints are bypassed. We demonstrate that in films of finite thickness, the transverse redistribution of normal and superfluid charge densities enables a unique coupling mechanism to electromagnetic radiation - a feature fundamentally absent in the conventional Carlson-Goldman scenario. Our theory predicts an acoustic mode whose dispersion, temperature scaling, and dependence on film thickness are in remarkable agreement with recent experimental observations. By delineating the regime of this acoustic response, we reconcile the observed electromagnetic activity of collective excitations with the fundamental principles of superconductivity.

</details>


### [82] [Thickness-Driven Superconductor-Insulator Transition in (Cu,C)-1234 and Proximity-Induced Superconductivity Recovery in (Cu,C)-1234/YBCO Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11707)
*Zhihang Xu,Detian Yang,Ping Zhu,Ruoxian Sun,Xiaoyang Cai,Yanqun Guo,Chuanbing Cai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过在LAO(001)衬底上制备(Cu,C)-1234/YBCO异质结构，系统研究了不同厚度(Cu,C)-1234层（188nm、87nm、18nm及约1.2nm）与固定厚度YBCO之间的超导邻近效应（superconducting proximity effect）。研究发现(Cu,C)-1234层在厚度减小时发生超导体-绝缘体转变（SIT），但与其上层的超导YBCO耦合后超导性得以恢复，直至厚度降至约1.2nm时恢复的超导被强烈抑制，揭示了界面耦合与维度限制之间的竞争关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导邻近效应及相关的厚度驱动性质演化对于理解高温超导体具有重要意义，而超导体-超导体（S-S'）体系因两侧均具有内禀超导性，为研究界面处不同超导序的竞争、耦合与重构提供了独特平台。

Method: 在LAO(001)衬底上制备(Cu,C)-1234/YBCO异质结构，其中底部YBCO层厚度固定为150nm，顶部(Cu,C)-1234层厚度分别设为188nm、87nm、18nm及约1.2nm，并保留各组分薄膜作为对照，通过电输运测量表征其超导性质。

Result: 电输运测量表明，随着厚度减小，(Cu,C)-1234薄膜劣化并在厚度小于18nm时经历超导体-绝缘体转变（SIT）。然而，当绝缘的(Cu,C)-1234层与超导YBCO耦合后，输运测量中重新观察到超导性；但当(Cu,C)-1234厚度进一步降至约1.2nm时，恢复的超导性被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 观察到的厚度依赖性与如下场景一致：在有限的厚度范围内，界面耦合恢复了超导性，随后在二维极限下不断增强的无序效应和维度限制占据主导。该工作为研究铜氧化物超导体之间的界面耦合提供了有前景的平台，并为高温超导异质结构中的超导邻近效应提供了新见解。

Abstract: Superconducting proximity effect and related thickness-driven property evolution remain an important issue in understanding high temperature superconductors. Among proximity systems, superconductor-superconductor (S-S') is special for the existence of intrinsic superconductivity in both materials. Such platform allows the different superconducting orders to compete, couple and reconstruct at the interface. In this paper, (Cu,C)-1234/YBCO heterostructure grown on LAO (001) with fixed thickness of bottom YBCO layer as 150 nm and varied thickness of top (Cu,C)-1234 layer as 188nm, 87 nm, 18nm and estimated 1.2 nm were fabricated and component films were preserved. Electrical transport characterization indicated that as the thickness decrease the (Cu,C)-1234 film degrades and underwent the superconductor-insulator transition (SIT) from thicker to less than 18 nm. In contrast, superconductivity is re-established in transport measurements when the insulating (Cu,C)-1234 layer is coupled to superconducting YBCO As the (Cu,C)-1234 thickness is further reduced to approximately 1.2 nm, the recovered superconductivity is strongly suppressed. The observed thickness dependence is consistent with a scenario in which interfacial coupling restores superconductivity over a finite thickness range before increasing disorder and dimensional confinement dominate in the two-dimensional limit. This work establishes a promising platform for investigating interfacial coupling between cuprate superconductors and provides new insight into the superconducting proximity effect in high-temperature superconducting heterostructures.

</details>


### [83] [Spin-polarized supercurrents and Josephson diode effect in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11906)
*Janus F. Niebuhr,Matthias Eschrig,Danilo Nikolić*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文针对d波交变磁体（altermagnet, AM）置于两个BCS超导体之间的约瑟夫森结进行了系统性理论研究，基于全量子（Gor'kov）与准经典（Eilenberger）格林函数技术，揭示了强自旋极化体系中因量子几何相位导致的约瑟夫森二极管效应，电荷二极管效率超过30%，自旋二极管效率达到100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体作为兼具类铁磁与类反铁磁特性的新型磁性材料，其在超导结中的约瑟夫森效应此前缺乏系统性理论理解，特别是自旋极化电流与非互易输运行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 模型采用自旋依赖的δ势阱描述自旋活性SC/AM界面，通过全量子（Gor'kov）和准经典（Eilenberger）格林函数技术分别处理弱自旋极化和强自旋极化两种AM情形，并区分共面与非共面交换场分布以分析约瑟夫森电流-相位关系（current-phase relation, CPR）。

Result: 在弱自旋极化区，CPR呈现正常约瑟夫森效应并发生0-π跃迁，取向不同时分别表现出类铁磁或类反铁磁结的特征；在强自旋极化区，共面交换场产生含稳定长程二次谐波的正常约瑟夫森效应，而非共面交换场引发量子几何相位，导致CPR失去相位反转中心，进而产生约瑟夫森二极管效应，电荷二极管效率超过30%，自旋二极管效率达100%。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了d波交变磁体/超导结的完整理论框架，并首次预测了基于非共面交换场与量子几何相位的约瑟夫森二极管效应，为自旋电子学与超导电子学交叉领域的器件设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a systematic theoretical study of the Josephson effect in junctions consisting of a d-wave altermagnet (AM) placed between two BCS superconductors (SC). In general, the SC/AM interfaces are spin-active and modeled by spin-dependent $δ$ potentials, allowing for an arbitrary direction of the local exchange field vector. The model is formulated within the fully quantum (Gor'kov) and quasiclassical (Eilenberger) Green's function technique, applied to two distinct cases of (i) a weakly spin-polarized AM (exchange field much smaller compared to the Fermi energy) and (ii) a strongly spin-polarized AM (exchange field comparable to the Fermi energy). We apply our model to the SC/AM/SC geometry, accounting for the Josephson current-phase relation (CPR). In the weakly spin-polarized regime, the CPR displays the normal Josephson effect. Irrespective of the orientation of the altermagnet, the junction undergoes the $0-π$ transition. Depending on the orientation, the system displays the features similar to those of a ferromagnetic or an antiferromagnetic junction. To investigate the spin-polarized currents and nonreciprocal transport as the central results of the present work, we put the main focus on the strongly spin-polarized regime. Within this regime, we distinguish two cases. A coplanar exchange field profile across the junctions displays the normal Josephson effect; however, with a pure and stable long-range second harmonic in the CPR. In contrast, a noncoplanar exchange field profile gives rise to the so-called quantum geometric phases across the junction, leading to the absence of the phase-inversion center in the Josephson CPR. As a result, a Josephson diode effect emerges with a significant charge diode efficiency larger than 30% and a perfect spin diode efficiency of 100%.

</details>

### [84] [Giant Effective Permeability in Drude Thin Films Probed by THz Time-Domain Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11432)
*Gian Paolo Papari,Zahra Mazaheri,Antonio Vettoliere,Carmine Granata,Roberto Russo,Giovanni Ausanio,Umar Farooq,Junaid Yaseen,Can Koral,Antonello Andreone*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过太赫兹时域光谱（terahertz time-domain spectroscopy）对10 nm厚的铝膜和铜膜进行透射与反射测量，结合自洽检索方法独立提取复介电常数和复磁导率，发现高导电薄膜的磁导率显著偏离1，并基于法拉第-诺依曼-楞次机制和集总元件模型（lumped-element model）对这一现象给出了物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹光谱分析金属薄膜电磁响应时通常假设相对磁导率为1，但这一假设在高导电薄膜的电动力反演中可能引入显著误差，因此需要检验该假设的适用性并建立更精确的检索方法。

Method: 对10 nm厚的铝膜和铜膜，在透射和反射两种配置下进行太赫兹时域光谱测量；采用自洽检索方法（self-consistent retrieval method）独立确定复介电常数（complex permittivity）和复磁导率（complex permeability），并基于法拉第-诺依曼-楞次机制与集总元件模型进行唯象解释。

Result: 研究发现，德鲁德型（Drude-type）介电响应当中始终伴随显著偏离1的磁导率；该偏离与薄膜的反应阻抗（reactive impedance）内在相关，揭示了光诱导下横向受限几何结构中大尺度屏蔽电流的产生。法拉第-诺依曼-楞次机制和集总元件阻抗模型能够解释观测趋势。

Conclusion: 在非磁性德鲁德薄膜中假设磁导率μ̃=1可能导致太赫兹波段电动力学表征不完整或产生偏差，表明该常用假设在高导电薄膜分析中需要修正。

Abstract: The electromagnetic response of metallic films is commonly analyzed in terahertz spectroscopy by assuming unit relative magnetic permeability. In this work we show that this assumption introduces significant distortions in the electrodynamic retrieval of highly conductive films. Aluminum and copper films, 10 nm thick, were investigated by terahertz time-domain spectroscopy in both transmission and reflection configurations. By applying a self consistent retrieval method that independently determines the complex permittivity and permeability, we show that the Drude-type dielectric response is systematically accompanied by a permeability that strongly departs from unity. This deviation is intrinsically linked to the reactive impedance of the films, which clarifies the light induced onset of large screening currents within a transversally confined geometry. A phenomenological interpretation based on the Faraday Neumann Lenz mechanism and a lumped-element model of the film impedance accounts for the observed trends. These results indicate that the common assumption $\tildeμ=1$ in non-magnetic Drude films can lead to an incomplete or biased electrodynamic characterization in the terahertz regime.

</details>


### [85] [Formally Verified Lock-Free Software Transactional Memory for Scientific Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12024)
*Kentaro Kitagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个无锁软件事务内存（lock-free software transactional memory, STM）系统，它是开源测量平台的核心组件，已用于核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance, NMR）和光探测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR）实验16年。该系统将共享状态组织为树结构，提供原子子树更新和一致子树快照，并通过自定义无锁原子共享指针实现O(1)时间复杂度的快照获取，同时通过TLA+模型检查和C11弱内存模型下的有界执行验证保证安全性和无活锁特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态测量实验的自动化需要仪器控制、数据采集和用户界面线程并发访问共享的天然层级结构状态。粗粒度加锁会延迟采集并导致样品损失，而细粒度加锁则需要跨仪器的易死锁锁顺序，因此需要一种无需复杂锁机制就能保证并发安全的方案。

Method: 采用无锁软件事务内存（STM）将共享状态组织为树结构，提供原子子树更新和一致子树快照操作。通过自定义无锁原子共享指针实现初始打包后O(1)时间的子树快照获取；使用TLA+规范在每个有界配置下通过TLC穷举检查状态空间，验证安全性和无活锁性质；原子共享指针实现的有界执行在C11弱内存模型下单独验证。

Result: 该系统已在实际测量平台中稳定运行16年，支撑NMR和ODMR等实验。TLC在有界TLA+配置下穷举检查了全部状态空间，确认了安全性和无活锁性质；C11弱内存模型下的有界执行检查验证了原子共享指针实现的正确性。相同的Snapshot和Transaction接口同时提供给Python脚本和AI辅助自动化使用。

Conclusion: 无锁STM方案成功替代了粗粒度与细粒度加锁策略，在保证并发安全的同时避免了锁顺序导致的死锁和加锁延迟导致的样品损失问题，并为Python脚本与AI辅助自动化提供了统一的事务接口。

Abstract: Automated measurement of condensed-matter experiments requires instrument-control, data-acquisition, and user-interface threads to access shared, naturally hierarchical state concurrently. Coarse-grained locking can delay acquisition and cause sample loss, whereas fine-grained locking requires deadlock-prone lock ordering across instruments. Instead, we describe the lock-free software transactional memory (STM) that has been at the core of an open-source measurement platform for 16 years, in nuclear magnetic resonance experiments and, more recently, in optically detected magnetic resonance experiments. The STM organizes this state as a tree and provides atomic subtree updates and consistent subtree snapshots. After initial bundling, an unchanged subtree snapshot is acquired in $O(1)$ time through a custom lock-free atomic shared pointer. Within each bounded TLA+ configuration, TLC exhaustively checks the state space, establishing the safety and livelock-freedom properties specified for that configuration. Bounded executions of the atomic shared-pointer implementation are separately checked under the C11 weak-memory model. The same Snapshot and Transaction interfaces are exposed to Python scripting and AI-assisted automation.

</details>

### [86] [Effective Dynamics of Disclination Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12233)
*Nicolò Briatico,Pierluigi Cesana,Marco Morandotti*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了单圆域内一对Frank角相反、受限于圆形域内的楔形向错（wedge disclination）在径向对称运动假设下的耗散动力学，识别出两种动力学区域（发散的向错区域与湮灭的偶极子区域），确定了它们各自的驻点及其稳定性，并给出了特征演化时间的定量估计。在湮灭偶极子区域，通过合适的时间标度变换，偶极子的运动定律与刃型位错（edge dislocation）的运动定律一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受限几何（圆形域）中成对楔形向错的耗散动力学，旨在理解两种不同运动模式（发散与湮灭）的状态特征，并建立向错动力学与位错动力学之间的联系。

Method: 在径向对称运动（radially symmetric motion）的假设下，对一对具有相反Frank角的楔形向错在圆形域内的耗散动力学进行分析。通过求解运动方程，识别驻点并对其稳定性进行全面表征，同时推导驻点附近特征演化时间的定量表达式；在湮灭偶极子区域，采用时间标度（time rescaling）变换比较所得运动定律与刃型位错的运动定律。

Result: 识别出两种截然不同的动力学区域：向错间距发散的向错区域和向错对湮灭的偶极子区域。两种区域的驻点均被确定并依据稳定性进行了完整分类，给出了驻点附近特征演化时间的定量估计。在湮灭偶极子区域中，经过适当的时间标度后，偶极子的运动定律与刃型位错完全一致。

Conclusion: 受限圆形域中成对楔形向错的耗散动力学存在两种互异的演化模式，且湮灭偶极子的运动可通过时间标度映射为刃型位错的运动，揭示了向错与位错之间在动力学层面上的深层对应关系。

Abstract: The dissipative dynamics of a pair of wedge disclinations with opposite Frank angles confined to a circular domain is investigated under the assumption of radially symmetric motion. Two distinct dynamical regimes are identified: a diverging-disclination regime and an annihilating-dipole regime. The stationary points associated with both regimes are determined and fully characterized in terms of their stability, and quantitative estimates for the characteristic evolution times in their vicinity are derived. In the annihilating-dipole regime, it is shown that, after a suitable time rescaling, the resulting law of motion for the dipole coincides with that of an edge dislocation.

</details>

### [87] [Dynamics of fluctuating populations in multi-state switching environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12208)
*Mauro Mobilia*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建包含有限个中间状态的多状态随机切换模型（multi-state stochastic switching model），对粗粒化的"盛宴-饥荒"周期（feast-famine cycle）中两株生长速率不同的菌株的资源竞争动态进行了计算与解析研究，考察了切换速率和承载能力分布对群体大小统计量、固定概率及平均固定时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物群体通常在随时间波动的环境中进化，传统研究多采用二元切换模型描述资源丰缺的突变转换。然而实验研究表明，盛宴-饥荒环境的时序动力学实际更为复杂，需要更细致的多状态模型来刻画营养资源逐渐恢复与耗竭的过程。

Method: 提出了多状态随机切换模型，其中环境在有限数量的中间状态间切换，每个状态拥有各自的承载能力，营养可利用性的渐变由状态间切换及其承载能力表征。通过计算与解析方法结合的方式，刻画了多状态波动环境中的群体动态。

Result: 研究了切换速率与承载能力分布对群体大小统计量（population-size statistics）、固定概率（fixation probability）和平均固定时间（mean fixation time）的影响，并将这些结果与二元环境下的对应结果进行了比较，厘清了环境波动的频率与振幅对粗粒化盛宴-饥荒周期中群体动态的影响。

Conclusion: 多状态波动环境相比二元环境能更真实地反映盛宴-饥荒周期中的菌株竞争动态，环境波动的频率与幅度的具体作用可通过与二元模型对比得到系统理解。

Abstract: Microbial populations generally evolve in fluctuating environments under time-varying conditions. These are often described by binary switching models, sometimes seen as coarse-grained feast-famine cycles, in which resource availability switches abruptly between abundant and scarce conditions. However, experimental studies suggest that feast-famine environments actually exhibit more complex temporal dynamics. Here, we study how two strains, one growing slightly slower than the other, compete for the same resources in fluctuating environments comprising a finite number of intermediate states, each having its own carrying capacity. Environmental switching between these states and their carrying capacities represents gradual changes in nutrient availability. This class of multi-state stochastic switching models can be interpreted as a coarse-grained description of feast--famine cycles and allows us to investigate strain competition under the gradual recovery and depletion of resources. By computational and analytical means, we characterise the population dynamics in these multi-state fluctuating environments. In particular, we study how the switching rates and distribution of carrying capacities affect the population-size statistics, fixation probability, and mean fixation time. By comparing these results with their counterparts in binary environments, we clarify how the frequency and amplitude of environmental fluctuations influence population dynamics in coarse-grained feast-famine cycles.

</details>

### [88] [Temperature-Driven Sequential Modeling for the Prediction of Annual Power Conversion Efficiency Profiles of Organic Photovoltaic Materials: Douala Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11261)
*Steve Cabrel Teguia Kouam,Rockefeller Rockefeller,Raoult Dabou Teukam,Jean-Pierre Tchapet Njafa,Patrick Sorrel Mvoto Kongo,Jean-Pierre Nguenang,Serge Guy Nana Engo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个气候原生（Climate-Native）计算框架，结合GFN2-xTB分子动力学、等变图神经网络代理模型和基于NASA POWER气候数据的序列深度学习模型，预测有机光伏（OPV）给体分子在热带地区实际运行条件下的全年功率转换效率（PCE）分布，并在喀麦隆三个城市进行了跨城市零样本迁移验证，显著优于静态基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟筛选工具仅在标准测试条件（STC）下报告静态功率转换效率，无法反映实际部署中温度驱动的性能退化，尤其影响分布式太阳能应用前景广阔的热带地区。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：利用GFN2-xTB进行分子动力学模拟，以等变图神经网络（equivariant graph neural network）作为代理模型（基于268个Neyman分层CEP分子、120,600个训练几何构型，较显式量子化学方法提速约1050倍），并在以喀麦隆杜阿拉（Douala）NASA POWER气候数据锚定的年时间序列上训练序列深度学习模型，实现跨城市零样本迁移验证。

Result: 对约30,000个哈佛清洁能源项目（CEP）分子进行筛选，并用350个HOPV15实验器件测量验证，序列模型较时间平均基线实现35%-48%的相对平均绝对误差（MAE）改进，验证了热构象动力学包含超出平均几何结构的信息；引入的季节稳定性评分可识别出部署适用性与静态PCE排名显著不同的分子。

Conclusion: 序列深度学习方法比静态基准和基于时间平均几何的模型更能捕捉温度驱动的OPV性能退化，所引入的季节稳定性评分可作为静态PCE排名的有力补充，提升热带地区OPV材料筛选的可靠性。

Abstract: Organic photovoltaic (OPV) materials are promising candidates for distributed solar energy in tropical regions, yet existing virtual screening tools report static power conversion efficiency (PCE) values at standard testing conditions (STC) that fail to capture the temperature-driven performance degradation experienced under real deployment conditions. Here we introduce a Climate-Native computational framework that forecasts the annual PCE profile of OPV donor molecules under geographically realistic operating conditions. The framework combines GFN2-xTB molecular dynamics with an equivariant graph neural network surrogate ($268$ Neyman-stratified CEP molecules; $120,600$ training geometries; $\sim 1050\times$ speedup over explicit quantum chemistry) and sequential deep learning models trained on annual time series anchored in NASA POWER climate data for Douala, Cameroon, and validated by zero-shot transfer to Yaoundé and Maroua. Applied to $\sim 30,000$ molecules from the Harvard Clean Energy Project (CEP) and validated against $350$ HOPV15 experimental device measurements, the framework demonstrates that sequential models trained on full molecular dynamics trajectories outperform time-averaged baselines ($35\%$-$48\%$ relative MAE improvement over static baselines), confirming that thermal conformational dynamics carry information beyond mean geometry. We further introduce a seasonal stability score that reranks OPV candidates by performance consistency under tropical conditions, identifying molecules whose deployment suitability differs substantially from their static PCE ranking.

</details>


### [89] [Chemically Meaningful Textualization Enables Explainable Validation of Metal-Organic Frameworks by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11283)
*Guobin Zhao,Xiao-Yan Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）作为金属有机框架（MOF）结构的可解释验证工具。通过将晶体学信息转化为化学文本描述，研究发现验证效果取决于结构信息表征的语言可学习性而非信息量本身。基于mof2text专用描述符微调的LLM在图谱级模型中达到相当性能，并能为错误结构生成诊断性解释，从而将LLM从通用文本模型转变为实用的数据库筛选工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向高通量筛选的MOF数据库需要计算就绪（computation-ready）的晶体结构，但许多已报道结构存在化学不合理或无序问题。现有验证方法依赖启发式规则、受许可证限制或缺乏可解释性，亟需一种既能准确识别又能给出诊断解释的验证方案。

Method: 将MOF的晶体学信息转化为化学语义文本描述，基准测试九种描述符，评估LLM在不同表示下识别不合理结构的性能。采用LLM微调（fine-tuning）配合专用描述符mof2text，并与基于图的模型进行对比，同时训练模型生成错误根源的诊断性理由和错误类别预测。

Result: 基于LLM的验证成效不取决于结构信息量的多少，而取决于局域配位（local coordination）、框架连接性（framework connectivity）和化学上下文是否被组织成语义上可学习的表示。使用mof2text微调的LLM在识别不合理MOF方面达到与图基模型相当的性能，并能输出异常键合、连接性和电荷状态等错误来源的诊断性解释。

Conclusion: 化学信息文本化（chemically informed textualization）是使LLM从通用文本模型转变为实用且可解释的MOF数据库筛选工具的关键步骤，为MOF结构验证提供了兼具准确性和可解释性的新范式。

Abstract: Computation-ready metal-organic framework (MOF) databases are essential for high-throughput screening, yet many reported crystal structures remain chemically unreasonable or disordered, compromising simulation fidelity. Existing validation approaches can identify non-computation-ready structures, but they often rely on heuristic rules, license requirement, or offer limited interpretability. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can serve as interpretable validators of MOF structures when crystallographic information is transformed into chemically meaningful text. By benchmarking nine descriptors, we find that successful LLM-based validation depends not on the amount of structural information alone, but on whether local coordination, framework connectivity, and chemical context are organized into a linguistically learnable representation. Fine-tuned LLMs using specialized descriptors (mof2text) achieve performance comparable to graph-based models in identifying unreasonable MOFs. Importantly, these models extend beyond black-box classification by generating diagnostic rationales for likely error sources, including abnormal bonding, connectivity, and charge states, as well as error-category predictions for annotated datasets. This work establishes chemically informed textualization as the key step that transforms LLMs from generic text models into practical and explainable tools for curating MOF databases.

</details>


### [90] [Strain-controlled magnetism and magnetoelasticity in monolayer NiPS$_3$ and CrPS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11356)
*Balázs Nagyfalusi,Alvaro Bermejillo-Seco,Linde de Jong,Ritesh Das,Yaroslav M. Blanter,Herre S. J. van der Zant,Amador Garcia-Fuente,and Jaime Ferrer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于应变相关的海森堡模型（strain-dependent Heisenberg model）提出了二维磁体中磁弹耦合（magnetoelastic coupling）的第一性原理研究框架，并将其应用于单层NiPS₃和CrPS₄。结果表明NiPS₃具有弱且近乎各向同性的自旋-晶格耦合，而CrPS₄展现出强各向异性耦合，可通过应变驱动自旋螺旋与铁磁相之间的转变，突出其为应变工程磁性有序的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁体的磁弹耦合对其磁性基态的稳定性和可调控性至关重要，但缺乏从微观自旋相互作用出发系统量化磁弹效应的第一性原理框架，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建基于应变依赖的海森堡模型的磁弹耦合第一性原理计算框架，通过交换相互作用（exchange interactions）的应变导数直接获取磁致伸缩（magnetostriction）以及磁性重整化（magnetic renormalization）后的弹性张量，从而在自旋相互作用与弹性响应之间建立微观联系。该方法被应用于单层NiPS₃和CrPS₄两种材料。

Result: NiPS₃表现出弱且近乎各向同性的自旋-晶格耦合，与其稳健的之字形反铁磁（zigzag antiferromagnetic）基态一致；CrPS₄则表现出强各向异性耦合，可导致应变驱动的自旋螺旋（spin-spiral）与铁磁（ferromagnetic）相之间的转变，并显著改变临界温度（critical temperature）和弹性响应。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条定量研究低维磁体中磁弹效应的通用途径，并表明CrPS₄有望成为通过应变工程调控磁性有序性的理想平台。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for magnetoelastic coupling in two-dimensional magnets based on a strain-dependent Heisenberg model. In this approach, strain derivatives of the exchange interactions provide direct access to magnetostriction and to the magnetic renormalization of the elastic tensor, establishing a microscopic link between spin interactions and elastic response. We apply the method to monolayer NiPS$_3$ and CrPS$_4$, which exhibit contrasting magnetoelastic behavior. NiPS$_3$ shows weak and nearly isotropic spin-lattice coupling, consistent with a robust zigzag antiferromagnetic ground state. In contrast, CrPS$_4$ displays strong anisotropic coupling, leading to strain-driven transitions between spin-spiral and ferromagnetic phases and significant changes in the critical temperature and elastic response. Result: 对称性降低的影响强烈依赖卤素组成：对FAPbCl3和FAPbBr3，立方相向伪立方相转变会降低晶格刚度、声学声子速度和德拜温度；而FAPbI3表现出相反趋势。应力-应变分析进一步揭示出显著的非线性、各向异性和非对称力学行为，对称性降低可依据卤素种类激活或抑制应变容纳机制。

Abstract: Formamidinium-based hybrid halide perovskites (FAPbX3, X = Cl, Br, and I) have attracted considerable attention for optoelectronic applications owing to their outstanding optical and electronic properties. However, the influence of crystal symmetry reduction on their mechanical behavior and stability has not yet been comprehensively understood. In this work, density functional theory (DFT) calculations were performed to investigate the structural, elastic, dynamical, and nonlinear mechanical properties of the cubic and ps-cubic phases of FAPbX3. The elastic constants, bulk, shear, and Young's moduli, Poisson's ratio, sound velocities, and Debye temperature were evaluated and correlated with the second Piola-Kirchhoff stress-strain response under tensile and compressive loading. The results reveal that the effect of symmetry reduction is strongly dependent on the halide composition. For FAPbCl3 and FAPbBr3, the transition from the cubic to the ps-cubic phase reduces the lattice stiffness, decreases the acoustic phonon velocities, and lowers the Debye temperature, whereas the opposite trend is observed for FAPbI3. The stress-strain analysis further reveals pronounced nonlinear, anisotropic, and asymmetric mechanical behavior, demonstrating that symmetry reduction can either activate or suppress strain-accommodation mechanisms depending on the halide species, thereby governing the mechanical stability and the onset of structural softening. These findings provide microscopic insight into the relationship between crystal symmetry, lattice dynamics, and nonlinear mechanical response in formamidinium-based halide perovskites, offering useful guidance for the design of mechanically robust optoelectronic materials.

### [93] [Accurate Evaluation of Nanoscale Spatiotemporal Dynamics with Electron Correlation Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11466)
*Po-Cheng Kung,Ajay Annamareddy,Mark D. Ediger,Dane Morgan,Paul M. Voyles*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出面向电子关联显微术（electron correlation microscopy, ECM）的物理启发的强度归一化框架，用于消除纳米束电子衍射中时间平均和散射矢量平均带来的系统性伪影（如人为反关联和基线抬升），并通过分子动力学模拟和实验数据验证，正确识别了样品中稳定的纳米晶相，为评估局域时空弛豫行为提供了无伪影的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECM虽能以纳米级空间分辨率测量材料动力学，但直接沿用X射线光子相关谱学（XPCS）的归一化框架计算强度关联会引入误差。由于纳米束电子衍射中受限的采样体积和较大的散斑（speckle）尺寸，XPCS式的时间平均和散射矢量平均会产生系统性伪影，导致结构弛豫时间和拉伸指数（stretching exponent）的系统性误差。

Method: 本研究提出了ECM特有的、基于物理启发的强度归一化方法，对第一衍射环的时间平均和方位角平均强度进行归一化以限制人为误差。框架通过CuZr过冷液体的分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟进行验证，以自中间散射函数（self intermediate scattering function）作为基准。

Result: 该框架在模拟中成功复现了弛豫时间。应用于Pt57.5Cu14.7Ni5.3P22.5纳米线的实验时间分辨4D STEM数据集时，方法正确识别出高度稳定且不变的纳米晶相，而这些相在此前框架下被错误分类为弛豫区域。文章还基于这些发现重新评估了其他既有的ECM研究。

Conclusion: 此鲁棒方法为评估局域时空弛豫行为建立了无伪影的途径，纠正了以往ECM框架中存在的系统性误判问题。

Abstract: Electron correlation microscopy (ECM) can measure materials dynamics with nanoscale spatial resolution from intensity correlation functions. However, adopting X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) normalization frameworks unchanged when calculating intensity correlations can introduce errors. Due to the constrained sampling volumes and larger speckle sizes in nanobeam electron diffraction, XPCS-style time-averaging and scattering-vector averaging introduce systematic artifacts, such as artificial anticorrelations or elevated baselines that lead to systematic errors in structural relaxation times and stretching exponents. This work presents physics-inspired, ECM-specific intensity normalizations over time- and azimuthal-averaged intensities of the first diffraction ring that limit those errors. The framework is validated using molecular dynamics simulations of a CuZr supercooled liquid to benchmark against the self intermediate scattering function, successfully reproducing relaxation times. When applied to experimental time-resolved 4D STEM datasets of a Pt57.5Cu14.7Ni5.3P22.5 nanowire, the method correctly identifies highly stable, unchanging nanoscale crystalline phases that were erroneously misclassified as relaxing domains by previous frameworks. Other previous ECM research is reevaluated in light of these observation. This robust approach establishes an artifact-free pathway for evaluating localized spatiotemporal relaxation behaviors.

</details>


### [94] [Soft-Phonon-Driven Effective Inversion-Symmetry Crossover in Quantum Paraelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11471)
*Xiaojiang Li,Guodong Zhao,Fei Yang,Seng Huat Lee,Richard D. Schaller,Jong-Woo Kim,Matthew Krogstad,Long-Qing Chen,Philip J. Ryan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合光学二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）、漫散射X射线散射和微观理论，揭示了量子顺电体材料KTaO3中由声子调控的局部反演对称性破缺的温度依赖表现机制，证明氧缺陷介导的非线性光学通道与软极性声子模式强耦合，其热涨落可压制局部反演对称性破缺的非线性表现，从而在不伴随结构变化的情况下实现光学表现上的温度驱动交叉转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性是凝聚态物理的基础，光学二次谐波产生被广泛视为反演对称性破缺的特征指纹，但集体晶格动力学如何调控非线性光学中局部反演对称性破缺的表现仍然未知，这一空白构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 研究综合运用光学二次谐波产生（SHG）、漫散射X射线散射（diffuse X-ray scattering）以及微观理论（microscopic theory）三种方法，对量子顺电材料钛酸钾（KTaO3）进行系统表征与分析。

Result: 研究发现氧缺陷介导的非线性光学通道与宿主软极性声子模式（soft polar mode）存在强耦合；软声子的热涨落会扰乱相关的电子相位相干性，从而在升高的温度下压制局部反演对称性破缺的非线性表现。非线性响应表现出从光学可见的局部反演对称性破缺区域向有效中心对称区域的温度驱动交叉转变，且不伴随任何结构变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果修正了量子顺电体中反演对称性破缺温度依赖表现的传统认知，并为理解和工程调控具有低能极性激发（low-energy polar excitations）材料中缺陷介导的非线性光学响应建立了统一的框架。

Abstract: Symmetry lays the foundation of condensed matter physics and its experimental manifestation provides fundamental insight into the collective behaviors of quantum materials. Optical second-harmonic generation (SHG) is widely regarded as a fingerprint of inversion-symmetry breaking, yet whether and how collective lattice dynamics govern the nonlinear optical manifestation of local inversion-symmetry breaking remains unknown. Here, combining optical SHG, diffuse X-ray scattering, and microscopic theory, we reveal a phonon-regulated mechanism governing the temperature-dependent manifestation of local inversion-symmetry breaking in quantum paraelectric material KTaO3. We demonstrate that an oxygen-defect-mediated nonlinear optical channel is strongly coupled to the host soft polar mode, whose thermal fluctuations scramble the associated electronic phase coherence and thereby suppress the nonlinear manifestation of local inversion-symmetry breaking at elevated temperatures. Consequently, the nonlinear response exhibits a temperature-driven crossover from a regime in which local inversion-symmetry breaking is optically manifest to one that appears effectively centrosymmetric, without any accompanying structural change. Our findings revise the conventional picture of the temperature-dependent manifestation of inversion-symmetry breaking in quantum paraelectrics and establish a framework for understanding and engineering defect-mediated nonlinear optical responses in materials hosting low-energy polar excitations.

</details>


### [95] [Advances and opportunities for automated robotic preparation of 2D materials and fabrication of 2D heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11485)
*S. Davari,D. L. Duong,T. Faltermeier,J. A. Goss,A. Hasani,D. Kotekar-Patil,J. Peabody,S. Wyss,H. O. H. Churchill,N. J. Borys*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了近年来机器人仪器在二维（2D）材料制备与复杂二维异质结构（heterostructure）组装方面的科学、仪器与工艺进展，涵盖机械剥离、光学计量、堆叠及相关支撑技术，并展望了由人工智能（AI）驱动的自动化先进制造工具的发展前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华（van der Waals）材料的机械剥离、转移与堆叠技术使得逐原子层工程化二维异质结构成为可能，并催生了许多奇异物理现象。然而，目前高质量的二维异质结构制备主要依赖人工操作，随着结构复杂度持续提升，人工制备能力即将无法满足前沿科学需求，因此迫切需要开发机器人仪器以实现更高质量、更高通量和更好可重复性的自动化制备。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）方法，系统梳理了近年来在机械剥离（mechanical exfoliation）、二维晶体光学计量（optical metrology）、堆叠（stacking）等环节的机器人仪器进展，并涵盖无有机物印章（organic-free stamps）、真空兼容处理工具以及人工智能等支撑技术的相关发展。

Result: 综述显示，机器人仪器在二维材料制备与异质结构组装各关键环节已取得显著进展，相关支撑技术如无有机物印章、真空兼容处理工具和人工智能的应用正在加速推动自动化程度提升。预计新一代由人工智能驱动的自动化先进制造工具将从现有成果中涌现。

Conclusion: 当前的技术积累有望催生新一代AI驱动的自动化先进制造工具，这些工具将架起现有二维异质结构科学前沿与未来高质量、复杂多层二维异质结构系统精密制造之间的桥梁，推动该领域进入新的科学疆界。

Abstract: The mechanical exfoliation, transfer, and stacking of 2D atomic sheets from van der Waals crystals synergize to enable atomic layer-by-atomic layer engineering of 2D heterostructures with tailored properties that yield new exotic phenomena and states of matter. With the huge variety of van der Waals materials available, there is a limitless number of ways to couple 2D semiconducting, insulating, magnetic, metallic, topological, etc. systems with one another. Experimental exploration of this vast space starts with the fabrication of high-quality 2D heterostructures, which is commonly performed manually, relying on humans to execute delicate operations. Many scientific advancements have been achieved in this manner, revealing immense potential for further discovery and innovation of increasingly sophisticated 2D heterostructures. However, soon, the complexity of the 2D heterostructures that define the scientific state-of-the-art will exceed the capabilities of manual fabrication. Therefore, the demand for robotic instruments for preparing 2D materials and fabricating complex 2D heterostructures with greater quality, at higher rates, and with better reproducibility is increasing. This review covers recent scientific, instrumentation, and processing advances rising to this challenge. Robotic instruments for mechanical exfoliation, optical metrology of 2D crystallites, stacking, as well as advancements in supporting technologies such as organic-free stamps, vacuum-compatible processing tools, and artificial intelligence (AI) are covered. Looking forward, a new generation of AI-driven, automated advanced manufacturing tools is anticipated to emerge from these current advancements. These new tools will bridge the current state-of-the-art of 2D heterostructure science to new scientific frontiers defined by precision fabrication of high-quality, complex, many-layer 2D heterostructure systems.

</details>


### [96] [Magnetoelastic coupling descriptor for high-throughput ab initio search of magnetocaloric materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11589)
*Debajit Chakraborty,Kirill D. Belashchenko,Aleksander L. Wysocki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于朗道相变理论（Landau theory of phase transitions）设计了一个磁弹耦合描述符（magnetoelastic coupling descriptor），用于识别接近一级相变（first-order transition）且可能产生强磁热效应（magnetocaloric effect）的磁性材料。该描述符可从电子结构计算中求得，并在顺磁态下进行结构优化，通过特殊准随机共线自旋构型建模。在对已知磁热材料验证后，该方法被应用于L1₂化合物的高通量从头算筛选，最终识别出Mn₃Ge和Mn₃Sb的L1₂立方相为强磁弹耦合且具有大磁热响应的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁热效应通常出现在一级相变附近，但现有方法缺乏一个可直接从电子结构计算中获取的定量描述符来系统筛选此类材料。本文旨在利用朗道理论设计一个可计算的磁弹耦合描述符，实现高通量筛选具有强磁热潜力的磁性材料。

Method: 基于朗道相变理论构建磁弹耦合描述符，该描述符可从电子结构计算获得，并要求在顺磁态（paramagnetic state）下进行结构优化，通过特殊准随机共线自旋构型（special quasi-random collinear spin configurations）模拟顺磁态。首先在一组已知磁热材料上验证描述符的有效性，随后将其应用于磁性L1₂化合物的高通量从头算（ab initio）筛选。

Result: 描述符成功识别了已知磁热材料中由磁弹耦合驱动的磁热效应。高通量筛选进一步发现Mn₃Ge和Mn₃Sb的L1₂立方相具有强磁弹耦合，被预测为具有大磁热响应的潜在材料。

Conclusion: 该描述符为系统筛选具有强磁热效应的候选材料提供了一种可行的计算工具，并将Mn₃Ge和Mn₃Sb的L1₂相锁定为值得进一步实验验证的磁热材料候选。

Abstract: We use Landau theory of phase transitions to design a magnetoelastic coupling descriptor that can identify magnetic materials on the brink of a first-order transition, where a strong magnetocaloric effect (MCE) can arise. The descriptor can be computed from electronic structure calculations and requires structural optimization in the paramagnetic state, which we model using special quasi-random collinear spin configurations. We first evaluate the descriptor for a set of known magnetocaloric materials and identify compounds in which the MCE is driven by magnetoelastic coupling. We then apply the descriptor in a high-throughput \emph{ab initio} screening of magnetic L1$_2$ compounds. This search identifies the L1$_2$ cubic phases of Mn$_3$Ge and Mn$_3$Sb as materials with strong magnetoelastic coupling and potential for a large magnetocaloric response.

</details>


### [97] [First-principles cumulant approach to the vibronic structure of spin defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11687)
*Jinsoo Park,Yu Jin,Arpan Kundu,Jorge O. Sofo,Giulia Galli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于延迟累积量近似（retarded cumulant ansatz, RCA）的第一性原理格林函数框架，用于描述自旋缺陷中的电子-声子相互作用。将该方法应用于金刚石中带负电的氮空位中心（NV⁻），揭示了即使在零温下，多声子卫星结构也能覆盖400 meV的能量窗口，并发现其振动电子结构呈现出显著的自旋和轨道各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带隙半导体中的色心是固态量子技术的领先平台，但由于局域缺陷态中多声子过程的复杂性，对其振动电子结构（vibronic structure）的定量描述一直难以实现。

Method: 提出了一个基于延迟累积量近似的第一性原理格林函数框架来描述自旋缺陷中的电子-声子相互作用；该方法超越了广泛使用的方法所依赖的绝热近似（adiabatic approximation）和最低阶微扰论近似；需要在全布里渊区（Brillouin zone）采样模式、动量、自旋和轨道分辨的电子-声子矩阵元。

Result: 应用于金刚石中的NV⁻中心后，发现即使在零温下，多声子卫星结构可持续存在于400 meV的能量窗口中，其驱动力来自量子零点涨落（quantum zero-point fluctuation）；NV⁻中心的振动电子结构表现出强烈的自旋和轨道各向异性，不同轨道耦合到声子谱中性质不同的部分，且自旋选择性耦合同时影响边带位置和强度。

Conclusion: 精确的光谱函数计算要求在完整布里渊区采样分辨到模式、动量、自旋和轨道的电子-声子矩阵元，以正确考虑杂化和传播声子通道；RCA框架为定量描述色心振动电子结构提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Color centers in wide-band-gap semiconductors are leading platforms for solid-state quantum technologies, yet a quantitative description of their vibronic structure has remained elusive due to the complexity of multi-phonon processes in localized defect states. Here we present a first-principles Green's function framework based on the retarded cumulant ansatz (RCA) to describe electron-phonon interactions in spin defects; our approach goes beyond the adiabatic and lowest-order perturbation theory approximations underlying widely used approaches. Applied to the negatively charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) center in diamond, our method reveals that multi-phonon satellites persist over a 400 meV energy window even at zero temperature, driven by quantum zero-point fluctuations. We demonstrate that accurate spectral functions require mode-, momentum-, spin-, and orbital-resolved electron-phonon matrix elements sampled across the full Brillouin zone, to account for hybridized and propagating phonon channels. We find that the vibronic structure of the NV$^-$ center exhibits strong spin and orbital anisotropy, with different orbitals coupling to qualitatively distinct parts of the phonon spectrum, and spin-selective coupling affecting both sideband positions and intensities.

</details>


### [98] [Deep-Learning-Accelerated Dopant Selection for High-k HfO2 Dielectrics: A Disorder-Resolved Study of Y, Si and Al](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11725)
*Zunair Masroor,Bonwook Gu,Wonjoong Kim,Trinh Ngoc Le,Summal Zoha,Han-Bo-Ram Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个高通量计算框架，整合特殊准随机结构（special quasi-random structures）、机器学习势（machine-learning potentials，SevenNet）和图神经网络（graph neural networks，ALIGNN），系统评估了掺杂HfO2在三种掺杂剂（Al、Si、Y）和两种多晶型中的性能，揭示了形成焓、带隙与介电常数是解耦参数这一核心设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO2是现代硅技术中的基石高介电常数（high-k）介质材料，但其掺杂剂选择仍主要依赖经验方法，原因是热力学稳定性、电子绝缘性和介电响应之间存在相互耦合的复杂关系。由于硅器件制造工艺限制无法替换材料本身，掺杂是现有工艺流程内调控其带隙和介电常数的唯一主要手段，因此需要系统化的理论指导。

Method: 构建高通量计算框架，集成三种工具：特殊准随机结构（SQS）用于建模掺杂无序构型，机器学习势（SevenNet）加速能量与结构弛豫计算，图神经网络（ALIGNN）用于高效准确的属性预测。该框架系统评估了三种掺杂剂（Al、Si、Y）在单斜（monoclinic）和正交（orthorhombic）两种多晶型中的掺杂HfO2组分。

Result: 研究发现钇掺杂具有最低形成焓（-3.763 eV/atom），并在可兼容工艺热预算下有利于正交相稳定化；硅掺杂保持接近本征的带隙（约5.72 eV），对抑制栅介质中的漏电流至关重要；铝掺杂可实现浓度可调的带隙增宽（5.6-5.9 eV），适合电压尺度缩小（voltage scaling）应用。与实验文献和密度泛函理论（DFT）的对照验证了定量精度：硅掺杂带隙误差为0.02 eV，形成焓平均绝对误差小于0.001 eV/atom。

Conclusion: 形成焓、带隙和介电常数三者是解耦参数，需要依据具体应用场景进行优先级权衡而非同步优化，这为HfO2基介电材料及相关高介电常数氧化物体系的掺杂工程提供了理性的、以属性解耦为基础的指导原则。

Abstract: Hafnium oxide (HfO2) is the cornerstone high-k dielectric in modern silicon technology. Since the constraints of silicon device fabrication rule out replacing the material itself, dopant incorporation is the principal means available to engineer its band gap and dielectric constant within existing process flows. However, dopant selection is still largely empirical due to the coupled interplay among thermodynamic stability, electronic insulation, and dielectric response. Here, we present a high-throughput computational framework integrating special quasi-random structures (SQS), machine-learning potentials (SevenNet), and graph neural networks (ALIGNN) to systematically evaluate doped-HfO2 compositions across three dopants (Al, Si, Y) and two technologically relevant polymorphs (monoclinic and orthorhombic). Our analysis uncovers a fundamental design principle: formation energy, band gap, and dielectric constant are decoupled parameters requiring application-specific prioritization rather than simultaneous optimization. Yttrium achieves the lowest formation energy (-3.763 eV/atom) and favors orthorhombic phase stabilization at process-compatible thermal budgets; silicon preserves near-pristine band gaps (around 5.72 eV) critical for suppressing leakage in gate dielectric applications; and aluminum enables concentration-tunable band gap widening (5.6-5.9 eV) suited for voltage scaling. Validation against experimental literature and density functional theory (DFT) confirms quantitative accuracy (0.02 eV band gap error for Si-doping, mean absolute error less than 0.001 eV/atom formation energy). This framework provides rational, property-decoupled guidance for dopant engineering in HfO2-based dielectrics and related high-k oxide systems.

</details>


### [99] [Advancing in situ hydrogen embrittlement studies through an integrated charging cell for SEM micromechanical testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11773)
*Lavakumar Bathini,Guillaume Kermouche,Sergio Sao-Joao,Frédéric Christien,Szilvia Kalácska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于三电极（3-electrode）背面充氢（back-side charging）系统的新型扫描电子显微镜（SEM）原位微力学测试装置，并通过Fe-25Cr单晶（110）微柱压缩实验验证其可行性，发现氢（H）可提高屈服强度和表观应变硬化率，激活多滑移系并增强位错密度与缠结，导致显著的林位错硬化（forest hardening）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观尺度下氢-变形相互作用（hydrogen-deformation interactions）的全面理解对揭示氢脆（hydrogen embrittlement）机理至关重要，因此近年来原位微力学与原位充氢相结合的技术受到广泛关注。然而现有原位充氢装置存在诸多缺陷，亟需开发更稳健的试验系统。

Method: 开发了一种基于三电极的背面充氢系统，可集成于扫描电子显微镜内完成多种微力学测试，并用该装置对Fe-25Cr单晶（110）在充氢条件下进行微柱压缩（micropillar compression）试验，同时结合电子显微镜表征位错结构，并通过应变率跳变试验（strain-rate jump tests）估算激活体积（activation volume）。

Result: 氢显著提高了材料的屈服强度（yield strength）和表观应变硬化率（apparent strain-hardening rate）；电子显微观察表明，氢激活了多个滑移系并增强位错密度和位错缠结，导致显著的林位错硬化。激活体积估算结果显示，变形由溶质拖曳效应（solute drag effect）对扭折迁移率（kink mobility）的控制以及位错林硬化共同主导。

Conclusion: 新开发的三电极背面充氢原位微力学系统能有效克服现有装置的缺陷，可为氢脆机理研究提供有力工具；氢通过促进多滑移系激活和位错缠结增强林位错硬化，且变形机制涉及溶质拖曳效应与林位错硬化的协同作用。

Abstract: A comprehensive understanding of hydrogen-deformation interactions at the microscale is essential for revealing hydrogen embrittlement mechanisms. In situ micromechanics with simultaneous hydrogen (H-) charging has therefore gained traction in recent times. In the present study, we aim to address the drawbacks of current in situ H-charging setups by developing a more robust 3-electrode-based back-side charging system for a scanning electron microscope to perform various micromechanical tests. The development of the novel setup is discussed and demonstrated through micropillar compression of an Fe-25Cr single crystal (110) during H-charging. H has increased the yield strength and the apparent strain-hardening rate. H activates multiple slip systems and enhances dislocation density and entanglement, leading to pronounced forest hardening as revealed by electron microscopy. Estimation of activation volume from strain-rate jump tests indicates that the deformation is controlled by the solute drag effect on kink mobility and dislocation forest hardening.

</details>


### [100] [Lithography-free patterning of SrTiO$_3$-based two-dimensional electron gases using direct atomic layer processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11809)
*Anshu Gupta,Karolis Parfeniukas,Amit Chanda,Thor Hvid-Olsen,Mira Baraket,Maksym Plakhotnyuk,Kasper S. Pedersen,Felix Trier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种可规模化、免光刻（lithography-free）的策略，通过在TiO₂图形化SrTiO₃(100)表面沉积Al，利用磁控溅射实现二维电子气（2DEG）的制备。该方法无需后生长微加工即可直接进行输运测量，磁输运测得载流子密度约为5–7×10¹³ cm⁻²，与脉冲激光沉积制备的SrTiO₃基异质结构相当，并具备有效静电调谐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有氧化物二维电子气的制备多依赖光刻和复杂的后生长加工步骤，工艺成本高且可扩展性有限。本文旨在开发一种简单、经济且与工业兼容的免光刻策略，以实现在SrTiO₃中可靠地构筑二维电子气，为规模化器件制造提供途径。

Method: 采用磁控溅射在TiO₂图形化的SrTiO₃(100)衬底上沉积Al；其中15 nm厚的TiO₂层通过直接原子层处理（direct atomic layer processing）沉积，用于空间界定导电区域。Al沉积后在无TiO₂覆盖区域形成绝缘的AlOₓ覆盖层，同时诱导SrTiO₃中产生氧空位（oxygen vacancies），其作为电子施主填充Ti 3d导带，在界面处形成受限的二维电子气。

Result: 磁输运测量显示片载流子密度约为5–7×10¹³ cm⁻²，与脉冲激光沉积生长的SrTiO₃基异质结构水平相当；同时该2DEG表现出有效的静电可调谐性（electrostatic tunability）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种简单、经济且与产业兼容的氧化物二维电子气工程方法，为可扩展器件制备和界面输运研究提供了通用平台。

Abstract: We present a scalable and lithography-free strategy for the realization of a two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) in TiO$_2$-patterned SrTiO$_3$ (100) via Al deposition using magnetron sputtering. A 15 nm thick TiO$_2$ layer, deposited by direct atomic layer processing, is employed to spatially define the conducting regions, enabling direct transport measurements without post-growth microfabrication. Upon Al deposition, an insulating AlO$_x$ overlayer is formed, and the region lacking the TiO$_2$ pattern leads to the creation of oxygen vacancies in SrTiO$_3$. These oxygen vacancies act as electron donors, populating the Ti 3$d$ conduction bands and giving rise to a confined 2DEG at the interface. Magneto-transport measurements reveal a sheet carrier density on the order of $\approx5-7\times10^{13}$ cm$^{-2}$, comparable to values typically achieved in pulsed laser deposition-grown SrTiO$_3$-based heterostructures, along with effective electrostatic tunability. This work demonstrates a simple, cost-effective, and industry-compatible route for engineering oxide 2DEGs, providing a versatile platform for scalable device fabrication and interfacial transport studies.

</details>


### [101] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of DPA-4 Force Fields to DFT+U Energetics: A Case Study in NiO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11812)
*Fengyu Xie,Peiheng Jiang,Zhicheng Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以NiO为案例，验证了基础机器学习力场（MLFF）在源层面具有错误的相能量学（phase energetics）时，可通过目标层面的微调（fine-tuning）进行有效纠正。预训练的DPA-4模型仅需约170个PBE+U标签即可达到能量和力均方根误差分别约0.5 meV/原子和30 meV/Å的精度，且先前在相反能面（no-U）上微调的模型仍能以近乎相同的目标数据效率恢复正确的PBE+U相序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习力场通常在海量的材料数据集上预训练，其电子结构约定可能无法复现特定关联材料所需的相能量学。作者希望探讨：当源层面的相能量学不正确时，能否通过目标层面的微调来高效修正这一偏差。

Method: 以NiO为案例，沿一条常见的结构插值路径，比较非自旋极化PBE（non-spin-polarized PBE）与铁磁PBE+U（ferromagnetic PBE+U）对八面体（Oct）和平面正方形（Sqr）两相能量排序的预测差异。在此基础上，对预训练的DPA-4模型以及预先在no-U能面上微调过的模型分别进行PBE+U目标层面的微调，并评估其数据效率与精度。

Result: 预训练DPA-4模型在约170个PBE+U标签下实现了约0.5 meV/原子和30 meV/Å的能量与力均方根误差。先前在相反的no-U能面上微调的模型能够恢复正确的PBE+U相序，且其目标数据效率与直接从预训练初始化出发微调的模型几乎相同。

Conclusion: 错误的源层面相能量学可以通过目标层面的微调得以反转。该结果支持一种实用多保真度（multi-fidelity）策略：预训练阶段优先获取广泛、一致且成本可承受的数据，而通过紧凑的目标层面数据集配合应用特定的微调来植入正确的能量学。

Abstract: Foundation machine-learned force fields (MLFFs) are often pretrained on broad materials datasets whose electronic-structure conventions may not reproduce the phase energetics required for a specific correlated material. Using NiO as a case study, we examine whether incorrect source-level phase energetics can be corrected efficiently through target-level fine-tuning. Along a common structural interpolation, non-spin-polarized PBE and ferromagnetic PBE+U predict opposite energetic orderings of the octahedral Oct and square-planar Sqr phases. Pretrained DPA-4 models adapt rapidly to the NiO PBE+U surface, reaching energy and force root-mean-square errors (RMSEs) of approximately 0.5 meV/atom and 30 meV/Å, respectively, with approximately 170 PBE+U labels. Crucially, models previously fine-tuned to the opposing no-U surface recover the qualitative PBE+U phase ordering with nearly the same target-data efficiency as models fine-tuned directly from their respective pretrained initializations. Our results show that incorrect source-level phase energetics can be reversed through target-level fine-tuning, and suggest a practical multi-fidelity strategy in which pretraining prioritizes broad, consistent, and affordable data, while compact target-level datasets impose energetics through application-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [102] [Optically Tunable Threshold Switching and Thermally Activated Transport in Planar Ag/MAPbI$_3$ Thin Single-Crystal Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11832)
*Ofelia Durante,Valeria Demontis,Sebastiano De Stefano,Selene Matta,Adolfo Mazzotti,Daniela Marongiu,Emanuele Meloni,Elisa Pili,Fang Liu,Nicola Sestu,Angelica Simbula,Mauro Carta,Michele Saba,Andrea Mura,Massimiliano Di Ventra,Giovanni Bongiovanni,Antonio Di Bartolomeo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过空间限定法生长出薄层甲基铵铅碘(MAPbI₃)钙钛矿单晶，并将其集成到具有银(Ag)直接接触的平面两端器件中。器件在暗场下表现出超低暗电流和可忽略的迟滞，而在光照下则出现明显的极性依赖迟滞以及两个电导态之间的阈值型转变，揭示了Ag/钙钛矿界面与离子过程耦合的输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿的混合离子-电子导电性正从稳定性问题转变为存储和类神经形态器件的功能自由度，尤其在光照等外部刺激下。单晶因其抑制晶界效应和微结构无序而成为研究本征输运与界面机制的重要模型，但目前对其在光照下迟滞行为的内在机制仍缺乏清晰理解。

Method: 采用空间限定法生长薄的MAPbI₃单晶，并将其集成到具有直接沉积Ag接触的平面两端器件中。通过室温暗场电流-电压(I-V)测量表征其基础电学特性，进一步在光照条件下测量I-V特性以观察迟滞和电导态转变；结合300-400 K温度依赖暗测量、背对背肖特基二极管(back-to-back Schottky diode)分析，以及与更惰性接触材料的对照器件，综合分析传输机制。

Result: 室温暗场下器件显示超低暗电流(10⁻¹³-10⁻¹² A)和可忽略的迟滞；光照下电流因光生载流子而增大，I-V曲线出现明显的极性依赖迟滞和两个电导态之间的阈值型转变。温度依赖暗测量(300-400 K)显示热离子激活的、受接触影响的输运以及弱变化的归一化迟滞指标。

Conclusion: 结合背对背肖特基二极管分析和惰性接触对照实验，结果表明Ag/钙钛矿界面在输运中起核心作用，迟滞响应受界面过程和离子过程的耦合影响。该工作支持将混合离子-电子传导与界面工程相结合的器件设计思路，为钙钛矿在神经形态和记忆器件中的应用提供了机制层面的理解。

Abstract: Halide perovskites have enabled major advances in optoelectronics, extending well beyond photovoltaics. Their mixed ionic-electronic conduction, once regarded as detrimental to device stability, is increasingly viewed as a functional degree of freedom for memory and neuromorphic-inspired devices, especially when coupled to external stimuli such as light. Specifically, single crystals are attractive models because they suppress grain-boundary effects and microstructural disorder that can mask intrinsic transport and interfacial mechanisms in polycrystalline films. Here, we report the growth of thin methylammonium lead iodide (MAPbI$_3$) single crystals by a space-confined method and their integration into planar two-terminal devices with directly deposited Ag contacts. At room temperature, the devices exhibit ultra-low dark currents ($10^{-13}-10^{-12}$ A) and negligible hysteresis in the dark. Under illumination, the current increases due to photogeneration and the I-V characteristics develop a pronounced polarity-dependent hysteresis and a threshold-like transition between two conductance states. Temperature-dependent dark measurements (300-400 K) show thermionically activated, contact-influenced transport and a weakly varying normalized hysteresis metric. Together with the back-to-back Schottky-diode analysis and control devices using more inert contact materials, these results support a transport model in which Ag/perovskite interfaces play a central role and the hysteretic response is influenced by coupled interfacial and ionic processes.

</details>


### [103] [Strain-controlled sign reversal of the anomalous Hall effect in Ru/[Co/Ni]$_N$ multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11897)
*Jingying Zhang,Sigang Wang,Yue Xiang,Wenhui Xie,Zhe Yuan,Yi Liu,Zongzhi Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了Ru/[Co/Ni]$_N$多层膜中由Ru层的堆叠顺序控制的异常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）符号反转现象，并揭示其物理根源在于Ru底层对面内张应变（in-plane tensile strain）的施加，该应变重塑了费米能级附近的电子结构和贝里曲率（Berry curvature）分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常霍尔效应是铁磁体中由相对论性自旋-轨道相互作用（spin-orbit interaction）引起的标志性输运现象，但如何有效调控其符号和强度仍是未解问题，本文旨在探索通过界面应变实现对贝里曲率驱动输运的调控途径。

Method: 系统改变多层膜重复层数N，结合输运测量（transport measurements）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），对比Ru层置于Co/Ni多层膜上层与下层两种堆叠结构下的异常霍尔信号。

Result: 当Ru层置于Co/Ni多层膜下方而非上方时，异常霍尔信号从正号切换为负号；该反转源于Ru底层施加的面内张应变，它重塑了电子结构并在费米能级附近重新分布贝里曲率。

Conclusion: 界面应变可作为调控贝里曲率驱动输运的有效手段，为磁性多层膜中应变控制的拓扑输运现象（topological transport phenomena）提供新路径。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) is a hallmark transport phenomenon in ferromagnets arising from relativistic spin-orbit interaction. Here, we report an unexpected sign reversal of the AHE in Ru/[Co/Ni]$_N$ multilayers controlled by the stacking sequence of the Ru layer. When Ru is placed beneath, rather than atop, the Co/Ni multilayers, the anomalous Hall signal switches from positive to negative. By systematically varying the multilayer repeat number N and combining transport measurements with first-principles calculations, we show that this reversal originates from in-plane tensile strain imposed by the Ru underlayer, which reshapes the electronic structure and redistributes Berry curvature near the Fermi level. Our findings establish interfacial strain as an effective knob for tuning Berry-curvature-driven transport and suggest a pathway toward strain-controlled topological transport phenomena in magnetic multilayers.

</details>


### [104] [Magneto-optical magnetoelectric voltage sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11910)
*Michael P. Path,Jeffrey McCord*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种基于应变介导磁电耦合与磁光读取的新型电压传感器，利用掺铋钇铁石榴石磁光指示膜与压电致动器的机械耦合实现电压感应，并通过偏置场设置畴结构临界态，在毫伏每根赫兹量级的等效电压噪声密度下同时实现交流和直流电压读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压和电磁恶劣环境中的应用需要可靠的电流隔离电压传感，可通过光学读取实现。现有的光学电压传感器依赖电光效应或压电应变结合直接光学检测，而应变介导磁电耦合结合磁光读取的替代电压传感原理基本未被探索。

Method: 传感器由掺铋钇铁石榴石（bismuth-substituted yttrium iron garnet）磁光指示膜与压电致动器（piezoelectric actuator）机械耦合构成。电压感生的应力通过磁弹耦合（magnetoelastic coupling）改变面外磁化，经磁光法拉第旋转（Faraday rotation）检测。通过外加偏置场设定畴结构临界态，使电压感生的成核与畴壁运动主导响应。

Result: 在高灵敏度区实现了交流和直流电压读取两种模式，分别基于电压驱动的磁化反转和电压感生的磁化回路形状改变。等效电压噪声密度达到毫伏每根赫兹（mV/√Hz）量级。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了应变介导磁光电压传感（strain-mediated magneto-optical voltage sensing）作为光学隔离电压测量的一种独特方法。

Abstract: Applications in high-voltage and electromagnetically harsh environments require reliable galvanically isolated voltage sensing, which can be achieved using optical readout. While established optical voltage sensors rely on electro-optic effects or piezoelectric strain with direct optical detection, strain-mediated magnetoelectric coupling combined with magneto-optical readout offers an alternative voltage sensing principle that remains largely unexplored. Here, such a sensor based on a bismuth-substituted yttrium iron garnet magneto-optical indicator film mechanically coupled to a piezoelectric actuator is presented. Voltage induced stress results in changes of the out-of-plane magnetization via magnetoelastic coupling which is detected through magneto-optical Faraday rotation. A critical state of the domain structure is set via an applied bias field, in which voltage-induced nucleation and domain-wall motion dominates the response. In this high sensitivity regime, both AC and DC voltage readout modes are demonstrated, based on either voltage-driven magnetization reversal or voltage-induced modifications of the magnetization loop shape. Equivalent voltage noise densities in the millivolt per root hertz range are achieved. The results establish strain-mediated magneto-optical voltage sensing as a distinct approach to optically isolated voltage measurement.

</details>


### [105] [Resonant Raman signatures of bright and momentum-dark exciton coupled by intervalley phonon scattering in monolayer WSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11914)
*Hendrik Lambers,Nihit Saigal,Lara Blinov,Jonas Kiemle,Alexander W. Holleitner,Ursula Wurstbauer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过低温共振拉曼光谱（resonance Raman spectroscopy）在hBN封装WSe_2单层中探测激子-声子耦合，发现A_1'/E'光学声子模式的共振轮廓呈现两个不对称共振峰，其能量间隔远超声子能量。通过引入涉及有限动量声子的三阶拉曼散射（third-order Raman scattering），模型成功解释了该异常，并拟合出动量暗激子（momentum-dark exciton）X_d位于亮激子（bright exciton）X_KK下方约45-55 meV，表明亮态到暗态的耦合效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单原子层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides）中激子-声子耦合支配着激子热化和带间散射等关键过程，但通过光学光谱直接探测该耦合具有挑战性，亟需建立微观框架来理解WSe_2单层中异常明亮的发射光谱。

Method: 在低温下对hBN封装的WSe_2单层进行共振拉曼光谱测量，将激发激光调谐跨越亮激子X_KK共振，获得丰富的拉曼光谱并聚焦于A_1'/E'光学声子模式的共振轮廓。将包含相干一阶和二阶（三阶）散射的理论模型与实验共振轮廓进行拟合。

Result: 观测到A_1'/E'模式共振轮廓呈现两个不对称共振峰，其能量间隔显著超过声子能量。加入三阶拉曼散射（涉及有限动量声子实现的从亮激子X_KK到动量暗激子X_d的带间散射）后模型与实验一致，两个独立样本拟合得到一致的激子能量和线宽，动量暗激子位于亮激子下方约45-55 meV。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明亮态到暗态的激子耦合效率高，为理解WSe_2单层尽管最低激子跃迁是自旋禁止的但仍具有异常明亮发射光谱提供了微观框架，并强调了动量暗激子在共振光-物质相互作用中的关键作用。

Abstract: Exciton-phonon coupling in atomically thin transition metal dichalcogenides governs key processes such as exciton thermalization and intervalley scattering and remains challenging to access directly by optical spectroscopy. Here, we employ resonance Raman spectroscopy at cryogenic temperatures to probe exciton-phonon coupling in hBN-encapsulated WSe_2 monolayers. Tuning the excitation laser across the bright exciton X_KK resonance, we observe rich Raman spectra and focus on the resonance profile of the degenerate A_1'/E' optical phonon mode. The profile exhibits two asymmetric resonance peaks whose energetic separation significantly exceeds the phonon energy - a feature that cannot be explained by first-order Raman scattering alone. We demonstrate that this discrepancy is resolved by including third-order Raman scattering, in which intervalley scattering enabled by a finite-momentum phonon couples the bright exciton X_KK to a momentum-dark exciton X_d. Fitting the experimental resonance profiles of two independent samples with a model comprising coherent first- and third-order scattering yields consistent exciton energies and linewidths, with a momentum-dark exciton X_d approximately 45meV to 55meV below the bright exciton X_KK. The results indicate efficient bright-to-dark exciton coupling. Our findings provide a microscopic framework for understanding the anomalously bright emission spectra of WSe_2 monolayers despite its spin-forbidden lowest exciton transition and highlight the role of momentum-dark excitons in resonant light-matter interaction.

</details>


### [106] [Electronic structure, band offset, and interface electron population of the LaInO$_3$/BaSnO$_3$ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11952)
*G. Hoffmann,A. A. Riaz,C. Kalha,A. Gloskovskii,C. Schlueter,O. Brandt,W. Aggoune,C. Draxl,O. Bierwagen,A. Regoutz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过软 X 射线光电子能谱（SXPS）和硬 X 射线光电子能谱（HAXPES）实验，结合基于杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid DFT）计算的态密度（DOS），系统研究了 BaSnO₃、LaInO₃ 及其异质结（heterostructure）的电子结构与化学键合，并利用 HAXPES 直接探测了 BaSnO₃/LaInO₃ 界面处的能带偏移（band offset）和二维电子气（2DEG）态占据情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxides）及其异质结构具有丰富的功能性质，BaSnO₃/LaInO₃ 异质结界面可形成高迁移率二维电子气，且近期薄膜生长技术的进步已实现室温电子迁移率超过 100 cm²/Vs。然而，对该体系电子结构的系统理解仍不充分，为此需要结合实验光谱与理论计算对其进行全面表征。

Method: 采用软 X 射线与硬 X 射线光电子能谱（SXPS 和 HAXPES）分别测量 BaSnO₃、LaInO₃ 及其不同 LaInO₃ 覆盖层厚度的异质结样品；同时基于杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid DFT）计算导出态密度（DOS）。通过分析芯能级（core）、半芯能级（semi-core）和价带（valence）态，从实验与理论两方面交叉验证体系的电子结构与化学键合。

Result: 研究获得了 BaSnO₃、LaInO₃ 及其异质结中化学键合与电子结构的综合认识；在 BaSnO₃/LaInO₃ 异质结中，利用 HAXPES 直接探测了界面处的能带偏移及 2DEG 态的占据情况，从而量化了界面电子态的行为随 LaInO₃ 覆盖层厚度变化的规律。

Conclusion: 实验与理论相结合的方法能够全面揭示 BaSnO₃/LaInO₃ 异质结构中的电子结构细节，且 HAXPES 可直接表征界面 2DEG 态的占据行为，这为理解和优化该类高迁移率氧化物异质结提供了关键的电子结构依据。

Abstract: Perovskite oxides and their heterostructures exhibit a wide range of functional properties. Among these materials, BaSnO$_3$/LaInO$_3$ heterostructures form high-mobility two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs) at their interfaces. In particular, room-temperature electron mobilities exceeding 100~cm$^2$/Vs were enabled by recent advances in thin-film growth. This work presents a combined experimental and theoretical study of the electronic structure of BaSnO$_3$, LaInO$_3$, and BaSnO$_3$/LaInO$_3$ heterostructures with varying LaInO$_3$ overlayer thicknesses. Soft and hard X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (SXPS and HAXPES) measurements are combined with densities of states (DOS) derived from hybrid density functional theory (DFT) calculations. The analysis of core, semi-core, and valence states allows to arrive at a comprehensive understanding of the chemical bonding and electronic structure in the parent oxides as well as the formed heterostructures. For the BaSnO$_3$/LaInO$_3$ heterostructure, the band offset and population of 2DEG states at the interface is directly probed using HAXPES.

</details>


### [107] [Energy-Dependent Dechanneling in Cu: Insights from Monte Carlo Channeling Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12017)
*Przemyslaw Jozwik,Cyprian Mieszczynski,Renata Ratajczak,Andrzej Turos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于离子沟道（ion channeling）谱模拟的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方法及其配套软件McChasy，该方法能够在广泛的探测束能量范围内复现实验谱，并对晶体材料中的点缺陷（point defects）与扩展缺陷（extended defects）进行定量识别。通过重现铜晶体沟道谱中的特征"肘状"结构，验证了程序对多种晶体体系的建模与缺陷分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子沟道和背散射技术是研究晶体材料晶格无序和缺陷结构的有力工具，但准确解释沟道现象需要能够描述点缺陷、位错及扩展缺陷簇之间复杂相互作用的仿真模型。现有方法在定量识别缺陷类型和分布方面存在局限性，亟需一种能够跨不同束能量范围有效复现实验谱的通用模拟工具。

Method: 提出一种蒙特卡洛模拟方法，结合离子沟道实验数据与缺陷建模，在广泛的探测束能量范围内复现实验沟道谱。基于该方法开发了McChasy程序，支持对复杂半导体、多层外延薄膜及氧化物晶体等多种晶体体系进行灵活建模。该方法通过分析缺陷引起的能量依赖特征来区分点缺陷与扩展缺陷。

Result: 模拟结果显示，点缺陷与扩展缺陷展现出各自特征性的能量依赖行为，可据此实现缺陷类型的定量识别。McChasy程序成功复现了铜晶体沟道谱中此前实验中观察到的特征"肘状"（elbows）结构，验证了其模拟可靠性。

Conclusion: McChasy程序将离子沟道实验数据与缺陷建模相结合，为材料科学中的缺陷分析建立了稳健框架，可作为科学家研究金属和半导体中离子注入等过程所引起损伤的灵活、易用的工具。该框架为缺陷类型的定量识别与分布表征提供了新视角。

Abstract: Ion channeling and backscattering techniques are powerful tools for studying crystal lattice disorders and defect structures in crystalline materials. However, the accurate interpretation of channeling phenomena necessitates the utilization of simulation models that account for the intricate interactions between point defects, dislocations, and extended defect clusters. The present paper introduces a Monte Carlo method that reproduces experimental spectra over a wide range of analyzing beam energies and enables quantitative identification of defect types and distributions. The simulations reveal characteristic energy dependencies that distinguish point defects from extended defects, offering a novel perspective on disturbances caused, for example, by ion implantation in metals and semiconductors. To this end, the McChasy code has been developed as a flexible and accessible tool for scientists, enabling the modeling of various crystal systems, including complex semiconductors, multilayer epitaxial films, and oxide crystals. The program's integration of experimental data on ion channeling with defect modeling establishes a robust framework for defect analysis in materials science. The present article expounds upon the simulation capabilities of the program by reproducing the characteristic "elbows" in channeling spectra that were previously observed in experiments conducted on Cu crystals.

</details>


### [108] [Thermoelastic Harvesting Outperforming Thermoelectric Generators Below 100 °C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12092)
*Bruno Neumann,Andreas Henschke,Morik Nikolic,Sebastian Fähler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型热弹性（thermoelastic）废热收集器，通过三种系统设计变革（主角-对手架构、连续可调预应变机制、横向水流），实现了366 mW/cm³的直接测量功率密度，为目前所有热弹性器件中最优，并超越该温度范围内的热磁、热释电及热电发电器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低于100°C的低品位废热是固态能源转换领域中最大的未开发机会之一，而热弹性收集器作为三种铁性（ferroic）候选路线中最未被探索的路径，尽管其底层NiTi形状记忆合金（shape-memory alloy）线材已有数十年研究进展，仍缺乏有效的系统级设计。

Method: 作者从系统设计层面引入三项关键变革：采用主角-对手（protagonist-antagonist）架构以回收预应变所需的能量；设计连续可调的预应变机制以设定力-应变平衡；利用横向水流（transversal water flow）将循环频率与线长解耦。系统通过力和位移的直接测量绘制参数空间，不依赖材料性质估算。

Result: 该收集器以有效材料体积计算，实现了366 mW/cm³的直接测量功率密度，约为次优热弹性器件的1.7倍，优于所有已报道的热磁（thermomagnetic）和热释电（pyroelectric）发电机，并在该温度范围内相较于最佳热电（thermoelectric）发电机具有单位材料成本功耗优势。

Conclusion: 通过系统性设计改进，热弹性收集器从最未被探索的废热回收路线跃升为具竞争力的方案，在功率密度和材料成本效益上均表现出领先地位，为低品位废热回收提供了可行的固态能源转换路径。

Abstract: Low-grade waste heat below 100 degC is one of the largest untapped opportunities in solid-state energy conversion. Three ferroic routes are candidates for recovering this resource: thermomagnetic, pyroelectric, and thermoelastic harvesters. The last has remained the most unexplored, despite decades of progress on the underlying NiTi shape-memory alloy wires. Three system-design changes close this gap: a protagonist-antagonist architecture that recovers the energy for prestraining, a continuously tunable prestrain mechanism that sets the force-strain balance, and transversal water flow that decouples cycle frequency from wire length. The resulting harvester delivers a directly measured power density of 366 mW/cm^3 with respect to the active material, about 1.7 times the next-best thermoelastic device, ahead of every reported thermomagnetic and pyroelectric generator, and outperforming the best thermoelectric generators in this temperature range also with respect to power per material cost. The system maps the parameter space directly through force and displacement measurements, without using material-property estimates, giving a quantitative picture of how the alloy responds while the device is doing work.

</details>


### [109] [Beam Routing through Excitons in Transition Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12105)
*Yonas Lebsir,Jacob Terndrup Heiden,Jorge Barcia Rodríguez,Maria Papadopoulou,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,N. Asger Mortensen,Sergii Morozov,Nicolas Ubrig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用低温、角分辨阴极发光（cathodoluminescence）光谱技术，证明了半导体本征激子跃迁本身即可产生定向发光。作者在WSe₂、MoSe₂和MoTe₂单层中区分出亮激子、trion和自旋禁戒暗激子（spin-forbidden dark exciton），并发现面外偶极矩的暗激子能在掠射角方向产生定向发射通道，无需额外纳米结构即可实现纳米尺度的光路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度上路由光通常依赖纳米结构表面来赋予发射方向性，期望探索是否可以不借助纳米结构，仅利用材料本征的激子跃迁来实现定向发射。

Method: 采用低温、角分辨阴极发光光谱技术，对WSe₂、MoSe₂和MoTe₂单层进行探测，通过分辨各激子物种不同的角分辨发射剖面（angular emission profiles），并结合局部介电环境的调节来调控中性激子与带电激子发射之间的平衡。

Result: 亮激子与trion的面内跃迁偶极主要朝表面法线方向辐射，而暗激子的面外偶极在掠射角（大角度）方向产生定向发射通道；这些激子物种可通过各自独特的角发射分布加以分辨。进一步通过局部介电环境调控，可在中性激子与带电激子发射之间进行调节。

Conclusion: 暗激子可作为过渡金属二硫族化合物（TMD）单层中定向光发射的平台，无需额外的纳米结构化处理即可在紧凑光子架构中实现纳米尺度的光路由。

Abstract: Routing light at the nanoscale typically relies on nanostructured surfaces to imprint directionality on the emission. Using low-temperature, angle-resolved cathodoluminescence spectroscopy, we show that the intrinsic excitonic transitions of a semiconductor can themselves produce routed emission. We probe monolayers of WSe$_2$, MoSe$_2$, and MoTe$_2$ and resolve the excitonic species of monolayer WSe$_2$ -- the bright exciton, the trion, and the spin-forbidden dark exciton -- through their distinct angular emission profiles. While the in-plane transition dipoles of the bright exciton and trion radiate predominantly toward the surface normal, the out-of-plane dipole of the dark exciton, inaccessible under normal-incidence optical excitation, produces a directional emission channel at large angles. We further tune the balance between neutral and charged exciton emission through the local dielectric environment. Our results establish dark excitons in TMD monolayers as a platform for directional light emission in compact photonic architectures without additional nanostructuring.

</details>


### [110] [The electrical transport of intrinsic two-dimensional ferroelectric metal PtBi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12152)
*Dan Li,Liu Yang,Lei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过半经典玻尔兹曼方程（semiclassical Boltzmann equation）和第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），系统研究了二维铁电金属（2D ferroelectric metal）PtBi₂的线性和非线性输运响应，揭示了其高温居里温度（Curie temperature）高达800 K，并提出了一种基于畴壁散射机制的铁电金属场效应晶体管（FEM-FET）设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为铁电体必然是绝缘的，而二维铁电金属打破了这一偏见，兼具可切换电极化（switchable electric polarization）与金属导电性（metallic conductivity），为新型电输运现象和电子器件提供了独特平台，但其丰富的输运物理尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 采用半经典玻尔兹曼方程与第一性原理计算系统研究线性和非线性输运响应；利用从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）模拟确定居里温度；定量计算埃德尔斯坦效应（Edelstein effect）与本征自旋霍尔效应（intrinsic spin Hall effect）评估电荷-自旋转换效率；基于贝里曲率偶极子（Berry curvature dipole）分析非线性霍尔效应；并基于畴壁散射机制设计场效应晶体管。

Result: PtBi₂具有高达800 K的居里温度；高温顺电相的晶体结构可通过面内电导率的简单测量加以区分；其电荷-自旋转换效率可观，具有自旋电子学（spintronics）应用潜力；在单轴应变下出现贝里曲率偶极子诱导的非线性霍尔效应；设计的FEM-FET可在栅电压作用下实现高阻与低阻态之间的非易失性切换。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了二维铁电金属中丰富的输运物理，为下一代非易失性存储器（nonvolatile memory）和自旋电子学器件的设计提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Breaking the conventional stereotype that ferroelectrics are necessarily insulating, two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric metals combine seemingly incompatible switchable electric polarization and metallic conductivity, providing a fertile ground for the discovery of novel electrical transport phenomena and the development of innovative electronic devices. Using the semiclassical Boltzmann equation and first-principles calculations, we systematically investigate the linear and nonlinear transport responses of the intrinsic 2D ferroelectric metal \ch{PtBi2} to an applied electric field. Our \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics simulations reveal that it possesses a high Curie temperature reaching $800~\text{K}$. We propose that the crystal structure of its high-temperature paraelectric phase can be explicitly distinguished through simple measurements of the in-plane electrical conductivity. Quantitative calculations of the Edelstein effect and the intrinsic spin Hall effect demonstrate a sizable charge-to-spin conversion efficiency, highlighting its potential in spintronics. We also find that a Berry curvature dipole-induced nonlinear Hall effect emerges in uniaxially strained \ch{PtBi2}. Furthermore, we highlight the unique advantages of 2D ferroelectric metals in gate-controlled transport applications. Based on the domain wall scattering mechanism, we conceptually design a novel ferroelectric metal field-effect transistor (FEM-FET) capable of nonvolatile switching between high-resistance and low-resistance states under a gate voltage. Our work not only unveils the rich transport physics in 2D ferroelectric metals but also provides valuable insights into the design of next-generation nonvolatile memory and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [111] [PACE-SIMS: Checkpoint-Gated Autonomous SIMS Characterization with AI-Agent Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12277)
*Anton V Ievlev,Heather Hare,Yiyang Li,Sergei V Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-SIMS，一种基于智能体（agentic）工作流的人机协作框架，用于自动运行飞行时间二次离子质谱（ToF-SIMS）实验。研究者仅需指定科学问题和质量要求，AI智能体即可制定计划并在批准后自主执行测量，在检查点暂停以判断每次测量的质量并纠正、重试或上报。在18O富集WOx薄膜的盲随机双极性研究中验证了该方法，智能体做出了三项固定脚本会遗漏的纠错，四项预测全部与密封标注的真实值相符，且以不到两小时的专家注意力完成了整个8.1小时的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ToF-SIMS分析虽广泛用于材料局部化学研究，但其操作高度依赖专家时间：训练有素的研究人员必须在整个实验过程中动态调参并全程监督，通常跨越数天。这种专家密集型的运行模式成本高昂，亟需一种自动化方法来减少人工参与，同时保持测量质量。

Method: 作者开发了PACE-SIMS智能体工作流，以人类-AI协作为核心架构：研究者输入科学问题和质量要求，AI智能体据此构建实验计划，经研究者批准后自主执行；执行过程中在设定检查点暂停，智能体自主判断每次测量的质量，必要时进行纠正、重试或将问题升级给人类。该架构设计为分析技术无关、可泛化的通用框架。

Result: 将该方法应用于18O富集WOx薄膜的双极性（two-polarity）盲随机化学组分研究，整个实验耗时8.1小时、包含35次测量。智能体在此过程中做出了三项固定脚本无法发现的无脚本纠正，全部四项预测均与密封的实际值一致。同时产出了可迁移的测量科学结果：组分校准、两种离子极性间5.3%的同位素读数偏差，以及沉积过程的示踪剂递送机制，全程仅需不到两小时的专家关注。

Conclusion: PACE-SIMS验证了智能体驱动的人机协作可显著降低ToF-SIMS实验对专家时间的依赖，同时保持甚至提升测量质量。该智能体架构不限于SIMS技术，可推广至其他分析手段，尤其适用于传统优化方法难以有效应用的破坏性测量场景。

Abstract: Time-of-flight secondary ion mass spectrometry (ToF-SIMS) is widely used for local chemical investigations across a broad range of materials and systems. However, its operation is expensive in expert time: a trained researcher must supervise acquisition throughout, dynamically tuning parameters throughout the experiment, often across a campaign spanning multiple days. Here, we present PACE-SIMS, an agentic workflow which runs a SIMS study as a human-AI collaboration. Here, the researcher specifies the scientific questions and quality requirements, and an AI agent builds the plan and, after approval, executes it autonomously, pausing at checkpoints to judge each measurement and to correct, retry, or escalate. To validate the approach, we applied it to a study of chemical composition in 18O-enriched WOx films. During this blind randomized, two-polarity study (8.1 hours, 35 measurements) the agent made three unscripted corrections a fixed script would have missed, and all four predictions held against the sealed ground truth. The same run returned transferable measurement science, including a composition calibration, a 5.3% isotope-reading offset between the two ion polarities, and the deposition's tracer-delivery mechanism, from less than two hours of researcher attention. The developed agentic architecture is not specific to SIMS and can be applied to other analytical techniques, with primary target being the destructive measurements for which optimization methods are poorly applicable.

</details>

### [112] [Real-time topological rate at non-zero momentum in quenched QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12066)
*Claudio Bonanno,Massimo D'Elia,Roberto Dionisio,Giuseppe Gagliardi,Andrea Giorgieri,Francesco Sanfilippo,Alessandra Valentino,Giovanni Villadoro*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文在淬火格点QCD（quenched lattice QCD）中，于温度T≈1.24Tc≈360 MeV条件下，进行了非零动量下实时间拓扑率（real-time topological rate）的概念验证数值研究，为在完整QCD中确定该量迈出重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非零动量下的实时拓扑率是理解QCD拓扑性质的重要物理量，此前尚缺乏在淬火近似下的系统性研究；本文旨在通过概念验证计算，为未来在完整QCD中确定该量奠定基础。

Method: 研究采用逆问题求解策略，应用Hansen-Lupo-Tantalo（HLT）方法处理拓扑荷密度的热欧几里得时间关联函数以提取拓扑率；计算基于标准Wilson格点作用量，需要同时控制连续极限、HLT逆问题求解中湮灭宽度趋于零的极限以及拓扑荷密度关联函数计算中平滑半径趋于零的极限。

Result: 采用三个规范系综，时间方向格点数最高达Nτ=16以实现受控的连续极限；所有情况均采用纵横比LT=4，使得能够计算动量高达p/T~10的拓扑率。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究表明，所采用的HLT方法与逆问题求解策略在淬火近似下可行，并可覆盖较大动量范围，为后续在完整QCD中进行非零动量拓扑速率的计算提供了方法验证与数值框架。

Abstract: We present a proof-of-concept numerical study of the real-time topological rate at non-zero momentum in quenched lattice QCD at a temperature $T\simeq 1.24 \, T_c \simeq 360$ MeV, as an important step toward the determination of this quantity in full QCD. Our strategy, already applied to compute the sphaleron rate in pure Yang--Mills and in full QCD, extracts the rate from the resolution of an appropriate inverse problem, solved applying the Hansen--Lupo--Tantalo (HLT) method to the thermal Euclidean time-correlator of the topological charge density. This method requires to control three different limits: continuum limit, limit of vanishing smearing width used in the HLT inverse problem resolution, and limit of vanishing smoothing radius used in the topological charge density correlator computation. Our lattice calculation is based on the standard Wilson discretization for the gauge action, and on three gauge ensembles with up to $N_τ=16$ temporal points to achieve a controlled continuum limit. In all cases we employed an aspect ratio $LT=4$, which allowed us to compute the topological rate up to momenta as large as $p/T \sim 10$.

</details>

### [113] [New Families and Operations on Integrable Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12006)
*Matthew Visomirski,Christopher Griffin*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了由权图结构生成的Lotka-Volterra（复制子）系统的可积性，定义了新的辐条图族并证明其动力学可积，同时提出新的反克隆算子并显示其作用于平衡锦标赛图时生成可积动力学，而作用于其他图族时可能产生混沌行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Visomirski和Griffin（2025）及Evripidou等人（2022）的研究工作，旨在系统分类由有向图生成的可积Lotka-Volterra动力学，并探索新的图结构家族与算子以扩展可积系统的谱系。

Method: 定义了新的图族——辐条图（spoked graphs），证明其生成的全部动力学均具有可积性；引入新的反克隆算子（anti-cloning operator），并将其作用于具有奇数顶点的平衡锦标赛图（balanced tournament graphs）以生成新的可积动力学图；采用数值方法验证该算子在其他图族（如有向环）上的行为。

Result: 证明了辐条图族生成的所有动力学都是可积的；反克隆算子作用于平衡锦标赛图（奇数顶点数）时生成新图且其动力学可积；数值证据表明该算子作用于其他图族（如产生经典可积Volterra格子的有向环）时会导致混沌行为。

Conclusion: 本文完成了由最多六个顶点的有向图生成的所有可积动力学的分类体系（由Visomirski和Griffin于2025年启动），并提出了若干关于该主题的未来研究方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the integrability of Lotka-Volterra (replicator) systems arising from interaction matrices generated from corresponding graph structures, continuing work started by Visomirski and Griffin [J. Phys. A., 58:015701, 2025] and Evripidou et al. [J. Phys. A., 55:325201, 2022] (among others). In particular, we define a new family of graphs, the spoked graphs, and show that all dynamics generated from this family are integrable. In reference to Evripidou et al. (2022), we define a new anti-cloning operator and show that its action on balanced tournament graphs (with odd vertex count) generates new graphs whose dynamics are integrable. Interestingly, we provide numerical evidence that this anti-cloning operation leads to chaotic behaviour when applied to other graph families (e.g., the directed cycles that generate the classically integrable Volterra lattice). This work completes a taxonomy of all integrable dynamics generated by directed graphs with up to six vertices started by Visomirski and Griffin (2025), and suggests several future directions of study on this topic.

</details>

### [114] [Quantum mixing for eigenfunctions of rational polygons in configuration space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11728)
*Kai Hippi,Søren Mikkelsen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文在Marklof-Rudnick关于有理多边形几乎全部特征函数的位置可观测量等分布结果基础上，利用几乎所有方向的双向台球流（directional billiard flow）弱混合性质，将该结果推广到不包括环面（torus）的一类有理多边形的非对角（off-diagonal）元素；同时给出另一证明以确认二维环面（2-tori）上的相同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shnirelman-Zelditch-Colin de Verdière定理及其弱混合推广将量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）和量子混合与测地流（geodesic flow）的遍历性及弱混合联系起来，但可积系统（如平坦环面）通常不满足这些条件。Marklof和Rudnick已针对有理多边形建立了几乎全部特征函数对位置依赖可观测量（position-dependent observables）的等分布（equidistribution），本文旨在将其推广至非对角元素情形。

Method: 利用几乎所有方向的定向台球流（directional billiard flow）的弱混合（weak mixing）性质，将Marklof-Rudnick的等分布结果推广到某类有理多边形（不包括环面）的非对角元素；此外，针对二维环面给出另一独立的证明路径。

Result: 对某类有理多边形（环面除外），非对角元素上的等分布结果得以建立；同时，对二维环面通过另一种证明方法获得相同结论。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了有理多边形系统量子特征函数的等分布结果，表明在适当的动力学假设（弱混合的定向台球流）下，量子混合性质可类比于经典系统在特定可积片段上仍能成立，并补充了二维环面情形的独立证明。

Abstract: The Shnirelman-Zelditch-Colin de Verdière theorem and its weak mixing extension relate quantum ergodicity and quantum mixing with ergodicity and weak mixing of the geodesic flow. Integrable systems, such as the flat torus, do not satisfy either in general. Restricting to position-dependent observables, Marklof and Rudnick established equidistribution for almost all eigenfunctions of rational polygons. In this note, we extend this result to off-diagonal elements for a subset of rational polygons not including the torus using weak mixing of the directional billiard flow for almost all directions. Additionally, we provide a different proof that establishes the same result for $2$-tori.

</details>
