### [115] [Anomalies in Hadronic $B$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11298)
*Marianne Bouchard,David London*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在味SU(3)对称性（flavour SU(3) symmetry, SU(3)F）假设下研究B介子衰变到两个赝标量介子（B→PP）的过程。全局拟合结果显示与标准模型（Standard Model, SM）存在3.6σ偏差；扩展至包含η和η'介子的末态后，偏差增大至4.1σ，且在施加理论约束后接近5σ，暗示可能存在新物理（new physics）贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B→PP衰变（其中P为π或K）是检验标准模型和探索新物理的重要探针。此前在SU(3)F对称性假设下的拟合已出现与标准模型的显著偏差，但该研究中ΔS=0与ΔS=1衰变分别拟合得到的参数相差一个数量级，暗示SU(3)F破坏高达1000%，远超标准模型预期的约30%。为全面检验这一异常，需要将研究扩展至包含η和η'介子的末态，以更严格地测试SU(3)F对称性假设和新物理的可能性。

Method: 在SU(3)F对称性假设下，对B→PP衰变（P为π、K、η、η'）进行全局拟合分析；将ΔS=0与ΔS=1衰变分别拟合以检验SU(3)F对称性破坏的程度；通过施加理论约束条件（如|C̃/T̃|=0.2或Ã=0）进一步检验拟合的稳定性。

Result: 仅包含π和K末态的全局拟合显示与标准模型存在3.6σ偏差；ΔS=0与ΔS=1分别拟合得到的参数相差10倍，表明SU(3)F破坏约为1000%，远超标准模型预期的约30%。扩展至包含η和η'介子后，全局拟合质量变差，偏差增大至4.1σ；施加理论约束|C̃/T̃|=0.2或Ã=0后偏差进一步增大，接近5σ。

Conclusion: 所有这些结果均指向这些B→PP衰变中可能存在超出标准模型的新物理贡献，SU(3)F对称性假设下的标准模型无法充分解释观测数据。

Abstract: The decays $B\to PP$, where the pseudoscalar $P$ is a $π$ or $K$, have been studied under the assumption of flavour SU(3) symmetry [SU(3)$_F$]. The global fit shows a 3.6$σ$ discrepancy with the Standard Model (SM). Separate fits for $ΔS = 0$ and $ΔS = 1$ decays find parameter sets that differ by a factor of 10, suggesting 1000% SU(3)$_F$ breaking, significantly larger than the $\sim$ 30% breaking expected in the SM. This study has been extended to include final states with $η$ and $η'$ mesons. The resulting global fit, once again under the assumption of SU(3)$_F$ symmetry, is worse, with a 4.1$σ$ deviation from the SM. When theoretical constraints $|\widetilde C/\widetilde T| = 0.2$ or $\widetilde A = 0$ are imposed, the fits worsen, with the discrepancy approaching 5$σ$. These results hint at new-physics contributions to these decays.

</details>


### [116] [Physics of the Electron-Ion Collider in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11712)
*Bo-Wen Xiao,Yuxiang Zhao,Jian Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了中国电子-离子对撞机（EicC）的物理潜力，这是一台在建的尖端设施，通过高强偏振电子与离子（偏振质子、偏振氘核、偏振³He及非偏振重离子）在质心能量15-20 GeV、亮度(2-4)×10³³cm⁻²s⁻¹条件下对撞，旨在揭示核子与原子核的内部结构。论文重点回顾了核子质量分解、核子能量-能量关联、量子信息以及人工智能在核物理中的应用等新兴方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EicC作为在建的前沿设施，其核心物理学目标涵盖核子自旋与动量结构的三维层析成像、核子质量的起源、核子环境中的部分子结构以及奇异强子态的探索。由于传统三维核子结构课题已有大量综述，本文旨在突出EicC在新兴快速发展领域的具体物理潜力，为社区提供更新视角。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统梳理EicC的物理能力，结合其独特的运动学覆盖范围与高亮度优势，重点介绍核子质量分解、核子能量-能量关联、量子信息、人工智能在高能核物理中的应用等近期的、快速发展的研究方向及其在EicC上的可能实现。

Result: 综述表明EicC凭借其专用运动学覆盖和高亮度特性，在推进精密核子结构研究方面具备独特能力；同时论文专门辟出大量篇幅讨论了核子质量分解、核子能量-能量关联、量子信息和人工智能应用等新兴课题的近期进展，这些方向已引起学界极大关注。

Conclusion: EicC有望在多方面推动核子结构精密研究的进步，且其物理目标已超越传统三维核子结构框架，拓展至质量起源、量子信息与人工智能等前沿交叉方向，展现出广泛而独特的科学潜力。

Abstract: The Electron-Ion Collider in China (EicC), a cutting-edge facility under development, aims to unveil the internal structure of nucleons and nuclei by leveraging collisions of high-intensity polarized electrons and ions (polarized protons, polarized deuterons, polarized $^{3}$He, and unpolarized heavy ions up to Uranium) at center-of-mass energies of 15-20 GeV and luminosity of (2-4)$\times 10^{33}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. Its primary physics objectives include 3D tomography of nucleon spin and momentum structure, fundamental questions regarding the origin of nucleon mass, partonic structure of nuclei and parton interactions with the nuclear environment, and exploration of exotic hadronic states. In this paper, we review the physics potential of the EicC and highlight its unique capabilities for advancing precision nucleon structure studies by combining its specialized kinematic coverage and high luminosity. Since traditional topics like 3D nucleon structure have already been well-covered by several extensive reviews, we have deliberately dedicated significant space to recent progress in nucleon mass decomposition, nucleon energy-energy correlation, quantum information, and artificial intelligence applications in high-energy nuclear physics, which have been emerging rapidly and attracted a tremendous amount of attention in the community.

</details>


### [117] [Effective Lagrangians from functional matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11306)
*Stefan Dittmaier,Sebastian Schuhmacher,Maximilian Stahlhofen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文简述一种功能匹配（functional matching）的变体方法，用于在自上而下（top-down）路径下推导包含重粒子的有效场论（Effective Field Theory, EFT）单圈配位数。该方法在去除质量混合效应后，通过背景场方法分离树图和圈图贡献，借助区域展开（expansion by regions）分离硬模与软模，并以标准模型加上单重态希格斯粒子（Higgs Singlet Extension）为例进行演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高能物理中，从完整理论推导包含重自由度的有效场论是连接微观与低能理论的关键步骤。作者希望提出一种系统且自洽的单圈级匹配方法，能够在不自设EFT类型（SMEFT或HEFT）的前提下完成推导，并阐明最终所得理论的结构特征。

Method: 方法采用自上而下视角，首先将重场（对应质量本征态）积分掉，即先通过对角化质量矩阵消除混合效应；随后使用背景场方法（background-field method）将树图与圈图贡献分离，并利用区域展开法区分硬（hard）与软（soft）动量模式。方法以标准模型的希格斯单重态扩展为例加以验证，其中额外希格斯玻色子质量M_H取大值，混合角α按1/M_H标度以保证大M_H极限下的退耦（decoupling）行为。

Result: 所得有效场论对EFT类型（SMEFT或HEFT）不预设承诺（agnostic）；虽可在后续将其拉氏量转化为SMEFT形式，但这一转化需要引入费米型EFT算符，而这些算符并非直接由对重希格斯场求解泛函积分时生成。

Conclusion: 该功能匹配变体方法能够系统、单圈级地推导含重粒子的EFT，但需注意最终转化到SMEFT可能引入额外费米型算符；这一发现提示了直接从泛函积分路径得到的结果在SMEFT与HEFT之间可能存在的结构差异。

Abstract: We briefly review a variant of functional matching to derive an Effective Field Theory (EFT) for heavy particles at the one-loop level in the top-down approach. The method integrates out heavy fields that correspond to mass eigenstates, i.e. after removing mixing effects by diagonalizing mass matrices. Tree- and loop-level effects are separated by employing the background-field method, hard and soft modes are separated with the use of the expansion by regions. The method is exemplified for the Higgs Singlet Extension of the Standard Model where the mass $M_\mathrm{H}$ of the additional Higgs boson is considered large, and the Higgs mixing angle $α$ is assumed to scale like $1/M_\mathrm{H}$, in order to guarantee decoupling in the large-$M_\mathrm{H}$ limit. Our calculation is agnostic w.r.t. the type (SMEFT vs. HEFT) of the emerging EFT. Eventually the emerging EFT Lagrangian can be transformed into SMEFT form, but only at the cost of introducing fermionic EFT operators, although no such operators are directly generated upon solving the functional integral over the heavy Higgs field.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Logarithmic intermittency of the critical 2D SHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12270)
*Shirshendu Ganguly,Kyeongsik Nam*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维临界随机热流（stochastic heat flow, SHF）支撑集的尖锐奇异性和间歇性（intermittency）量化结果，精确刻画了其对数分形行为：几乎所有质量都集中在极少数半径为ε的小球上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维随机热流与1+1维的随机热方程不同，存在弱到强的无序转变（weak-to-strong disorder transition），并预期表现出丰富的间歇性。已有工作建立了SHF几乎必然关于勒贝格测度奇异，但对支撑集的奇异程度缺乏精确量化。作者旨在给出支撑集奇异性和间歇性的精确结果。

Method: 证明的关键工具是精细的大偏差理论（large-deviations theory），该理论通过利用SHF拟临界尺度下的类高斯行为来访问条件分布。另一关键观察是：将布朗运动条件化在其端点异常大时，本质上会使增量的均值发生平移，而在足够小的尺度下其分布变化不显著。

Result: 作者证明，几乎必然地，对所有充分小的ε>0，在允许一个趋于零的误差下，任意区域中点到平面SHF的全部质量集中在约1/(ε²log^{1/2+o(1)}(1/ε))个半径为ε的小球上，每个小球含有约ε²log^{1/2+o(1)}(1/ε)的质量，从而精确确立了其对数分形行为（logarithmic fractal behavior）。

Conclusion: 该结果令人信服地刻画了SHF的支撑集奇异性和间歇性，精确量化了其对数分形结构，为理解二维临界随机热流的精细统计性质提供了新的理论工具和深刻见解。

Abstract: While the solution to the $1+1$ dimensional stochastic heat equation with multiplicative noise is closely related to the exponential of a Brownian motion, the two-dimensional picture exhibits an additional weak-to-strong disorder transition. In [CSZ '23], the critical two-dimensional stochastic heat flow (SHF) was constructed as the scaling limit of the partition function of $2+1$ dimensional directed polymers under the logarithmic intermediate-disorder scaling at criticality. The SHF is a random measure and, like many naturally occurring random measures, it is expected to exhibit rich intermittency. [CSZ '25] established that it is almost surely singular with respect to the Lebesgue measure. More recently, [GT '26] showed that the logarithm of the SHF averaged over small balls is asymptotically Gaussian, with both its mean and variance diverging as the ball radius tends to zero. In this paper we prove a sharp result quantifying the singularity of the support of the SHF as well as its intermittency. In particular, we show that, almost surely, for all small $\varepsilon>0$, up to a vanishing error, all the mass of the point-to-plane SHF in any domain is concentrated on ${1}/{\big(\varepsilon^2\log^{1/2+o(1)}(1/\varepsilon)\big)}$ balls of radius $\varepsilon$, each containing $\varepsilon^2{\log^{1/2+o(1)}(1/\varepsilon)}$ mass, thus precisely establishing its logarithmic fractal behavior. A key ingredient in the proof is a refined large-deviations theory, which allows access to conditional distributions, by taking advantage of the Gaussian-like behavior of the SHF at quasi-critical scales. A further useful observation that features prominently is that conditioning a Brownian motion on its endpoint being unusually large essentially induces a shift in the mean of its increments, and consequently, at small enough scales, their distributions do not alter significantly.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将统计物理学（statistical physics）的观察转化为方法：用由少量廉价查询拟合出的低参数模型替代每个大语言模型（LLM）智能体，从而在笔记本电脑上以任意规模N运行社会仿真，并通过一个[交互阶数×记忆]分类学预测替代误差的N趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多LLM智能体的社会仿真相较昂贵，而这类仿真通常关注的是宏观问题（如相行为、风格化事实、随智能体数量N的标度），而非单个智能体的认知细节。因此需要一种低成本但又保真宏观行为的方法。

Method: 将每个LLM智能体替换为从数百到数千次廉价查询中拟合出的低参数模型，然后在任意N下于笔记本电脑上运行整个社会仿真。作者引入一个[交互阶数×记忆]分类学（interaction order × memory taxonomy），将感知与记忆映射到有效理论（effective theory），并预测替代误差的N趋势。

Result: 在忠实重实现的LLM宏观经济仿真EconAgent以及其他七个具名LLM仿真上验证了该方法，智能体决策由真实LLM（主要为DeepSeek）的elicitation克隆而来，成本仅需数美元。预测的误差趋势逐格成立；两个被反驳的预测均发生在强饱和响应上，且归因于其曲率，这些偏差本身也被无自由参数的理论定量匹配。

Conclusion: 仿真是否有效在运行前即可判定，主要取决于每个智能体的感知特征。该方法在低成本条件下成功预测了替代误差的宏观趋势，并能在理论框架内定量解释例外情况。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [120] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以持久记忆（persistent memory）为核心的自进化记忆框架（self-evolving memory framework），将材料科学研究经验存储为可检视的事实（facts）和可执行的技能（skills），供人工智能智能体跨模型检索、修订和迁移。在49个真实材料工具使用问题和138个可执行子任务中，记忆机制在无需更新模型参数的情况下将GPT-5.2的任务成功率提升近一倍；同时在状态方程计算和13个实际材料仿真工作流中显著减少了错误和资源消耗，表明智能体记忆可作为超越单一模型的持久科学资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究的进展依赖于积累的经验（如可用的脚本、可信的协议、失败计算的警告以及将新问题与旧结果关联的判断），这些经验对可复现性和知识传递至关重要，但通常分散在笔记本、仓库、作业日志和个人记忆中，且难以在人工智能智能体之间移植。本文旨在解决这一碎片化问题，提出以持久记忆而非特定智能体实现为中心来设计材料科学的终身AI合作伙伴。

Method: 提出一个自进化的记忆框架，将科学经验结构化为可检视的事实和可执行的技能两类模块，使观察结果、失败边界、协议和验证检查能够被检索、修订并在不同模型间迁移。框架在三个计算场景中评估：真实材料工具使用问答（49个问题、138个可执行子任务）、元素固体状态方程计算、以及13个实际材料仿真工作流。

Result: 在49个真实材料工具使用问题中，记忆机制使GPT-5.2任务成功率近乎翻倍且无需模型参数更新。在元素固体状态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前护栏（guardrail），使Correct/Partial/Error结果从22/1/4改善为25/2/0，并避免了92%的重复错误。在13个实用材料仿真工作流中，记忆技能和失败事实使聚合轨迹负担（token数）减半，第三轮起工具调用减少超过一半，同时保持了带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析中物理上有意义的输出。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆可以作为持久的科学资产（durable scientific asset），形成一份便携的、自改进的材料研究经验记录，其有效期超越任何单一模型或智能体堆栈。这表明围绕持久记忆而非特定智能体实现来设计材料科学AI伴侣是可行且有效的方向。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Aggregation-engineered loss-tolerant strong coupling in metallic microcavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11778)
*Andrea Betti,Eleonora Cara,Giulia Serrano,Lorenzo Poggini,Alessia Valzelli,Natascia De Leo,Paolo Bartolini,Andrea Taschin,Renato Torre,Alice Boschetti*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究通过将分子聚集（molecular aggregation）作为设计参数而非寄生效应，在低品质因子的银法布里-珀罗微腔中实现了溶液加工的罗丹明6G-聚乙烯醇薄膜的室温强耦合（room-temperature strong coupling），耦合能量高达324 meV，并阐明基态聚集体与激发态类激基缔合物在极化激元形成与发射中的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机微腔中的室温强耦合通常需要高品质光学谐振腔与高度有序的激子介质相结合，这一要求限制了材料的可扩展性和加工灵活性。作者试图通过将分子聚集作为可控的设计变量，放宽这一约束条件。

Method: 采用溶液加工的罗丹明6G-聚乙烯醇（Rhodamine 6G-poly(vinyl alcohol)）薄膜，嵌入低品质因子银法布里-珀罗微腔中，通过角度分辨测量呈现反交叉行为；并利用双激子耦合振子模型（two-exciton coupled-oscillator model）分析两种激子物种的相对权重对集体耦合强度的影响，调节染料负载和旋涂条件进行调控。

Result: 实验实现了高达324 meV的耦合能量，尽管金属镜面存在较大光学损耗；角度分辨光致发光主要由红移的宽下极化激子发射主导，与致密堆积分子域中形成的类激基缔合物态的弛豫一致。

Conclusion: 分子聚集可作为一种实用的设计杠杆，用于湿法工艺金属腔中的低损耗容限强耦合；基态聚集体和激发态类激基缔合物种在极化激元形成和发射过程中扮演不同的角色。

Abstract: Room-temperature strong coupling in organic microcavities is usually achieved by combining high-quality optical resonators with highly ordered excitonic media, a requirement that limits scalability and processing flexibility. Here we show that this constraint can be relaxed by using molecular aggregation as a design parameter rather than treating it as a parasitic effect. We realize solution-processed Rhodamine 6G-poly(vinyl alcohol) films embedded in low-quality-factor silver Fabry-Perot microcavities and demonstrate clear angle-resolved anticrossing with coupling energies up to 324 meV despite the large optical losses of the metallic mirrors. A two-exciton coupled-oscillator model shows that the relative weight of these species controls the collective coupling strength and can be tuned through dye loading and spin-coating conditions. In contrast, angle-resolved photoluminescence is dominated by a broad, red-shifted lower-polariton emission, consistent with relaxation through excimer-like states formed in densely packed molecular domains. These results identify molecular aggregation as a practical design lever for loss-tolerant strong coupling in wet-processed metallic cavities and suggest that ground-state aggregates and excited-state excimer-like species play distinct roles in polariton formation and emission.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Large bias-tunable magnetoresistance from spin-dependent interlayer hybridization in van der Waals antiferromagnet CrSBr-based heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11389)
*Sadeed Hameed,Aditya Kumar,Chengjie Yu,Aravind P. Balan,Xinran Wang,Lichuan Zhang,Yuriy Mokrousov,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了hBN/少层石墨烯/CrSBr/少层石墨烯异质结构中的大幅磁电阻（magnetoresistance, MR）效应，通过偏压和温度调控实现了20K下高达350%的磁电阻，并揭示了其机制源于磁构型依赖的势垒带边（band edge），而非电极自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CrSBr基异质结中大幅磁电阻的物理机制，特别是揭示磁电阻对偏压的非单调依赖关系背后的根源。

Method: 以偏置电压和温度为独立调控参数，测量磁电阻随偏压和温度的变化；通过硬轴（hard-axis）磁场连续调控磁化角度θ，系统分析势垒带边偏移与cos(θ/2)的线性关系以验证自旋依赖层间杂化（spin-dependent interlayer hybridization）假设。

Result: 在20K下实现高达350%的磁电阻，表现为±0.5V附近对称的M形极大值；磁化角θ连续调控显示势垒带边偏移与cos(θ/2)呈线性关系，排除Jullière模型和自旋过滤投影机制。

Conclusion: CrSBr结中的大幅磁电阻由磁构型依赖的带边决定，而非电极自旋极化主导，为一阶自旋依赖层间杂化效应。

Abstract: We explore the large magnetoresistance (MR) in \ce{hBN}/few-layer-graphene/\ce{CrSBr}/few-layer-graphene heterostructures and reveal the mechanism behind its non-monotonic bias dependence. Using bias voltage and temperature as independent tuning knobs, we achieve MR up to \SI{350}{\percent} at \SI{20}{K}, characterized by symmetric M-shaped maxima around $\pm 0.5\,\mathrm{V}$. Continuous tuning of the magnetization angle $θ$ via a hard-axis magnetic field shows that the barrier band-edge offset varies linearly with $\cos(θ/2)$, a first-order signature of spin-dependent interlayer hybridization. This linear relationship rules out the Jullière model and a spin-filter projection. We conclude that the magnetic-configuration-dependent band edge, rather than electrode spin polarization, dictates the large magnetoresistance in \ce{CrSBr} junctions.

</details>


### [123] [Layer-Number-Controlled Symmetry Breaking and Surface-State Transport in Rhombohedral Graphene Multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11450)
*Bosai Lyu,Jian Zheng,Kai Liu,Yulu Ren,Size Wu,Yating Sha,Shuhan Liu,Youngju Park,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jinfeng Jia,Zhiwen Shi,Jeil Jung,Weidong Luo,Guorui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究制备了菱面体多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene），通过系统电输运测量揭示了层数对相变和表面屏蔽效应的非常规依赖关系：层反铁磁（LAF）到半金属转变的临界位移场（D_c）在四层至六层中保持不变，而半金属到层极化绝缘体（LPI）的转变D_c随层数增加而增大；六层中观察到表面态主导的输运及强层间屏蔽效应，磁场下呈现谷-层锁定的朗道能级（Landau levels）和耗散态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体多层石墨烯承载层极化平带（layer-polarized flat bands），是强关联和拓扑电子态的重要平台，但层数如何调控对称性破缺和表面屏蔽（surface screening）此前尚不清楚，因此有必要系统研究层数依赖的电输运行为。

Method: 制备菱面体石墨烯多层样品，对四层至六层体系开展系统的电输运（electrical transport）测量，并施加位移场（displacement field）和高磁场条件，结合门电压（gate voltage）分别调控来探测不同层数下的相变边界、朗道能级和表面态输运特征。

Result: 发现LPI转变的位移场临界值不遵循未屏蔽库仑相互作用模型的预期，而是随层数增加而增大；在六层样品中出现表面态主导的输运，朗道能级和电阻峰可被相邻门单独控制；高磁场下观测到谷-层锁定（valley-layer-locked）的朗道能级以及可能源于层间背散射（interlayer backscattering）的耗散态。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了层数（layer number）是调控菱面体石墨烯多层中关联相与拓扑相的重要实验旋钮，并揭示了强层间屏蔽（interlayer screening）在较厚堆叠中的关键作用。

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene hosts layer-polarized flat bands, providing an intriguing platform for correlated and topological electronic states; however, the role of layer number in governing symmetry breaking and surface screening remains elusive. Here we prepare rhombohedral graphene multilayers and systematically conduct electrical transport measurements. We uncover an unconventional layer dependence of phase transitions: the critical displacement field (D$_{c}$) for the layer-antiferromagnetic (LAF)-to-semimetal transitions remains constant across tetralayer to hexalayer graphene, whereas the D$_{c}$ for semimetal-to-layer-polarized-insulator (LPI) transition increases with layer number, defying unscreened Coulomb interaction models. In hexalayer graphene, surface-state-dominated transport emerges, with Landau levels (LLs) and resistive peaks selectively controlled by adjacent gates, a signature of strong interlayer screening absent in thinner stacks. High magnetic fields reveal valley-layer-locked LLs and dissipative states possibly from interlayer backscattering, highlighting the presence of decoupled surface states. Our findings establish layer number as a key tuning knob for engineering correlated and topological phases in rhombohedral graphene multilayers.

</details>


### [124] [Dynamics of the spontaneous emission factor in multiple quantum well nanowire lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11523)
*Parya Reyhanian,Christopher G Poulton,Arti Agrawal,Charlene J. Lobo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了多量子阱（multiple quantum well, MQW）纳米线激光器中自发发射因子（即 η 因子）的动态特性，展示了如何通过半导体量子阱中的价带-导带跃迁及腔模来直接计算 η 因子，而非将其视为常数参数，并考察了电荷载流子密度、量子阱厚度和组分对自发发射速率及 η 因子的影响及其对激光阈值和运行的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发发射因子（spontaneous emission factor，η 因子）是描述量子阱激光器的关键物理量，直接影响阈值功率和光输入-光输出（L-L）曲线的形状。然而过往对多量子阱纳米线激光器的建模通常将 η 因子视为一个可事后估计或拟合的常数参数，缺乏对其中物理机制的系统推导，因此需要对其动态本质开展深入研究。

Method: 作者从半导体量子阱的价带与导带之间的跃迁出发，结合腔模（cavity modes）信息对 η 因子进行理论推导与计算，而非将其视作常数进行后验拟合。在此基础上，系统考察了 η 因子及自发发射速率对电荷载流子密度（charge carrier density）、量子阱厚度（quantum well thickness）和组分（composition）的依赖关系。

Result: 研究揭示了 η 因子在多量子阱纳米线激光器中并非恒定常数，而是具有动态性质，并给出了其可计算的具体方法与途径。同时阐明了自发发射速率和 η 因子随电荷载流子密度、量子阱厚度和组分的变化规律及其对激光阈值和器件运行的潜在影响。

Conclusion: 多量子阱纳米线激光器的 η 因子应作为一个依赖物理参数的动态量而非常数来处理，其可由量子阱能带跃迁与腔模信息直接计算，相关结果对理解并优化激光阈值与器件运行特性具有指导意义。

Abstract: The spontaneous emission factor - often known as the \b{eta} factor - is an important quantity in the description of quantum well lasers, influencing both the threshold power as well as the general shape of the light in-light out (L-L) curve. Past work on modelling multiple quantum well (MQW) nanowire laser devices has typically assumed that the \b{eta} factor is a constant parameter that can either be estimated or fit in a post-hoc manner. However, the \b{eta} factor can be derived from the transitions between valence and conduction bands in semiconductor quantum wells, together with knowledge of the cavity modes. Here we investigate the dynamic nature of the \b{eta} factor for MQW nanowire lasers, and show how it can be computed. We also examine the dependence of the spontaneous emission rate and spontaneous emission factor \b{eta} on the charge carrier density, quantum well thickness, and composition, and discuss the impact on laser threshold and operation.

</details>


### [125] [Spatially Resolving the Pre-Thermal Anatomy of a Driven Bosonic Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11561)
*Shantam Ravan,Aaron Müller,Johannes Cremer,Jonathan Curtis,Daniel Fernandez,Ronald Walsworth,Eugene Demler,Amir Yacoby*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用氮空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）磁测量技术对钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）薄膜中室温下的磁子（magnon）动力学进行微米尺度空间成像，解析了从单色注入磁子向热平衡演化的多级参数散射级联过程，并定量提取了四磁子相互作用耦合强度、级联阶数及非线性系数，将空间分辨磁子学确立为可视化非平衡多体动力学的强大平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相干驱动量子多体系统在热平衡之前如何重新分配能量，是多体物理学中的一个核心挑战。

Method: 采用NV磁测量技术对YIG薄膜中的室温磁子动力学进行微米尺度空间成像；通过双音混频（two-tone wave-mixing）协议分离基本四磁子相互作用，并通过散射产物的空间增长提取耦合强度；随后以强单频激发驱动系统接近铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance），观察自发多代散射级联。数据分析依托近场磁子学（near-field magnonics）框架，从功率依赖频移中提取级联阶数和非线性系数。

Result: 研究揭示了一个离散参数散射事件的分级结构，这些事件充当通往热平衡的确定性垫脚石；每一代中占主导的散射通道均对应一个散射磁子处于慢磁子区，类似于慢光系统中光学非线性的增强效应。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了单色注入磁子向平衡态演化的多阶段动力学过程，将空间分辨磁子学确立为可视化非平衡多体动力学的强大平台。

Abstract: Understanding how coherently driven quantum many-body systems redistribute energy prior to thermal equilibrium remains a central challenge in many-body physics. Here, we utilize nitrogen-vacancy (NV) magnetometry to perform micron-scale spatial imaging of room-temperature magnon dynamics in a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) thin film. We resolve a hierarchy of discrete parametric scattering events that serve as deterministic stepping stones toward thermalization. By applying a two-tone wave-mixing protocol, we first isolate the elementary four-magnon interaction and extract its coupling strength via the spatial growth of the scattering product. We then drive the system with an intense single-frequency excitation near ferromagnetic resonance, revealing that magnon-magnon interactions trigger a spontaneous, multi-generation scattering cascade. We demonstrate that in each generation, the dominant scattering channels correspond to one of the out-scattered magnons being in the slow magnon regime, reminiscent of the enhancement of optical nonlinearities in slow light systems. We capture this dynamics quantitatively using a near field magnonics framework and extract the cascade order and nonlinear coefficients directly from power-dependent frequency shifts. By revealing the multi-stage dynamical process through which monochromatic injected magnons evolve toward equilibrium, our work establishes spatially resolved magnonics as a powerful platform for visualizing non-equilibrium many-body kinetics.

</details>


### [126] [Realization of Arbitrary Gauge Fields via Symmetry-Protected Zero Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11609)
*J. X. Dai,Bingbing Wang,Jiangzi Chen,Y. X. Zhao,Haoran Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用方案，利用子格不平衡二部单元（sublattice-imbalanced bipartite units）的对称性保护零模（symmetry-protected zero modes），在人工系统中实现任意静态 O(N) 晶格规范场构型。靶规范哈密顿量作为微观紧束缚模型的精确谱块而非微扰近似，并在声学晶体中通过 Z₂ 四极拓扑绝缘体、SO(2) Hofstadter 模型和 SO(3) 非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范场（gauge field）是现代物理学的基础，但在人工系统中，预先设定的规范构型往往难以实现。因此，亟需一种通用且可行的方法来构造任意的 O(N) 晶格规范构型。

Method: 利用子格不平衡二部结构的对称性保护零模，将每个键上的靶 O(N) 链编码在正微观耦合的连通性和强度中。通过将零模流形与其余模式解耦，靶规范哈密顿量以精确谱块（exact spectral block）形式嵌入微观紧束缚模型，而非依赖微扰近似。

Result: 在声学晶体中分别实现了 Z₂ 四极拓扑绝缘体（quadrupole topological insulator）、SO(2) Hofstadter 模型以及 SO(3) 非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体（non-Abelian topological insulator），验证了该框架的普遍适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为人工系统中规范场物理提供了一条通用且易于实现的路径，可广泛应用于各类人工平台中的规范场模拟。

Abstract: Gauge fields are fundamental to modern physics, but prescribed gauge configurations are often difficult to implement in artificial systems. Here, we present a general scheme for realizing arbitrary static $\mathrm{O}(N)$ lattice gauge configurations using symmetry-protected zero modes of sublattice-imbalanced bipartite units. The target $\mathrm{O}(N)$ link on each bond is encoded in the connectivity and strengths of positive microscopic couplings. By decoupling the zero-mode manifold from the remaining modes, the target gauge Hamiltonian forms an exact spectral block of the microscopic tight-binding model rather than a perturbative approximation. We experimentally demonstrate this framework in acoustic crystals through a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quadrupole topological insulator, an $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ Hofstadter model, and an $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ non-Abelian topological insulator. Our results provide a general and accessible route to gauge-field physics in artificial systems.

</details>


### [127] [Wormhole Geometry from a Magnetic Vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12285)
*Dušan Đorđ ević,Fabián Molina,Vladimir Juričić*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明了磁性涡旋（magnetic vortex）中电子的强耦合行为可实现Ellis虫洞（Ellis wormhole）的外域时空几何，并给出了电子偏转塌缩到单一Ellis曲线以及自旋Berry相产生半通量Aharonov–Bohm响应的两个可分离特征，为量子材料中探测涌现几何与Berry通量提供了实验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强耦合到磁性纹理（magnetic texture）的电子是否能在涌现的弯曲空间（emergent curved space）中传播，以及这种涌现几何能否通过具体拓扑缺陷实现并产生可观测的物理信号。

Method: 通过分析磁性涡旋中电子与磁纹理的强耦合，展示其对应Ellis虫洞的超静态喉口（ultrastatic throat）外域时空几何；并进一步在人为设计的蜂窝晶格（designer honeycomb lattice）中模拟相同的度量结构。

Result: 发现两个可分立的可观测特征：电子偏转角由涡旋绕数（winding）与交换耦合决定并塌缩到单一Ellis曲线；自旋Berry相产生由绕数奇偶性开关的半通量Aharonov–Bohm响应。

Conclusion: 磁性涡旋将拓扑缺陷转变为可调控的弯曲空间透镜，可通过实空间电子偏转与散射实验直接探测涌现几何和Berry通量，适用于量子材料及人为设计晶格系统。

Abstract: Strong coupling to a magnetic texture makes an electron propagate through an emergent curved space. We show that an elementary vortex realizes the exterior spatial geometry of an Ellis wormhole: an ultrastatic throat with radius fixed by the topological charge and Hund exchange, cut off at short distances by the microscopic core. Two separable signatures follow directly: the electron deflection collapses onto a single Ellis curve governed by the vortex winding and exchange coupling, while the spin Berry phase produces a half-flux Aharonov--Bohm response switched on and off by winding parity. The same metric can be emulated in a designer honeycomb lattice, where the valley-symmetrized wave-packet response follows the predicted exterior geodesic. These signatures are accessible through real-space electron deflection and scattering, providing experimentally distinct probes of the emergent geometry and Berry flux. The magnetic vortex thus turns a topological defect into a tunable curved-space lens for electrons in quantum materials and designer lattices.

</details>


### [128] [Second-Chern Bounds in Non-Abelian Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12221)
*Junwen Zhao,Zhiming Pan,Kang Yang,Congjun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维参数空间中双重简并能级的量子几何，推导出度规迹平方与行列式之间的不等式关系，并揭示了行列式不等式饱和时诱导出四元数凯勒（quaternion Kähler）结构，以四带Dirac哈密顿量为例验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在推广二维Chern绝缘体中理想能带条件所诱导的复结构思想，探索四维参数空间中双重简并能级的量子几何约束及其拓扑意义。

Method: 针对具有SU(2)规范结构的简并能级对，推导出量子几何不等式 (tr g)²/16 ≥ √(det g) ≥ |Tr(F∧F)|/12；通过分析不等式的饱和条件，建立与四元数凯勒结构的对应关系，并以四带Dirac哈密顿量为具体例子进行验证。

Result: 证明第一不等式刻画度规的各向异性；第二行列式不等式度量曲率在Hodge星运算下的自对偶性及三重SU(2)旋转下不闭合的能级间过程。行列式界饱和时四维参数空间诱导出四元数凯勒结构。四带Dirac哈密顿量自动饱和该界，并携带Tr(F∧F)的拓扑零点。

Conclusion: 四维参数空间中双简并能级对的量子几何受严格不等式约束，其饱和条件对应四元数凯勒结构，与二维Chern绝缘体中的复结构相类比；该结论为理解四维拓扑态的几何与拓扑性质提供了新的框架。

Abstract: We study the quantum geometry of doubly degenerate energy levels in a four-dimensional parameter space. For degenerate pairs with $SU(2)$ gauge structures, the quantum geometry obeys $\big(\textrm{tr } g\big)^2/16\geq\sqrt{\det g}\geq |\textrm{Tr}(F\wedge F)|/12$. The first inequality characterizes the anisotropy in the metric. The second determinant inequality measures the self-duality of the curvature under Hodge star operation and the inter-level processes that do not close under the three $SU(2)$ rotations of the doubly degenerate levels. The saturation of the determinant bound induces a quaternion Kähler structure on the four-dimensional parameter space, analogous to the complex structure induced by the ideal-band condition in two-dimensional Chern insulators. As examples, four-band Dirac Hamiltonians automatically saturate the determinant bound and possess a topological zero in $\textrm{Tr}(F\wedge F)$. We discuss the comparison to degenerate pairs with $U(2)$ gauge structures.

</details>


### [129] [Spin lifetime anisotropy in graphene induced by the SiO2 interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11615)
*Aron W. Cummings,Chunhao Guo,Andrew Grieder,Shihao Tu,Mayank Gupta,Junqing Xu,Juan Marmolejo-Tejada,Yuan Ping*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理密度矩阵动力学模拟和紧束缚（tight-binding, TB）输运模拟，系统性研究了SiO₂衬底如何改变石墨烯的自旋织构并主导自旋弛豫过程，发现二维SiO₂衬底诱导以Rashba型螺旋自旋织构为主、自旋寿命各向异性为1/2，而体相SiO₂破坏面内对称性导致各向异性的面内与面外自旋分量，输运模拟得到的自旋寿命各向异性为0.5–1，与实验测量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解常见介电衬底如何影响石墨烯的自旋输运性质，是推动基于石墨烯的自旋电子学技术发展的关键前提，需要揭示SiO₂衬底对石墨烯自旋织构和自旋弛豫的调控机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度矩阵动力学模拟，以及紧束缚（tight-binding, TB）输运模拟，定量刻画了电子-声子散射、杂质散射和静电无序对自旋弛豫过程的影响，并发展了一个新的TB模型来捕捉体相SiO₂衬底破坏石墨烯面内对称性所产生的各向异性自旋织构。

Result: 二维SiO₂衬底在石墨烯中诱导以Rashba型螺旋自旋织构为主的效应，产生1/2的自旋寿命各向异性；体相SiO₂则进一步破坏面内对称性，导致自旋织构在面内与面外分量上呈现各向异性；在真实无序条件下的输运模拟给出自旋寿命各向异性介于0.5到1之间，与SiO₂衬底上石墨烯自旋阀的测量结果吻合。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了石墨烯/SiO₂界面处比标准Rashba模型更复杂的自旋弛豫图景，为实验结果的解读和石墨烯自旋电子学中衬底工程的指导提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Understanding how common dielectric substrates influence the spin transport properties of graphene is essential for advancing graphene-based spintronic technologies. Here we use a comprehensive set of numerical simulations to reveal how a SiO$_2$ substrate modifies the spin texture and governs spin relaxation in graphene. Using first-principles density matrix dynamics simulations, as well as tight-binding (TB) transport simulations, we quantify the effects of electron-phonon scattering, impurity scattering, and electrostatic disorder on the spin relaxation process. We find that a 2D SiO$_2$ substrate induces a predominantly Rashba-type helical spin texture in graphene, leading to a spin lifetime anisotropy of 1/2. Meanwhile, bulk SiO$_2$ breaks in-plane symmetry in graphene, leading to anisotropic in-plane and out-of-plane components in the spin texture, which we capture with a newly-developed TB model of graphene. Transport simulations under realistic disorder conditions reveal a spin lifetime anisotropy between 0.5 and 1, similar to what is seen in measurements of graphene spin valves on a SiO$_2$ substrate. Our results reveal a more complex picture of spin relaxation at the ubiquitous graphene/SiO$_2$ interface, beyond the standard Rashba model, providing critical insight for interpreting experiments and guiding substrate engineering for graphene spintronics.

</details>


### [130] [Exact solution for stationary states of a closed memristor with mobile charged vacancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12264)
*Irina V. Boylo,Konstantin L. Metlov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文考虑移动带电空位之间的静电相互作用（electrostatic interaction），构建忆阻器（memristor）的非线性模型，并给出了电流作用下形成的稳态（极限）空位分布的解析表达式，发现"开/关"态极限电阻在空位浓度的一阶近似下不依赖静电相互作用的强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有忆阻器模型多忽略带电移动空位之间的静电相互作用，但该相互作用会显著影响器件中空位的稳态分布，进而影响忆阻器的工作特性，因此有必要在模型中系统地纳入这一效应。

Method: 建立基于带电移动空位（charged mobile vacancies）的非线性忆阻器模型，将空位间的静电相互作用纳入考量，并推导在流经忆阻器的电流作用下形成的稳态（极限）空位分布的解析表达式。

Result: 静电相互作用显著影响忆阻器中空位的稳态分布；在空位浓度降低与升高的区域之间，会因静电相互作用形成一个电中性（electrically neutral）的中间区域。此外，忆阻器"开/关"（on/off）状态的极限电阻至少在空位浓度的一阶项下不依赖于静电相互作用的强度。

Conclusion: 带电空位间的静电相互作用虽然改变了忆阻器内部的空位分布结构（引入中间电中性区域），但不会影响"开/关"态极限电阻（在空位浓度一阶近似下），这意味着静电相互作用对忆阻器宏观电阻特性的影响可能是高阶效应。

Abstract: A nonlinear model of a memristor based on charged mobile vacancies is considered taking into account the electrostatic interaction between them. This interaction significantly affects the stationary (limiting) vacancy distributions formed under the action of the electric current flowing through the memristor, for which analytical expressions are obtained in this work. Between the regions with reduced and increased vacancy concentrations, an intermediate electrically neutral region is formed due to the electrostatic interaction. Interestingly, the limiting resistances in the ``on'' and ``off'' states of such a memristor do not depend on the strength of the electrostatic interaction, at least in the leading first order in the vacancy concentration.

</details>


### [131] [Magnetic noise of a dark exciton Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11740)
*Pieter M. Gunnink*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用氮空位（NV）中心磁力测量技术检测三重态激子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）产生的杂散磁场噪声，从而为光学禁止的低重结合三重态激子BEC提供一种可行的实验验证途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）虽具前景，但因光学探测被禁止，其实验识别一直是难题，尤其是自旋S_z=±1的三重态激子BEC的观测信号长期缺失。

Method: 利用NV中心磁力计测量三重态激子BEC的磁性特性所产生的杂散磁场噪声；通过外加磁场将系统从反铁磁序调谐至铁磁序，使BEC的纵向自旋声学模式软化至GHz频率范围，匹配NV自旋弛豫速率以实现直接探测。

Result: 证明在距样品小距离d处存在非常规的紫外（UV）标度行为，该行为源于声学模式色散三次方修正及Beliaev阻尼；并建立了自旋分量作为探测激子BEC新途径的可行性。

Conclusion: 激子BEC的自旋组分为其检测提供了新方法，弥补了光学探测无法触及的局限。

Abstract: Excitons provide a promising platform for the realization of solid-state Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC), offering quantum coherence, strongly correlated electron-hole physics, and superfluidity. Yet, its unambiguous experimental identification remains challenging. In particular, $S_z= \pm 1$ triplet excitons are excellent candidates to realize an exciton BEC, because of their intrinsically limited recombination rate and thus long lifetimes. However, since their optical detection is inherently forbidden, experimental signatures remain elusive. In this work, we demonstrate that the magnetic nature of a $S_z= \pm 1$ triplet exciton BEC gives rise to stray magnetic field noise, that can be measured using nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry. Using an external magnetic field to tune the system from an antiferromagnetic to a ferromagnetic ordering, the longitudinal spin sound mode of the BEC softens, bringing the mode into the characteristic gigahertz frequency range of the NV spin relaxation rate and thus allowing direct detection. Furthermore, we demonstrate an unconventional UV scaling at small distances $d$ from the sample, owing to the cubic corrections to the sound mode dispersion and damping rate in the form of Beliaev damping. Through this approach, we establish that the spin component of exciton BECs offers new approaches to detect exciton BECs.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Anomalous Hall Effect in $d^{10}$ Oxide Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11855)
*Zeyu Li,Xudong Zhu,Yulei Han,Zhenhua Qiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一类实验可行的二维氧化物M₂DO₆（M = Zn, Cd; D = Se, Te），通过半填充O-2p轨道诱导的自发铁磁性实现量子反常霍尔效应（quantum anomalous Hall effect），并得益于C₃旋转对称性形成八个对称相关的交叉点，最终在自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）打开能隙后得到Chern数为4（𝒞=4）的高Chern数QAHE相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QAHE的磁性通常源于过渡金属d轨道或关联驱动的moireé能带，本文旨在探索能否从O-2p轨道出发实现QAHE，扩大磁性拓扑平台的物理实现途径。

Method: 提出M₂DO₆单层的具体材料体系，通过O-2p轨道半填充诱导自发铁磁性；在布里渊区中K/K′谷和Γ-K/Γ-K′线上出现自旋极化Dirac点，结合C₃旋转对称性形成八个对称相关的交叉点；再利用自旋轨道耦合打开能隙，每个有质量Dirac点贡献半Chern数，总计得到Chern数𝒞=4。

Result: 获得高Chern数QAHE相（𝒞=4）；同时提出阳离子脱嵌（cation deintercalation）作为激活氧化物中O-2p铁磁性的通用策略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从O-2p铁磁性实现QAHE的可行路径，并为超越传统d电子体系的氧基磁性拓扑平台提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Quantum anomalous Hall effect (QAHE) arises from the interplay between magnetic order and spin-orbit coupling, which opens up a topologically nontrivial band gap to host chiral edge states in the absence of magnetic field. So far, magnetic order of QAHE usually originates from partially filled transition-metal $d$ orbitals or correlation-driven moiré bands. Here, we propose an experimentally accessible family of two-dimensional oxides, M$_2$DO$_6$ (M = Zn, Cd; D = Se, Te), that can realize QAHE from the half-filled O-$2p$ orbital induced spontaneous ferromagnetism. In M$_2$DO$_6$ monolayers, spin-polarized Dirac points appear at K/K$^{\prime}$ valleys and along $Γ$-K/$Γ$-K$^{\prime}$ lines. $C_3$ rotational symmetry then generates eight symmetry-related crossings in the first Brillouin zone. Upon gap opening by spin-orbit coupling, each massive Dirac point contributes half Chern number, resulting in a high-Chern-number QAHE phase with $\mathcal{C}=4$. We establish cation deintercalation as a general strategy to activate O-$2p$ ferromagnetism in oxides. Our finding provides a route to realize QAHE from O-$2p$ ferromagnetism and offers design principles applicable to oxygen-based magnetic topology platforms beyond conventional $d$-electron systems.

</details>


### [133] [Dimensional crossover and local strain induced deflection of the spin spiral state in multiferroic NiI2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11944)
*Tianxing Jiang,Lianchuang Li,Haiyan Zhu,Hongyu Wang,Junchao Tian,Wenzhao Wang,Weiyi Pan,Haitao Wang,Changlin Zheng,Hongjun Xiang,Changsong Xu,Donglai Feng,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究结合自旋极化扫描隧道显微镜、逐层薄膜生长和多尺度理论建模，系统研究了单层范德华（vdW）材料NiI₂薄膜中自旋螺旋态随厚度的演化规律，发现层间交换能的增强驱动了维度交叉，并揭示薄膜褶皱可通过局域曲率改变交换相互作用来偏转自旋螺旋波矢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维多铁材料在集成磁电器件中具有重要前景，单层NiI₂中的自旋螺旋态已被证实可诱导铁电性，但其在二维极限下如何演化及如何被调控仍不清晰。

Method: 采用自旋极化扫描隧道显微镜（spin-polarized scanning tunneling microscopy）观测自旋螺旋结构，结合逐层（layer-by-layer）薄膜生长控制厚度，并辅以多尺度（multi-scale）理论建模进行解释与预测。

Result: 随着薄膜厚度从1增至7个单层，自旋螺旋波长持续增大，波矢方向从近[110]转动至[1-10]方向，表明维度交叉主要由增强的层间交换能（interlayer exchange energy）驱动；同时发现薄膜褶皱可通过局域曲率修饰交换相互作用，引起自旋螺旋波矢偏转。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了厚度和局域应变作为两种调控手段，可用于工程化范德华多铁材料中的非共线螺旋磁性及伴随的电极化，为低维多铁器件的设计与调控提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Low-dimensional multiferroics hold great promise for integrated magnetoelectric devices. Spin spiral state has recently been shown to induce ferroelectricity in single-layer van der Waals (vdW) material NiI2. However, how this state evolves and can be tuned towards the two-dimensional limit remain unclear. Here, we combine spin-polarized scanning tunneling microscopy, layer-by-layer film growth, and multi-scale theoretical modeling to investigate the spin spirals in NiI2 thin films. As the film thickness increases from 1 to 7 monolayers, we observed a continuous increase of spin-spiral wavelength and a rotation of wavevector from near [110] to [1-10] direction, which evidences a dimensional crossover primarily driven by enhanced interlayer exchange energy. Moreover, we find that the film wrinkles can cause deflection of the spin spiral wavevector, which is caused by local curvature induced modification of exchange interactions. Our findings establish thickness and local strain as two tuning methods for engineering non-collinear helical magnetism and accompanied electric polarization in vdW multiferroics.

</details>


### [134] [Mismatch between Raman shear modes and ferroelectric polarization in 3R-MoS$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11988)
*Johannes Schwandt-Krause,Jan-Niklas Heidkamp,Mohammed El Amine Miloudi,Swarup Deb,Elisabeth Grune,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Rico Schwartz,Oliver Kühn,Tobias Korn*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文结合开尔文探针力显微镜（Kelvin-probe force microscopy, KPFM）与低频拉曼光谱（low-frequency Raman spectroscopy），对hBN衬底上剥离的三层3R-MoS₂薄片进行极化畴与堆叠构型表征，发现零净极化堆叠ABA与BAB在KPFM中不可区分但低频剪切模显著不同，并指出标准键极化率模型无法解释该差异，提出了组合光谱-扫描探针联合表征方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行堆叠的二维范德华材料中的滑动铁电性（sliding ferroelectricity）可支撑多种新型器件概念，但利用该性质需要可靠且无损地确定底层堆叠序与极化态，现有单一表征手段存在局限性。

Method: 将KPFM与低频拉曼光谱相结合，探测hBN衬底上剥离的三层3R-MoS₂薄片；通过对比ABA与BAB两种零净极化堆叠的KPFM响应、低频剪切模以及低温光致发光（photoluminescence）信号进行系统表征。

Result: KPFM无法区分ABA与BAB两种零净极化堆叠，而两者的低频剪切模却有显著差异；该现象在多个薄片上重复观察到，并经低温光致发光验证；标准键极化率模型（bond-polarizability model）不能解释ABA与BAB剪切模活性的差异。

Conclusion: 单独使用任一现有技术均不足以确定滑动铁电堆叠序，需要采用组合的光谱-扫描探针联合表征策略来可靠地分配堆叠构型与极化态。

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectricity in parallel-stacked two-dimensional van der Waals materials enables a broad range of novel device concepts, but exploiting it requires reliable, non-destructive assignment of the underlying stacking order and polarization state. Here, we combine Kelvin-probe force microscopy (KPFM) with low-frequency Raman spectroscopy to probe the polarization domains and stacking configurations of a exfoliated trilayer 3R-MoS$_{2}$ flake on a hBN substrate. We find that ABA and BAB - both stackings with zero net polarization - are indistinguishable in KPFM, yet show drastically different low-frequency shear modes. This observation is reproduced across multiple flakes and is corroborated by low-temperature photoluminescence. Notably, the standard bond-polarizability model does not account for the difference in shear-mode activity between the ABA and BAB configurations, indicating that the interlayer Raman response of these stackings is governed by physics beyond a simple polarizability picture. Our results show that none of the here-used individual techniques alone is sufficient to assign sliding-ferroelectric stacking order and motivate a combined spectroscopic-scanning-probe approach.

</details>


### [135] [Lectures on ultrathin film ferromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12189)
*D. Pescia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本讲义回顾了由3d过渡金属覆盖层构成的超薄膜（ultrathin film）铁磁性研究的基本原理，重点探讨了量子阱效应、二维自旋体系的基态性质、有限温度下的铁磁有序、临界行为以及垂直自旋取向引发的拓扑激发与条纹相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超薄膜铁磁性的根本机制，特别是二维性如何影响磁基态、磁有序的有限温度行为以及磁各向异性，从而为纳米磁性材料的设计提供理论基础。

Method: 基于层状生长的3d过渡金属覆盖层超薄膜，从量子阱限制出发构建二维自旋体系；采用重整化群（renormalization group）框架分析有限温度磁有序与临界行为，并通过临界点附近数据的精确分析来验证二维Ising普适类（two-dimensional Ising universality class）的描述。

Result: 研究发现二维性导致“死”磁层（dead magnetic layers）、增强磁矩（enhanced magnetic moments）、层间磁耦合振荡以及异常的垂直与面内各向异性；铁磁序在有限温度下得以保持，且其临界行为符合二维Ising普适类；垂直自旋取向可通过重取向相变（reorientation phase transition）转为面内；垂直取向引入由反向垂直自旋条纹构成的拓扑激发（topological excitations）。

Conclusion: 二维超薄膜铁磁性可由重整化群框架自洽解释，但条纹有序向顺磁态的相变机制仍未被完全理解，是未来研究的一个重要开放问题。

Abstract: In these Lecture Notes we review some of the fundamental principles that have emerged from research on the ferromagnetism of ultrathin films consisting of 3d transition-metal overlayers. Their growth is often layer-by-layer. This growth mode produces quantum wells along the vertical direction that profoundly impact any physical property of the materials. In addition, the vertical confinement establishes spin ensembles that extend to macroscopic distances along the in-plane directions and are finite along the vertical (perpendicular) direction, i.e. they are two-dimensional. Accordingly, they display ground state properties that originate from the two-dimensionality, such as ``dead'' magnetic layers or ``enhanced magnetic moments'', an oscillatory interlayer magnetic coupling and an anomalous perpendicular versus in-plane magnetic anisotropy that produces, in some specific situations, a perpendicular collective orientation of the spins. At finite temperatures, ferromagnetic order is observed to persist and an analysis of the magnetic order of ultrathin films in terms of the renormalization group provides a suitable framework for explaining this observation. The ferromagnetic order is lost at a phase transition which follows closely the two-dimensional Ising universality class, as shown by an accurate analysis of data in the vicinity of the critical point. The perpendicular spin orientation is often observed to turn in-plane by a reorientation phase transition which is also properly described by a renormalization group argument. Finally, the perpendicular spin orientation introduces topological excitations of the ferromagnetic order, consisting of stripes of reversed perpendicular spin direction. The stripe order undergoes a phase transition to the paramagnetic state that is not yet completely understood.

</details>


### [136] [Electron transport in a 1.6~nm-thick double-gated (100) silicon nanosheet: A theoretical study accounting for phonon confinement and remote-phonon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12214)
*Shoaib Mansoori,Bimin Cai,Edward Chen,Dallin O. Nielsen,Massimo V. Fischetti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了1.6纳米厚的（100）硅纳米片（silicon nanosheet）中电子输运行为，重点考察声子束缚（phonon confinement）和界面杂化等离激元-声子激发（IPP，即"远程声子"）对电子迁移率与饱和速度的影响。研究发现，声子边界条件的选取显著影响迁移率；声子束缚效应使室温迁移率显著低于体相假设的结果；高场饱和速度因primed子带的复杂结构而低于体相值；IPP散射对低场迁移率压制较小，但能提高饱和速度，因此高k栅介质不会负面影响硅纳米片场效应晶体管性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硅纳米片晶体管的尺寸不断缩小，量子束缚和界面效应（如远程声子散射）对载流子输运的影响日益显著，但现有理论对相关物理过程的理解尚不充分，尤其是声子束缚的边界条件选择以及界面杂化激发（IPP）对输运特性的具体影响亟待澄清。

Method: 利用局域经验赝势（local empirical pseudopotentials）计算能带结构；采用近似的弹性连续模型（elastic continuum model）考虑声学声子束缚；利用介电连续极限（dielectric continuum limit）处理IPPs；对比不同的声子边界条件并计算低场迁移率与高场饱和速度。

Result: 在更真实的声子边界条件（声学声子在SiO2/HfO2界面被钳制，光学声子在Si/SiO2界面被钳制）下，室温迁移率远低于采用体相声子假设的结果。高场饱和速度因primed子带的复杂结构而显著低于体相值，这一现象曾在Si反型层中观测到但此前未被理论解释。IPP散射对低场迁移率有轻微压制，但由于SiO2中间层和金属栅的邻近效应，压制程度较小；同时IPP使电子保持"更冷"，反而提高了饱和速度。

Conclusion: 在硅纳米片场效应晶体管中，声子束缚的边界条件选择对电子输运计算结果影响重大；高k介质栅堆叠中的IPP散射不会显著损害器件性能，因为SiO2界面层的存在削弱了IPPs的散射强度，且IPP散射反而通过降低电子温度提升饱和速度。

Abstract: We study theoretically electron transport in an top-and bottom-gated (100) 1.6 nm-thin silicon nanosheet with SiO2/HfO2 gate stacks, focusing on the intrinsic physical processes that affect transport: the confinement of phonons and the presence of interface hybrid plasmon-phonon excitations (IPPs or `remote phonons'). The band structure is calculated using local empirical pseudopotentials; an approximated elastic continuum model is used to consider the confinement of acoustic phonons; the dielectric continuum limit is used to deal with the IPPs. We find that the electron mobility is affected significantly by the boundary conditions chosen to deal with phonon confinement. The more realistic assumption of phonons clamped at the SiO2/HfO2 interfaces and optical phonons at the Si/SiO2 interfaces results in a room temperature mobility much smaller than what is obtained using the common assumption of bulk phonons in the elastic, high-temperature approximation. We also find that, as a result of the complicated structure of the primed subbands, the high-field saturated velocity is significantly lower than its bulk value, as it had been measured in the past in the case of Si inversion layers but never explained theoretically. Finally, we find that IPP scattering does depress the low-field mobility but to a small extent, thanks to the presence of the interfacial SiO2 layers and to the proximity of the metal gates. Moreover, by keeping electrons `cooler', IPP scattering results in a higher saturated velocity. Therefore, the presence of high-kappa materials in the gate-insulator stacks should not affect negatively the performance of field effect transistors based on Si nanosheets.

</details>

### [137] [Robust Quantum Machine Learning for Collider Event Selection under Detector Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11330)
*Christopher Brown,Michael Spannowsky,Simon Williams*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了参数化量子模型（parameterised quantum models）在高能物理对撞机事件选择中的稳健性归纳偏置问题。在无监督和有监督两种设置下，量子自编码器（quantum autoencoder）和含数据重上传（data reuploading）的量子分类器在特征模糊（smearing）条件下表现出比经典基线的更小输出漂移，表明量子模型能提供有用的稳健性归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验进入高亮度时代，检测器退化、运行条件变化和校准漂移会导致数据分布偏移，使训练于干净参考样本上的模型在部署后性能下降。作者希望探究参数化量子模型是否能提供一种有用的归纳偏置，以增强对撞机事件选择方法的稳健性。

Method: 研究涵盖两种互补设置：无监督方面，将在背景事件上训练的量子自编码器与经典自编码器和变分自编码器（variational autoencoder）在异常检测任务上比较；有监督方面，训练含数据重上传的量子分类器区分超对称（supersymmetric）信号与背景，并与线性和多层感知机（multi-layer perceptron）分类器比较。所有模型在参考条件下训练并在受控特征级模糊下评估，同时固定模型参数和预处理变换。

Result: 在干净输入上，量子自编码器达到与经典方法竞争的异常检测性能，包括触发所需的低假阳性率区间；更深的数据重上传分类器达到了与非线性经典基线相当的判别能力。在模糊条件下，量子模型总体输出分数的漂移更小，且比表达性强的经典基线更有效地保持判别力。

Conclusion: 参数化量子模型可为对撞机事件选择提供有用的稳健性归纳偏置，值得在更真实的探测器系统效应、有限射击统计（finite-shot statistics）和量子设备噪声条件下开展进一步研究。

Abstract: Robust machine-learning methods are becoming increasingly important for high-energy physics data analysis as experiments enter the era of higher luminosity and future higher-energy colliders. Detector degradation, changing running conditions and calibration drift can shift data distributions, causing models trained on clean reference samples to degrade after deployment. We investigate whether parameterised quantum models provide a useful inductive bias for robust collider-event selection in two complementary settings. In the unsupervised study, quantum autoencoders trained on background events are compared with classical and variational autoencoders for anomaly detection. In the supervised study, quantum classifiers with data reuploading are trained to distinguish a supersymmetric signal from background and are compared with linear and multilayer-perceptron classifiers. All models are trained under reference conditions and subsequently evaluated under controlled feature-level smearing while their parameters and preprocessing transformations are held fixed. On clean inputs, the quantum autoencoders achieve competitive anomaly-detection performance, including in the low-false-positive-rate regime relevant for triggering, while the deeper data-reuploading classifier attains discrimination comparable to the non-linear classical baseline. Under smearing, the quantum models generally exhibit smaller shifts in their output scores and retain their discrimination more effectively than the expressive classical baselines. These results suggest that parameterised quantum models can provide a useful robustness inductive bias for collider-event selection and motivate further studies with realistic detector systematics, finite-shot statistics and quantum-device noise.

</details>


### [138] [Spin-coherent quantum designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11310)
*Marcin Rudziński,Aaron Z. Goldberg,Andrei B. Klimov,Luis L. Sánchez-Soto,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限自旋相干态（finite spin-coherent states）在秩分辨（rank-resolved）框架下重构量子算符的问题，证明了一个算符在秩-${S}$扇区中能被精确重构的充要条件是采样点构成球面$(2J+S)$-设计，并给出了名为自旋相干量子设计（spin-coherent quantum designs）的构型以及显式的正权Gauss-Legendre构造方法，为自旋系统提供了类似von Neumann晶格的统一读出框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干态连接了量子与经典物理，但其过完备且非正交的性质使得识别重构量子信息所需的最小离散点集变得困难。此前已有有限自旋相干层析和离散相干态算符基的研究，但本文希望进一步解决更具体的秩分辨问题，即保持典范反变符号（canonical contravariant-symbol）表示。

Method: 本文通过数学严格证明建立了充要条件：在秩-$S$扇区中，典范有限相干态公式能够精确重构所有算符，当且仅当采样点构成球面$(2J+S)$-设计。同时，本文提出了一个完全显式的正权Gauss-Legendre构造方案，避免了对等权球面设计（equal-weight spherical design）的依赖。

Result: 建立了自旋相干量子设计的统一框架，为自旋系统提供了类似于von Neumann晶格（von Neumann lattice）之于典范相干态的作用。此外，推导了从这些构造中估计自旋算符矩（moments of spin operators）的实际协议，可直接应用于偏振测量（polarimetry）、磁力测量（magnetometry）和量子态层析（quantum state tomography）。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了从少量测量样本中读出物理可观测量（physical observables）的统一框架，为自旋系统的量子信息重构提供了理论基础和实用协议。

Abstract: Coherent states bridge the gap between quantum and classical physics, but their overcomplete and nonorthogonal nature makes it difficult to identify the minimal discrete set needed to reconstruct quantum information. Finite spin-coherent tomography and discrete coherent-state operator bases are known, but here we address the more specific rank-resolved problem of preserving the canonical contravariant-symbol representation. We show that the canonical finite coherent-state formula reconstructs every operator in the rank-$S$ sector exactly if and only if the sampling points form a spherical $(2J+S)$-design. We call the associated configurations spin-coherent quantum designs. We further give a fully explicit positive-weight Gauss-Legendre construction that avoids the need for an equal-weight spherical design. Together, these results establish a unified framework for reading out physical observables from a handful of measurement samples, playing for spin systems the role that the so-called von Neumann lattice plays for canonical coherent states. Finally, we derive practical protocols for estimating moments of spin operators from these constructions, with direct applications to polarimetry, magnetometry, and quantum state tomography.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum Inequalities from the Second Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11817)
*Andrea Palessandro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从热力学第二定律出发，在一个将量子场与小型热探测器耦合的操作性框架中，推导出了Ford-Roman量子不等式，证明了负能量密度区域的大小与持续时间受限于一个与状态无关的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子场论（quantum field theory）中，经典能量条件可被局部违反，从而出现负能量密度区域。Ford-Roman量子不等式（Ford-Roman quantum inequalities）用于定量刻画这类负能量区域的幅度与持续时间，但需要给出一个更根本的物理推导基础。

Method: 采用一个操作性框架，将量子场耦合到一个小型热探测器（thermal detector），以此作为场能量密度的物理探针。利用两个系统之间纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的非负性，推导出探测器热损失的严格下界。

Result: 证明了探测器热损失（heat loss）被一个与状态无关的量从下方所界定，从而由热力学第二定律的一致性要求直接推出Ford-Roman量子不等式。

Conclusion: Ford-Roman量子不等式可以从热力学第二定律及纠缠熵的非负性中导出，为其提供了基于热力学原理的物理推导路径。

Abstract: Quantum field theory allows local violations of the classical energy conditions, such as regions of negative energy density. The Ford-Roman quantum inequalities quantify how negative these regions can be and for how long. We present a derivation of these inequalities from the second law of thermodynamics, in an operational framework that couples the field to a small thermal detector. The detector acts as a physical probe of the field's energy density and, by the non-negativity of the entanglement entropy between the two systems, one can show that its heat loss is bounded from below by a state-independent quantity.

</details>


### [140] [Limitations on Joint Coherence Transfer in Quantum Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11872)
*Piotr Ćwikliński,Michał Studziński*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多个相干性转移（coherence transfer）能否被同一热力学过程同时优化的问题。作者证明这在一般情况下是一个兼容性问题：利用热操作（thermal operations）的能量分辨Stinespring表示，相干转移振幅的饱和条件（Cauchy–Schwarz不等式等号条件）在多个转移之间无法独立满足。通过解析分析辅以计算机辅助的半定规划（semidefinite programming）验证，对于混合简并哈密顿量H=diag(0,1,1,3)，不存在协变Gibbs保持通道能同时达到三个单独最优的方向性相干转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学对能级之间的相干性有强约束，现有研究关注于单独优化相干转移，但尚未回答多个最优转移能否在同一热力学过程中同时实现这一基本兼容问题。作者希望从热操作的结构出发，揭示单独最优与联合最优之间的张力。

Method: 作者使用能量分辨的Stinespring表示将热操作中的基本相干转移振幅表示为总能量壳上浴权重向量的内积，Cauchy–Schwarz不等式的饱和要求这些向量共线。由于所有转移源于同一个能量保持的系综–浴酉演化，多个饱和条件不能独立施加；迹保持和Gibbs保持进一步引入相位闭合条件和多边形不等式。解析结果辅以严格的计算机辅助半定规划分析，对混合简并哈密顿量给出了机器验证的对偶界。

Result: 对于H=diag(0,1,1,3)，半定规划的原始和对偶界机器认证表明，不存在协变Gibbs保持通道能同时达到三个单独最优的方向性相干转移。由于每个热操作都是协变且Gibbs保持的，同一三重最优值也无法由热操作实现。该不相容性与混合简并能级的Choi扇区Gram矩阵中的范围包含约束相关。

Conclusion: 单独考虑时最优的相干转移不必然兼容于同一个物理热力学过程。作者指出，在热操作框架内对此不相容性进行完整刻画仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Coherences between different energy levels are strongly constrained by thermodynamics. Here we ask a related question: if several coherence transfers can be optimized separately, can they also be optimized simultaneously by the same thermodynamic process? We show that this is in general a compatibility problem. Using an energy-resolved Stinespring representation of thermal operations, we express elementary coherence-transfer amplitudes as inner products of bath-weighted vectors associated with total-energy shells. Saturation of the corresponding Cauchy--Schwarz bounds requires these vectors to be collinear. Since all transfers have to arise from the same energy-preserving system--bath unitary, such saturation conditions cannot in general be imposed independently. Trace and Gibbs preservation lead to additional phase-closure conditions and to polygon inequalities that can rule out simultaneous saturation for fixed population-transfer data.
  We complement these analytic results with a rigorous computer-assisted semidefinite-program analysis. For the mixed-degenerate Hamiltonian $H=\operatorname{diag}(0,1,1,3)$, machine-certified primal and dual bounds show that no covariant Gibbs-preserving channel can simultaneously attain three individually optimal directional coherence transfers. Since every thermal operation is covariant and Gibbs preserving, the same triple of optimal values cannot be attained by a thermal operation. We further relate this incompatibility to range-inclusion constraints in Choi-sector Gram matrices for mixed-degenerate energy levels. Our results show that coherence transfers which are optimal when considered separately need not be compatible with one physical thermodynamic process. A full characterization of this incompatibility directly within thermal operations remains open.

</details>


### [141] [Isospectral majorization and isoperimetric inequalities for coherent states on the Bloch sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12248)
*Luis Daniel Abreu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Lieb-Solovej不等式的等谱版本，并将其应用于球面上Toeplitz算子的Schatten范数不等式，从而无需使用球面等周不等式即可获得所有Schatten和的等周不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb-Solovej不等式在量子光学与Bloch相干态研究中具有重要意义，但以往仅已知其特定形式的估计。本文旨在建立该不等式的等谱（isospectral）推广，以获得更一般的不等式并应用于Toeplitz算子的特征值估计。

Method: 利用解析多项式构成的N+1维Hilbert空间上的Husimi函数，考虑将密度算子的特征值按降序重新排列到单项式基上得到的态，证明对于任意凸函数，该重排态的函数泛函积分不小于原始态的相应积分。同时利用Lieb-Solovej通道输出的受优（majorization）关系，结合球面上Toeplitz算子的符号分析建立特征值不等式。

Result: 建立了Lieb-Solovej不等式的等谱版本，并由此推导出球面的球冠（spherical caps）在固定面积条件下最大化Toeplitz算子所有Ky Fan范数与Schatten和；得到的锐利不等式将先前仅对r=1成立的结果推广到所有阶数。

Conclusion: 该等谱不等式为Husimi函数的凸泛函提供了统一框架，且其球面应用表明球冠具有最优性，无需借助球面等周不等式即可获得Toeplitz算子的Schatten和等周不等式。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{P}_{N}$ be the $(N+1)$-dimensional Hilbert space of analytic polynomials of degree at most $N$. This is the natural environment to define $SU(2)$ (Bloch) coherent states. Let $Q_{ρ}$ be the Husimi function of a density operator $ρ$ on $% \mathcal{P}_{N}$. We prove an isospectral version of Lieb-Solovej inequality: if $ρ^{\downarrow }$ is obtained by placing the eigenvalues of $ρ$ in decreasing order along the monomial basis, then \begin{equation*} \int_{\mathbb{C}}Φ(Q_{ρ}(z))\,dm(z)\leq \int_{\mathbb{C}}Φ(Q_{ρ^{\downarrow }}(z))\,dm(z) \end{equation*}% for every convex function $Φ$ on $[0,1]$. Applying the corresponding reversed inequality to the concave function $Φ(t)=-t\log t$ gives the Wehrl entropy. In the process it is show that the output state of $ρ$ under Lieb-Solovej's channel is majorized by the output of the state $ρ^{\downarrow }$.
  As an application, among all measurable subsets of the sphere having a fixed area, spherical caps maximize every partial sum of the eigenvalues of Toeplitz operators with symbol $1_Ω$. Equivalently, caps maximize all Ky Fan norms, leading to the sharp inequalities, previously known for $r=1$: \begin{equation*} \sum_{j=1}^{r}λ_{j}(Ω) \leq r-\sum_{k=0}^{r-1}(r-k)\binom{N+1}{k} m(Ω)^{k}\bigl(1-m(Ω)\bigr)^{N+1-k}. \end{equation*} This implies isoperimetric inequalities for all Schatten sums of $T_Ω$, obtained without using the spherical isoperimetric inequality.

</details>


### [142] [Spectrally local geometric response at the onset of many-body quantum chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11309)
*George Mihailescu,Pablo M. Poggi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入“几何响应的谱密度”（spectral density of geometric response）作为一种衡量本征态跨能量谱灵敏度的新指标，并将其应用于多体量子系统，揭示了从可积性到混沌的指数灵敏交叉现象。物理动机下的随机矩阵系综（random-matrix ensembles）能重现该行为，而高斯系综（Gaussian ensembles）不能，表明通往混沌的路径具有高斯系综无法捕捉的普适特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于量化多体量子系统中本征态对扰动的响应如何随能量谱变化，特别是理解可积性到混沌（integrability-to-chaos）过渡的微观机制，并检验现有随机矩阵模型能否准确捕捉这一过程中的普适规律。

Method: 作者引入“几何响应的谱密度”作为测量工具，用于刻画本征态在整个能量谱中的灵敏度分布。将该指标应用于多体量子系统，并对比物理动机驱动的随机矩阵系综与高斯系综的预测结果。

Result: 研究发现，在可积性到混沌的过渡中存在指数灵敏的交叉现象：本征基的形变起初集中在局部谱区域，随后才扩散至整个谱。物理动机化的随机矩阵系综能复现这一行为，而高斯系综不能。

Conclusion: 通往混沌的路径包含普适特征，但高斯系综这类“无特征”（featureless）的随机矩阵模型无法描述这些特征，说明需要物理上更具动机的系综才能正确建模量子多体系统中由可积到混沌的过渡。

Abstract: We introduce the spectral density of geometric response, a measure of eigenstate sensitivity across an energy spectrum. Applied to many-body quantum systems, it reveals an exponentially sensitive integrability-to-chaos crossover, where eigenbasis deformations first accumulate in localized spectral regions before spreading throughout the spectrum. Physically motivated random-matrix ensembles reproduce this behaviour, whereas Gaussian ensembles do not, indicating universal features of the route to chaos that are absent from featureless random-matrix models.

</details>


### [143] [Exchange Fluctuation Theorems for Non-Markovian Baths in Quantum Collisional Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11488)
*Sayan Mondal,Sukrut Mondkar,Ujjwal Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）动力学下量子交换涨落定理（quantum exchange fluctuation theorem）的推广，通过微观碰撞模型（microscopic collisional model）证明浴的记忆效应会增强逆温度梯度的热传递概率，修改了传统Jarzynski-Wójcik交换涨落定理的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子交换涨落定理是在马尔可夫浴假设下建立的，但实际环境中浴常具有记忆效应。本文旨在探究非马尔可夫动力学如何推广该定理，并建立环境记忆与非平衡热交换统计之间的微观联系。

Method: 采用微观碰撞模型，通过在每次热交换碰撞前让连续浴辅助体（bath auxiliaries）之间发生相互作用来生成浴记忆。分别对直接浴-浴相互作用和探针介导的稳态热交换情形推导交换涨落定理，并以量子比特辅助体为例进行验证。

Result: 推导出直接浴-浴相互作用及探针介导热交换两种情形下的交换涨落定理；以量子比特辅助体为例的数值分析表明，非马尔可夫记忆能够增强逆温度梯度的热传递（heat transfer against the temperature gradient）概率，从而偏离传统Jarzynski-Wójcik交换涨落定理的预测。

Conclusion: 研究建立了环境记忆（environmental memory）与非平衡热交换统计（non-equilibrium heat-exchange statistics）之间的微观联系，揭示了非马尔可夫浴对热流涨落行为的本质影响。

Abstract: The quantum exchange fluctuation theorem relates the probabilities of observing heat transfer along and against the temperature gradient between thermal baths at different temperatures. We investigate how this relation generalizes when the baths exhibit non-Markovian dynamics. Using a microscopic collisional model, bath memory is generated through interactions between successive bath auxiliaries before each heat-exchange collision. We derive exchange fluctuation theorems for both direct bath-bath interactions and probe-mediated heat exchange in the steady-state regime. As an illustrative example, we consider heat baths with qubit auxiliaries and show that non-Markovian memory enhances the probability of heat-transfer events against the temperature gradient, modifying the predictions made by the conventional Jarzynski-Wójcik exchange fluctuation theorem. Our results establish a microscopic connection between environmental memory and non-equilibrium heat-exchange statistics.

</details>


### [144] [Antibunching Enhancement via Non-Markovianity in a Hybrid Optical-Microwave Cross-Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11783)
*Shiying Gu,Jun-Qi Li,Liping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出在混合光-微波系统中利用掺铒Y₂SiO₅晶体诱导非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）效应，以增强光子阻塞（photon blockade）并产生高纯度反聚束（antibunched）光，二阶关联函数被抑制到10⁻⁷量级，并将环境非马尔可夫性从退相干源转化为可控量子资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统马尔可夫（Markovian）系统中不可逆耗散会逐步破坏相位相干性，限制了高纯度单光子的产生。作者旨在利用非马尔可夫环境中环境信息回流来增强光子阻塞，克服这一局限。

Method: 将Er³⁺:Y₂SiO₅晶体装载到混合光-微波系统中，对微波腔施加非马尔可夫效应；基于时间卷积消除（time-convolutionless）非马尔可夫框架，在弱耦合（weak-coupling）条件下推导出与时间相关的非马尔可夫衰变率和重整化（renormalized）微波驱动。

Result: 二阶关联函数被显著抑制，可达10⁻⁷量级，因为环境信息回流增强了光子阻塞；该结构化库（structured reservoir）工程通过光学失谐（optical detuning）提供了额外自由维度；延迟二阶关联呈现出展宽的反聚束轮廓，放宽了单光子同步的时间要求。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明环境非马尔可夫性可以从退相干源转化为可控量子资源，为高纯度单光子产生开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Unconventional photon blockade (UPB) provides an attractive route for generating antibunched light through quantum interference without requiring strong nonlinear coupling. In conventional Markovian systems, irreversible dissipation progressively destroys the phase coherence, even limiting the achievable single-photon purity. Here we propose a scheme for imposing non-Markovian effects on a microwave cavity where a Er$^{3+}$:Y$_2$SiO$_5$ crystal loaded into a hybrid optical-microwave system. Based on the time-convolutionless non-Markovian framework, we derive the time-dependent non-Markovian decay rate and the renormalized microwave drive in the weak-coupling regime. We show that the second-order correlation function is significantly suppressed, even to the order of $10^{-7}$, since the backflow of environmental information enhances photon blocking. In addition, this structured reservoir engineering not only provides an additional degree of freedom, but also further optimizes the blockade via optical detuning. Importantly, the delayed second-order correlation exhibits a broadened antibunching profile, thereby relaxing the timing requirements for single-photon synchronization. Our results demonstrate that environmental non-Markovianity can be transformed from a source of decoherence into a controllable quantum resource, opening new opportunities for high-purity single-photon generation.

</details>


### [145] [Hamilton-Zero: A Neural Tensor-Network Foundation Model for Ground States of Arbitrary Quadratic Qubit Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11911)
*Timothy Heightman,Elena Orlova,Philip Mantrov,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个约5亿变分参数的基础模型（foundation model），利用大型语言模型和深度强化学习的当代技术训练，能够将量子优势承诺所依赖的哈密顿量基态计算问题在任意通用的哈密顿量集合上进行摊销。通过将自旋-1/2量子基态学习表述为SU(2)^N流形上的变分优化，该模型实现了对超出经典模拟方法范畴的量子系统的基态计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势（quantum advantage）的核心承诺之一在于能够计算经典模拟方法无法触及的哈密顿系统基态。作者试图证明这一计算难题可以通过一个基础模型在不同哈密顿量之间进行有效的摊销（amortization），从而超越传统逐系统求解的范式。

Method: 方法上，作者将自旋-1/2（spin-1/2）量子基态学习形式化为SU(2)^N流形上的变分优化（manifold variational optimisation），将显式希尔伯特空间矢量振幅替换为由李导数（Lie derivatives）作用的多级函数，并通过自定义自动微分原语（custom automatic differentiation primitives）进行求值。利用Peter-Weyl定理证明了该流形上的变分原理保持自旋-1/2扇区基态上界。训练阶段采用了自研的SU(2)副本交换Langevin采样器和分片自然梯度优化（sharded natural-gradient optimisation），并扩展了Kronecker-Factored近似曲率（KFAC）优化器，在多达64量子比特的系统上训练；随后在多达1024量子比特的系统上微调，并在高达8100量子比特的系统上评估。

Result: 该基础模型在数十万个不同哈密顿系统的数据集上进行预训练，涵盖变化的连接拓扑、系统尺寸、相互作用类型和强度。在保留的泛化数据集上，模型能够进行微调并推广至更大规模系统，评估规模覆盖最高8100个量子比特的系统。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基态计算问题可以通过一个通用基础模型跨越任意哈密顿量集合得到有效摊销，为量子基态计算提供了新的范式路径，并同时融合了一个世纪以来的多体物理文献数据。

Abstract: A central promise of useful quantum advantage is the ability to compute ground states of Hamiltonian systems beyond the reach of classical simulation methods. Here we demonstrate that this problem can be effectively amortized across an arbitrary and universal set of Hamiltonians by a foundation model with $\sim0.5$B variational parameters, trained with contemporary techniques from large language models and deep reinforcement learning. To do this, we formulate $\text{spin-}1/2$ quantum ground-state learning as manifold variational optimisation over centrally odd scalar functions on $\mathrm{SU}(2)^N$. This replaces explicit Hilbert-space vector amplitudes with manifold functions on which the Hamiltonian acts through Lie derivatives, evaluated by custom automatic differentiation primitives. We prove that the resulting variational principle on this manifold preserves the $\text{spin-}1/2$ sector's ground-state upper bound using the Peter-Weyl theorem, then pre-train our foundation model on a dataset of hundreds of thousands of different Hamiltonian systems, varying the connection topology, system size, interaction types and strengths, bringing together a century of many-body literature. Using a novel $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ replica-exchange Langevin sampler and sharded natural-gradient optimisation, we train our model with our own extension of the Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (KFAC) optimiser on system sizes up to 64 qubits. On a held-out generalisation dataset, we fine-tune our model on system sizes of up to 1024 qubits, and evaluate on systems up to 8100 qubits.

</details>


### [146] [Enhancing the power of a quantum heat engine via control of the system--reservoir coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12055)
*Sabrina Burgardt,Julian Feß,Silvia Hiebel,Eric Lutz,Artur Widera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在实验上实现了对量子奥托发动机（quantum Otto engine）中系统与储热库之间微观相互作用的直接控制。研究以超冷铯-133原子作为工作介质、铷-87原子作为原子储热库，通过调节库原子的动能温度来改变非弹性s波散射速率，从而控制等容加热和冷却冲程中的换热定律和弛豫动力学，最终在固定效率下优化功率输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热机的性能预测表明，通过加速动力学可以增强其性能，但直接微观控制机器与储热库之间的热传递此前一直难以实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，实验演示对系统-储热库相互作用的微观控制能力。

Method: 实验采用超冷铯-133原子（Cs-133）作为量子奥托发动机的工作介质，耦合到超冷铷-87原子（Rb-87）构成的原子储热库。两者之间的热交换由非弹性s波碰撞介导，其能量依赖的散射截面导致等容加热与冷却冲程中出现非对称的平衡动力学。通过调节原子储热库的动能温度，作者改变相应的微观散射速率，从而调控换热定律。

Result: 通过控制储热库的动能温度，作者成功调制了微观的多指数弛豫动力学，实现了对发动机循环内时间分配的调控，从而在保持效率不变的条件下优化了功率输出。这首次实验证明了系统-储热库相互作用的微观控制能力。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了系统-储热库相互作用的微观控制作为纳米尺度下操纵热流、工程化量子热机有限时间性能的有效工具。这一突破为量子热力学器件的设计与优化提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium properties of open quantum systems are determined by the microscopic laws governing energy exchange with their environment. In particular, an enhancement of the performance of quantum heat engines has been predicted by speeding up the dynamics through control of the system--bath interaction. However, direct microscopic control of heat transfer between the machine and the reservoir has remained elusive so far. Here, we experimentally demonstrate such control in a quantum Otto engine realized with ultracold Cs-133 atoms coupled to an atomic reservoir of ultracold Rb-87 atoms. Heat exchange between the two is mediated by inelastic s-wave collisions whose energy-dependent scattering cross sections lead to an asymmetric equilibration dynamics in the isochoric heating and cooling strokes. By tuning the kinetic temperature of the atomic reservoir, we modify the associated microscopic scattering rates, and thereby the heat transfer law, giving control over the time allocation within the engine cycle through control over the microscopic, multi-exponential relaxation dynamics. This enables power output optimization at fixed efficiency. Our results establish microscopic control of system-reservoir interactions as a tool for manipulating heat flow at the nanoscale and engineering the finite-time performance of quantum thermal machines.

</details>


### [147] [Conditional dependence and Scrooge ensembles in shallow random quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12255)
*Yinchen Liu,Max McGinley,Thomas Schuster,David Gosset*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维浅层随机量子电路（geometrically local shallow random quantum circuits）在测量部分量子比特后产生的条件依赖结构，并提出一个猜想：当电路深度超过常数临界值d*时，给定B部分测量结果、对所有C部分测量结果取平均的后测量态系综可由Scrooge系综（一种Haar系综的推广）近似描述。作为推论，带有微小的去极化噪声（depolarizing noise rate Ω(log(n)/n)）的n比特浅层随机量子电路可以被经典高效模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维几何局域浅层随机量子电路的输出态因光锥（lightcone）结构而不具有长程关联，但测量部分量子比特会诱导长程纠缠和条件相关性。理解这种测量诱导的条件依赖结构及其对量子优势（quantum advantage）的影响，是该研究的主要动机。

Method: 作者将量子比特划分为三部分ABC，考虑给定B上特定测量结果、对C上所有可能测量结果取平均的后测量态系综。他们提出并论证了一个猜想：当电路深度超过常数临界值d*时，该系综可由Scrooge系综（Scrooge ensemble）近似，并提供了数值和解析两方面的支持性证据。

Result: 该猜想的含义是：剩余未测量量子比特上，状态保留了光锥结构，但同时通过测量过程发展出一些全局随机的特征。由此推论，在存在噪声率低至Ω(log(n)/n)的去极化噪声（depolarizing noise）时，二维n比特浅层随机量子电路可以被经典高效模拟。

Conclusion: 测量过程可以在浅层随机量子电路中诱导出条件性的长程相关性，这些相关性使未测量部分的状态在特定意义下接近全局随机（Scrooge）系综；然而这种全局随机性反而使得电路在轻微噪声条件下失去量子优势，变得可被经典高效模拟。

Abstract: The output state of a 2D geometrically local shallow random quantum circuit does not have long range correlations due to its lightcone structure. But this changes if one measures a subset of the qubits: long-range entanglement can be induced by the measurement process, leading to conditional correlations between distant qubits. In this paper we investigate the structure of conditional dependence in these circuits and its consequences for quantum advantage. For a tripartition $ABC$ of the qubits, we consider the ensemble of post-measurement states on $A$ that is conditioned on a specific measurement outcome on $B$ and ranges over all possible measurement outcomes on $C$. For circuit depths exceeding a constant critical value $d^*$, we conjecture that this ensemble is well approximated by a certain generalization of the Haar ensemble, called the Scrooge ensemble~[Jozsa \textit{et al.}, \href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevA.49.668}{Phys. Rev. A \textbf{49}, 668 (1994)}]; we also provide supporting numerical and analytical evidence. Our conjecture describes a precise sense in which the state retains its lightcone structure on the remaining unmeasured qubits, but also develops some globally random features arising from the measurement. A consequence is that $n$-qubit shallow random quantum circuits in two dimensions are classically efficiently simulable in the presence of a tiny depolarizing noise rate $Ω(\log(n)/n)$.

</details>
