# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Geometry of Noisy Quantum Many-Body Dynamics with Continuous Symmetries: Entanglement and Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11297)
*Marco Lastres,Sanjay Moudgalya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有全局连续对称性（如U(1)和SU(2)）的噪声Brownian模型中的酉幺正量子动力学，通过将平均晚时动力学映射到有效副本哈密顿量的低能物理，发现演化由k-交换子（k-commutant）对称代数的量子几何控制，从而为亚弹道Rényi纠缠增长和反常非流体动力学关联子衰减提供了统一的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已识别出U(1)对称系统中的"空穴"（void）态在可观测量动力学中的重要作用，但其机制尚不清晰。作者希望建立一个统一的一般性几何框架来系统研究具有连续对称性的噪声系统中的可观测量，包括Haar随机电路。

Method: 将平均晚时动力学映射到有效副本哈密顿量（replica Hamiltonian）的低能物理；利用时间依赖变分原理（time-dependent variational principle, TDVP）分析k-交换子流形（k-commutant manifolds）的几何结构；对比相互作用系统（含阿贝尔与非阿贝尔连续对称性）与自由费米子系统的k-交换子几何差异。

Result: 相互作用系统中k-交换子仅由系统对称性决定，与噪声演化的微观细节无关。亚弹道Rényi纠缠增长和反常非流体动力学关联子衰减与k-交换子流形中的奇点密切相关，这些奇点源于连续在位对称性产生的希尔伯特空间中冻结的"空穴"（void）态。阿贝尔与非阿贝尔连续对称性系统以及自由费米子系统的k-交换子几何存在差异。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通用的几何框架，用于系统研究具有连续对称性的噪声系统中的可观测量动力学，包括Haar随机电路，并阐明了空穴态在这些可观察量动力学中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study unitary quantum dynamics in noisy Brownian models with global continuous symmetries, such as $U(1)$ and $SU(2)$, focusing on Rényi entanglement entropies and hydrodynamic and non-hydrodynamic correlators. By mapping the averaged late-time dynamics to the low-energy physics of effective replica Hamiltonians, we find that the evolution is controlled by the quantum geometry of their ground-state manifolds, which is directly related to the geometry of $k$-commutants---the symmetry algebra of $k$ replicas of the system. In interacting systems, these $k$-commutants are generically determined solely by the symmetries of the system, independent of microscopic details of the noisy evolution. This allows us to use the time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) to provide simple geometric explanations for the sub-ballistic Rényi entanglement growth and the anomalous decay of non-hydrodynamic correlators in interacting systems with continuous symmetries. We find this behavior to be intimately connected to singularities within the $k$-commutant manifolds, arising from frozen ``void'' states in the Hilbert space that exist due to continuous on-site symmetries. This also demystifies the important role of voids in the dynamics of these observables, previously identified in $U(1)$ symmetric systems. We compare these behaviors in interacting systems with Abelian and non-Abelian continuous symmetries and in free-fermion systems, which differ in the geometry of their $k$-commutants. Ultimately, this work provides a general geometric framework for systematically studying observables in noisy systems with continuous symmetries, including Haar-random circuits.

</details>


### [149] [Operational identifiability of false-vacuum decay rates in the quantum Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11339)
*Boliang Yu,Ruixin Zhou,Hang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种多层级识别框架（multilevel identification framework），应用于一维量子Ising链中的假真空衰变（false-vacuum decay）实时张量网络模拟。在十二个参数点上，两-kink振幅可半定量预测无限链存活率与磁化动力学，但微观键响应呈现相干主导特征，热力学衰变率的解释仍需有限尺寸验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限时间量子动力学中提取热力学成核率（thermodynamic nucleation rate）需区分可观测衰变与估计器及有限尺寸有效性，现有方法难以同时保证定量准确性，因此需要系统化的多层级识别框架。

Method: 开发多层级识别框架，对一维量子Ising链的假真空衰变进行实时张量网络模拟；在十二个非空分析区间参数点上，利用相干两-kink振幅分别预测无限链存活率和磁化动力学，并引入匹配键维收敛准则和晶格分辨WKB作用量用于系统评估。

Result: 存活率系数中位格子-理论比值为0.902，磁化面积斜率比在0.809–0.953之间；真空-对相干贡献占最近邻键响应的60.0–81.5%；晶格分辨WKB作用量将固定前置因子差异降至4.13%。

Conclusion: 该框架可建立可靠的有限时间衰变系数，并识别出体热力学速率解释所需的额外有限尺寸及分支验证要求；定量跨级别一致性、可观测依赖的降阶模型误差与受限于有限尺寸验证的热力学速率解释可以共存。

Abstract: Extracting a thermodynamic nucleation rate from finite-time quantum dynamics requires separating observable decay from estimator and finite-size validity. We develop a multilevel identification framework to real-time tensor-network simulations of false-vacuum decay in the one-dimensional quantum Ising chain. Across twelve parameter points with non-empty analysis intervals, the same coherent two-kink amplitudes semi-quantitatively predict both infinite-chain survival and magnetization dynamics: the survival coefficient has a median lattice-to-theory ratio of 0.902, while the magnetization-area slope ratios span 0.809--0.953. By contrast, the microscopic nearest-neighbour bond response is coherence dominated: vacuum--pair coherence contributes 60.0--81.5\% within the seven parameter points satisfying the matched-bond-dimension convergence criterion, while substantial late-window slope discrepancies remain that cannot be removed by a scalar normalization. The framework establishes reliable finite-time decay coefficients and identifies the additional finite-size and branch-validation requirements for a bulk thermodynamic rate interpretation. Within the two-kink model, a lattice-resolved WKB action reduces the median fixed-prefactor discrepancy with the coherent-bubble spectral calculation to 4.13\%. Quantitative cross-level consistency, observable-dependent reduced-model error, and a thermodynamic-rate interpretation that remains subject to finite-size validation can therefore coexist.

</details>


### [150] [A piston-like polymer stochastic heat engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11507)
*Yi-Jui Chiu,Cheng-Hung Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于聚合物的随机热机（stochastic engine），通过在变温条件下将聚合物拉入窄通道的循环过程实现热到功的转换，其力-位置图对应传统热机的压强-体积图。数值结果表明该引擎在效率上满足普适低耗散界（universal low-dissipation bounds），在再生条件下恢复卡诺效率（Carnot efficiency），并表现出低耗散标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体随机热机虽被视为宏观活塞气缸热机的微观类比，但在实际应用中远不能直接作为力发生器。为此，研究者希望构建一种更接近活塞气缸引擎工作机制的随机热机，以弥补这一实用局限。

Method: 构建一种基于聚合物的随机热机，通过循环过程使聚合物在变温条件下被拉入和拉出窄通道，将热量转化为功。引擎所做的功直接从力-位置图中的循环轨迹获得，类似于传统热机的压强-体积图（pressure-volume diagram）。通过数值模拟分析其热力学性能。

Result: 尽管聚合物的自由度数目远高于胶体引擎，该聚合物引擎仍展现出胶体系统典型的若干特征。数值结果表明：效率与普适低耗散界一致，在再生（regeneration）条件下可恢复卡诺效率，且功与功率呈现低耗散标度行为。

Conclusion: 聚合物随机热机能够更真实地模拟活塞气缸引擎的运行方式，同时在小尺度随机系统中保留关键热力学普适特征，为微观力发生器的实际应用提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Colloidal stochastic engines are often regarded as microscopic analogues of macroscopic pis ton cylinder heat engines. However, although they share some underlying physical principles, such systems remain far from being direct force generators from a practical perspective. Motivated by this limitation, the present study introduces a polymer-based stochastic engine that more closely mimics the operation of piston cylinder engines. In this setup, heat is converted into work through a cyclic process in which a polymer is pulled into and out of a narrow channel under varying tem peratures. The work performed by the engine can be directly obtained from the cyclic trajectory in the force position diagram, analogous to the pressure volume diagram in traditional heat engines. Despite its much higher number of degrees of freedom compared to colloidal engines, the polymer en gine nevertheless follows several characteristic features observed in such systems. Numerical results demonstrate consistency with universal low-dissipation bounds for e ciency, recovery of Carnot e ciency under regeneration, and low-dissipation scaling of work and power.

</details>


### [151] [Macroscopic fluctuation theory for the multi-time statistics of current in non-stationary diffusive systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12119)
*Sabyasachi Chowdhury,Kapil Sharma,Sandeep Jangid,Tridib Sadhu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展宏观涨落理论（Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory, MFT）的涨落流体力学习框架，研究无限长扩散系统非平稳态下电流的多时统计（multi-time statistics）。针对非相互作用点阵气体和硬核布朗粒子等简单情形给出了多时大偏差统计的显式解，对一般系统采用微扰方法获得两时关联的显式结果，并揭示了电流涨落与分数布朗运动（fractional Brownian motion）之间的联系在阶跃初始条件下不再成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流涨落统计是非平衡物理学的核心研究对象，但现有工作大多集中于单时统计，多时推广的探索相对不足。本文旨在弥合这一空白，将MFT框架扩展到多时统计，以更全面地理解扩散系统的涨落行为。

Method: 将宏观涨落理论（MFT）的涨落流体力学习框架扩展到无界线上扩散系统非平稳态的多时统计。对非相互作用点阵气体与硬核布朗点粒子等简单情形给出MFT的显式解，得到多时大偏差统计；对一般系统采用微扰求解，推导两时关联的显式结果；并通过非相互作用气体和对称排斥简单过程（symmetric simple exclusion process, SSEP）的微观动力学精确求解以及数值模拟进行独立验证。

Result: 对简单系统获得了多时大偏差统计的显式解，对一般系统通过微扰方法得到了两时关联的解析结果。发现电流涨落与分数布朗运动之间的联系在平直初始条件下存在，但在阶跃（step）初始条件下不再保持。微观动力学精确解与数值模拟均独立验证了上述流体力学结论。

Conclusion: 本文成功将MFT拓展至多时统计领域，确立了初始条件对电流涨落与分数布朗运动关联结构的重要影响，为后续多时电流统计的研究提供了理论基础与分析工具。

Abstract: The statistics of current fluctuations has long been a central object of study in non-equilibrium physics. Most existing work has focused on one-time statistics, while their multi-time generalisation remains comparatively less explored. We address this gap by extending the fluctuating hydrodynamics framework of Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT) to study multi-time statistics in the non-stationary state of a diffusive system on an infinite line. For the simplest cases of a non-interacting lattice gas and hard-core Brownian point particles, we present explicit solution of the MFT leading to multi-time large-deviation statistics. For generic systems, the MFT is solved perturbatively, yielding explicit results for two-time correlations. These reveal that the connection between current fluctuations and fractional Brownian motion, previously observed for flat initial conditions, does not persist for step initial conditions. We independently verify these hydrodynamic results by solving the corresponding microscopic dynamics for the non-interacting gas and for the symmetric simple exclusion process. Additional confirmation comes from numerical simulations.

</details>


### [152] [Finite-depth scaling and an exact Bernoulli-leaf identity for the min-plus process on the binary tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12295)
*José Ricardo G. Mendonça*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对二叉树上的min-plus过程（一种随机凝聚-湮灭型过程）开展了大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，在有效树深达N=60的条件下，验证了p=1/2时根值X_N的Beta(2,1)拉伸指数极限，确认了次临界区的闭式结果及超临界区的均值增长，并针对Bernoulli初始条件变体给出了精确的序参量恒等式与吸收态相变位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: min-plus过程是数学、物理与计算机科学中最大值型递归分布方程（max-type recursive distributional equations）的一个易于处理的实例，但对其极限行为、临界点附近的精确性质以及超临界区的增长规律仍缺乏系统的数值验证。

Method: 采用大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，有效树深（effective tree depth）达N=60；利用逐层递归（level-wise recursion）和基于FFT的预计算叶表（FFT-based precomputed leaf table）技术，在保持递归树分布律的同时降低有效模拟深度。

Result: 在p=1/2时有限深度模拟佐证了根值X_N在非对称√N侧的Beta(2,1)拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）极限；次临界区验证了闭式结果P(X_∞=1)=(1-2p)/(1-p)；超临界区观测到均值增长超过初等下界(2p)^N。对Bernoulli(q)初始条件变体，在p=1/2处得到P(X_N=0)=q的精确恒等式，并将吸收态相变定位于算子混合概率p_c=1/2而非初始零密度。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持Chen、Duquesne与Shi以及Morfe提出的随机齐次系统分类框架，并揭示了超临界区均值增长的实际速率高于朴素下界；此外，所提出的模拟技术对大型树上相关递归方程的模拟具有潜在推广价值。

Abstract: The min-plus process is a stochastic coagulation-annihilation-type process on the binary tree, of interest in mathematics, physics, and computer science as a tractable instance of max-type recursive distributional equations. We carry out large Monte Carlo simulations at effective tree depths up to $N=60$ that provide finite-depth corroboration of the Beta(2,1) stretched-exponential limit for its root value $X_{N}$ at $p=1/2$, on the asymmetric $\sqrt{N}$ side of the random-homogeneous-systems classification recently introduced by Chen, Duquesne, and Shi and by Morfe. Off criticality, our simulations confirm the sub-critical closed form $\mathbb{P}(X_{\infty}=1)=(1-2p)/(1-p)$ within Monte Carlo error and document a super-critical mean growth exceeding the elementary $(2p)^{N}$ lower bound at the depths we reach. For a Bernoulli($q$)-initial-condition variant, we identify an elementary closed-form identity at $p=1/2$ that pins down the order parameter $\mathbb{P}(X_{N}=0)=q$ exactly, locates the absorbing-state phase transition at $p_{c}=1/2$ in the operator-mixing probability rather than in the initial-zero density, and shows that the conditional law on positives deforms substantially with $q$. Our simulations use a level-wise recursion and an FFT-based precomputed leaf table which reduce the effective simulation depth while preserving the recursive tree law and may be useful for the simulation of related recursive equations on large trees.

</details>


### [153] [Ideal heat engine cycles at maximal efficiency -- the ideal gas and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11966)
*Gregory Behrendt,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对给定热机循环，研究何种工作介质（working medium）能实现最高效率的问题。从依赖于摩尔体积和温度的多项式形式的亥姆霍兹势（Helmholtz potential）出发，推导出理想斯特林循环（Stirling cycle）、奥托循环（Otto cycle）和布雷顿循环（Brayton cycle）的精确表达式，发现最大效率普遍由工作介质对温度呈线性关系的热力学系统实现，包括理想气体、经典谐振子（harmonic oscillators）以及橡胶带的唯象模型（phenomenological models）等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者尝试回答一个看似简单但实际远比直觉复杂的问题：在给定热机循环下，何种工作介质能实现最高效率？虽然理想气体似乎是直觉上的答案，但实际上情况更为微妙，需要对工作介质的选择进行系统的理论分析。

Method: 从一般化的亥姆霍兹势出发，该势以摩尔体积和温度的多项式形式依赖，据此推导出理想斯特林、奥托和布雷顿循环的精确效率表达式，并通过对比不同工作介质的效率来寻找统一的规律。

Result: 研究发现，最大效率的达成具有相当普遍的普适性：工作介质对温度呈线性依赖的热力学系统能够取得最高的循环效率。该结论涵盖了理想气体，同时也适用于经典谐振子以及橡胶带的唯象模型等系统。

Conclusion: 理想气体并非唯一能够实现最高效率的工作介质；凡是工作介质对温度呈线性依赖的热力学系统，都能在斯特林、奥托和布雷顿等常见热机循环中取得普遍的最大效率。

Abstract: Given a particular heat engine cycle, what is the optimal working medium that results in the highest efficiency? While one might jump to the conclusion that it must surely be the ideal gas, the situation is actually more intricate. Starting with a general Hemlholtz potential that depends polynomially on molar volume and temperature we derive exact expressions for the ideal Stirling, Otto, and Brayton cycles. We find that quite universally the maximal efficiency is achieved for thermodynamics systems, whose working mediums are linear in temperature. This includes the ideal gas, but also classical harmonic oscillators and pheonmenological models of the rubber band.

</details>


### [154] [Shortcuts to Parameter Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12154)
*Chi Xiang,Guodong Cheng,Geng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出"参数扫描捷径"（Shortcuts to Parameter Sweeps, STPS）这一受控策略，通过引入辅助控制将概率密度沿预定的瞬时稳态族在有限时间内输运，从而在单次受控扫描中利用基于协方差的响应关系估计整个参数区间上的连续响应曲线，适用于平衡态和非平衡稳态体系，并以数值实验验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宽广参数范围内高效评估静态参数敏感性对于识别有影响力的训练数据、拟合力场（force field）以及预测材料响应至关重要，但传统的逐点方法需要进行反复的弛豫和采样，计算代价高昂。

Method: STPS作为一种工程化控制策略，利用辅助控制将概率密度（probability density）沿预先指定的一族瞬时稳态在有限时间内进行输运，并借助基于协方差的响应关系（covariance-based response relations）从单次受控扫描中估计整个参数区间上的连续响应曲线。该方法同时适用于平衡态和非平衡稳态系统，且无需已知稳态分布，可直接在高维场景中使用稳态样本实现。

Result: 对单粒子体系和相互作用多体体系的数值测试表明，STPS生成的响应曲线与参考结果高度一致，验证了该方法的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: STPS被确立为一种高效的、基于样本的连续敏感性分析框架，可在随机模拟中实现低成本的参数敏感性评估。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation of stationary parametric sensitivities over broad parameter ranges is important for identifying influential training data, fitting force fields, and predicting material responses, but standard pointwise approaches require repeated relaxation and sampling. Here we introduce Shortcuts to Parameter Sweeps (STPS), an engineered control strategy that uses an auxiliary control to transport the probability density along a prescribed family of instantaneous stationary states during a finite-time parameter sweep. This enables the continuous response curve over the full parameter interval to be estimated from a single controlled sweep using covariance-based response relations. STPS applies to both equilibrium and nonequilibrium steady-state systems, including those with unknown stationary distributions, and can be implemented directly using stationary samples in high-dimensional settings. Numerical tests on single-particle and interacting many-body systems show that STPS yields response curves in close agreement with reference results. These findings establish STPS as an efficient, sample-based framework for continuous sensitivity analysis in stochastic simulations.

</details>


### [155] [Interface phases and dynamics in two-dimensional quantum magnets: A "holographic" approach from universality to quantum simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12312)
*Abhishodh Prakash,Jaydev Singh Rao,Siddharth A. Parameswaran,Alessio Lerose*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用以对二维量子磁体中分隔有序体相的界面——等价于对偶格点规范理论中的限制弦——的量子相、相变及非平衡动力学进行分类，其核心是基于描述几何涨落的有效一维哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子磁体中的界面行为与对偶规范理论中的限制弦存在深层联系，但相关量子相结构及其非平衡动力学缺乏统一的分类框架。作者希望推广先前基于“全息”方法的工作，以揭示更丰富的量子相结构并探索可实验观测的物理量。

Method: 作者将[Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 120601 (2022)]中的“全息”方法重新诠释为一种精确玻色化（bosonization）方案，基于控制界面几何涨落的有效一维哈密顿量进行分类；通过二维波函数快照的统计性质区分不同界面相，并推导相应的时空标度律。

Result: 框架揭示出丰富的量子相结构：包括多种刚性和粗糙界面相，分别由一维有能隙（gapped）与无能隙（gapless）基态描述；预测了非平衡曲率驱动界面动力学在不同参数区间呈现不同的时空标度律；数值模拟中性原子阵列实验表明可直接测量所编码一维系统的电荷全计数统计量与对称性分辨性质。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为二维量子磁体界面的量子相与动力学提供了统一分类，还开辟了在现有实验中直接测量一维编码系统全计数统计和对称性分辨性质的未曾有过的实验途径。

Abstract: We introduce a framework to classify quantum phases, phase transitions, and non-equilibrium dynamics of interfaces separating ordered bulk domains in 2D quantum magnets - equivalently, confining strings in dual lattice gauge theories - based on effective 1D Hamiltonians governing geometric fluctuations. Building on a "holographic" approach from [Phys. Rev. Lett. 129, 120601 (2022)], here reinterpreted as an exact bosonization, we uncover a rich quantum phase structure, with a variety of stiff and rough interface phases described by gapped and gapless 1D ground states, respectively, all distinguishable through the statistics of 2D wave-function snapshots. Our framework allows us to predict distinct spatiotemporal scaling laws for non-equilibrium curvature-driven interface dynamics across parameter space, which can be readily probed in existing experiments. We finally show that our approach enables the unprecedented experimental opportunity of directly measuring charge full counting statistics and symmetry-resolved properties of an encoded 1D system, as we explicitly demonstrate by numerically simulating a neutral-atom array experiment.

</details>

### [156] [Quetzalcoatlite as a Disorder-Free Platform for Chiral Magnetism and Frustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11301)
*Aleksandar Razpopov,P. Peter Stavropoulos,Michael R. Norman,Roser Valentí*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次对天然矿物钾钒矿（quetzalcoatlite）Zn6Cu3(TeO6)2(OH)6·(AgxPbyClx+2y)进行了全面的从头算（ab initio）研究，揭示其作为结构理想kagome磁体的电子与磁性性质，发现其具有本征三维手性磁序，并指出适度压力、化学替换或结构修饰可能将体系推向量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然矿物钾钒矿在结构上是一种理想的kagome磁体，为探索几何阻挫（geometric frustration）、手性（chirality）与可调性之间的相互作用提供了一个无无序（disorder-free）框架，但目前尚缺乏对其电子与磁性性质的系统性认识。

Method: 采用全面的从头算方法对钾钒矿的电子结构与磁性性质进行第一性原理计算；通过将低能物理映射到海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）来分析磁交换相互作用。

Result: 电子结构由局域的半填充Cu dx²−y²轨道主导，并通过电子关联（electronic correlations）实现绝缘性。磁性主要由最近邻层内kagome耦合与次近邻层间耦合两种交换作用控制，其竞争稳定了非常规的三维手性磁态。每个kagome层呈现√3×√3有序，相邻层旋转60°，沿晶体c轴形成右手螺旋结构。

Conclusion: 钾钒矿固有手性磁序自然源于其晶体结构与磁相互作用，是完美kagome晶格上手性磁性的独特实现；其交换作用能标较小，体系接近竞争磁态边界，表明适度压力、化学替换或结构修饰可增强阻挫并可能驱动体系进入量子自旋液体态。

Abstract: The natural mineral quetzalcoatlite Zn$_6$Cu$_3$(TeO$_6$)$_2$(OH)$_6$ $\cdot$ (Ag$_x$Pb$_y$Cl$_{x+2y}$) is a structurally ideal kagome magnet, providing a platform for exploring the interplay of geometric frustration, chirality, and tunability in a disorder-free framework. Here, we present the (first) comprehensive ab initio study of its electronic and magnetic properties. The electronic structure is dominated by localized half-filled Cu $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals that become insulating through electronic correlations. Mapping the low-energy physics onto a Heisenberg model reveals that the magnetism is governed primarily by two exchange interactions: a nearest-neighbor intralayer kagome coupling and a next-nearest-neighbor interlayer coupling. Their competition stabilizes an unconventional three-dimensional chiral magnetic state. Each kagome layer hosts a $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ order, while adjacent layers are rotated by $60^\circ$, producing a right-handed spiral along the crystallographic $c$-axis. This intrinsic chiral order emerges naturally from the crystal structure and magnetic interactions, establishing quetzalcoatlite as a distinctive realization of chiral magnetism on a perfect kagome lattice. At the same time, the small energy scale of the exchange interactions places the material close to competing magnetic regimes, suggesting that moderate pressure, chemical substitution, or structural modifications may strongly enhance frustration, suppress long-range order, and potentially drive the system toward a quantum spin-liquid state.

</details>


### [157] [The Fate of Crystalline Topological Phenomena in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11302)
*Rajas Chari,Taylor L. Hughes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文运用代数拓扑（algebraic topology）与范畴论（category theory）方法，严格构造了有能隙玻色子相的连续极限（continuum limit），建立了晶格拓扑相与连续可逆拓扑相之间的满射全局映射，并发现部分晶格相不存在连续极限、部分不同晶格相共享同一连续像等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续极限是获取晶体系统连续场论（continuum field theory）的常用理论构造，但其是否保留拓扑性质尚不清楚。作者旨在严格化连续极限的表述，并系统探究晶格拓扑性质在连续化过程中的存留与坍缩问题。

Method: 利用代数拓扑和范畴论方法给出连续极限的严格定义，构造一个覆盖所有有限点群对称性的满射全局映射，将晶格拓扑相映射至连续可逆拓扑相（invertible topological phases）。该框架随后应用于多个具体实例，包括二维旋转对称相、高阶拓扑相（higher-order topological phases），以及反铁磁与价键固体退禁闭量子临界点（deconfined quantum critical point）的混合自旋-晶格反常。

Result: 研究发现：部分晶格相不存在连续极限；允许连续极限的不同晶格相可映射到同一连续像，即部分晶格拓扑数据会在连续化过程中坍缩；反之，每个连续可逆相均存在忠实的晶格实现（faithful crystalline realization）。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明连续极限并非总是保持晶格拓扑信息，晶格相与连续相之间存在复杂的映射关系，为理解晶格拓扑相与连续场论之间的对应提供了严格框架。

Abstract: The continuum limit is a widely used theoretical construct for obtaining continuum field-theories of crystalline systems. We study the formulation of a continuum limit for gapped bosonic phases and ask whether their topological properties survive passage to the continuum. Using methods in algebraic topology and category theory, we give a rigorous formulation of the continuum limit and construct a surjective global map relating crystalline topological phases across all finite point-group symmetries to continuum invertible topological phases. Consequently, we find that some lattice phases admit no continuum limit, while distinct lattice phases that do admit a continuum limit can share the same continuum image, hence implying that some lattice topological data can collapse. Conversely, we find that every continuum invertible phase admits a faithful crystalline realization. In addition to the general framework, we apply it to several examples, including studies of a 2D rotation-symmetric phase, higher-order topological phases, and the mixed spin-lattice anomaly of the deconfined quantum critical point between an antiferromagnet and valence bond solid.

</details>


### [158] [Thermodynamic Spectroscopy of Emergent Excitations in Quantum Spin Ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11305)
*Zhengbang Zhou,Tony An,Yong Baek Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种新型热力学探测方法，通过测量弱扰动共轭可观测量的温度导数，来探测量子自旋冰（quantum spin ice）中难以直接观测的环交换能标。该方法在非Kramers体系中表现为[100]与[010]方向热膨胀系数之差，在偶极-八极体系中表现为弱磁场下磁化强度的温度导数dM/dT，且符号可区分零通量（zero-flux）与π通量（π-flux）量子自旋冰态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋冰中的涌现激发（分数化自旋子、涌现光子、磁单极子）探测面临重大挑战：在非Kramers的Pr基体系中，低能峰被核Schottky异常遮蔽；在Ce基偶极-八极体系中，低能峰与自旋子贡献难以区分，因此需要寻找新的热力学探测手段。

Method: 在存在与赝自旋横向分量耦合的弱微扰条件下，分析与该弱微扰力共轭的可观测量的温度导数对环交换能标的敏感性。具体而言，在非Kramers体系中考察[100]与[010]方向热膨胀系数之差，在偶极-八极烧绿石体系中考察弱磁场下磁化强度的温度导数。

Result: 理论分析表明，[100]与[010]方向热膨胀系数之差在环交换能标处呈现峰值，可探测非Kramers量子自旋冰的环交换尺度；偶极-八极烧绿石中弱磁场下的dM/dT同样可检测该信号。此外，这些特征的符号能够区分零通量与π通量量子自旋冰态。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一种可实验实现的、对环交换能标高度敏感的新型热力学探针，有望解决量子自旋冰中涌现激发能量尺度难以直接测量的难题，并可作为区分不同通量态的实验判据。

Abstract: Quantum spin ice (QSI) is a three-dimensional quantum spin liquid where fractionalized spinons interact with emergent photons. The XXZ model on the pyrochlore lattice realizes such a $U(1)$ quantum spin liquid and a number of pyrochlore magnets have been investigated as candidate materials, but the detection of emergent excitations has been a major challenge. The specific heat is expected to show the higher-energy spinon excitations and a lower-energy anomaly at the ring-exchange scale, which sets both the photon bandwidth and the energy of the emergent magnetic monopoles (or visons). In reality, the lower-energy peak is obscured by the nuclear Schottky anomaly in non-Kramers Pr-based systems, while it is not clearly resolved from the spinon contributions in Ce-based dipolar-octupolar systems. In this work, we propose a novel thermodynamic probe of the elusive ring-exchange energy scale. We show that in the presence of a weak perturbation coupled to the transverse component of the pseudospin degrees of freedom, the temperature derivative of an observable conjugate to such a weak perturbing force is highly sensitive to the ring-exchange energy scale. Using this scheme, it is shown that the difference between thermal expansion coefficients along the [100] and [010] directions should show a peak at the ring-exchange energy scale for non-Kramers QSI. Similarly, the temperature derivative of the magnetization, $dM/dT$, of dipolar-octupolar pyrochlores under a weak magnetic field can also detect the same signal. Moreover, the sign of these signatures distinguishes the zero-flux and $π$-flux QSI states.

</details>


### [159] [Connecting the tensor-categorical formulation of anyon condensation with operator algebras and entropic order parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12157)
*Hua-Chen Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用Doplicher-Haag-Roberts双模（DHR bimodules）在拟局域C*-代数（quasi-local C*-algebras）上的形式体系来研究任意子凝聚（anyon condensation），该过程与某个算子代数的扩张相关联。作者通过直观的图示方式展示了张量范畴与算子代数两种表述之间的联系，并定义了一个熵序参量（entropic order parameter）作为表征凝聚的量子信息论度量，给出了其上界的简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子凝聚描述了拓扑量子场论（topological quantum field theory）之间的转变，可以分别用张量范畴（tensor categories）或算子代数（operator algebras）的语言表述。本文旨在将两种表述之间的概念联系以直观、图解化的方式阐明，并提供一个自然定义的量子信息论度量来刻画凝聚过程。

Method: 采用文献[1]中最近发展的拟局域C*-代数上的Doplicher-Haag-Roberts双模形式体系，将任意子凝聚与某个算子代数的扩张（extension）相关联，以此建立两种表述之间的联系。通过定义熵序参量作为量子信息论度量，并给出其上界的一个非常简洁的证明。

Result: 建立了张量范畴表述与算子代数表述之间概念联系的直观图示化展现，并自然定义了一个熵序参量作为表征任意子凝聚的量子信息论度量，同时给出了该序参量一个非常简洁的上界证明。

Conclusion: 该研究为任意子凝聚提供了一种统一的算子代数视角，使得两种已有表述的内在联系变得清晰，并提供了一种可量化的信息论指标及其紧致边界证明。

Abstract: Anyon condensation that describes the transition between topological quantum field theories can be formulated in the language of tensor categories or that of operator algebras. We deploy the formalism of Doplicher-Haag-Roberts bimodules over quasi-local $\mathrm{C}^{*}$-algebras recently developed in [1] to investigate anyon condensation, which is associated with an extension of a certain operator algebra. The connection between notions in the two formulations is thereby made manifest in an intuitive, diagrammatic manner. An entropic order parameter, as the quantum information-theoretic measure characterising a condensation, is naturally defined, and we give a very simple proof of a bound on it.

</details>


### [160] [Proliferation Transitions for Non-Abelian Anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12303)
*Sakura Schafer-Nameki,Yunqin Zheng,Andrea Antinucci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统构造二维加一维（2+1d）拓扑序中任意子凝聚相变的一般框架，核心工具是对称拓扑场论（Symmetry Topological Field Theory，SymTFT）。通过识别可凝聚任意子生成的透明线，将拓扑序嵌入到三维加一维（3+1d）SymTFT三明治结构中，并在对称边界上耦合标量场实现凝聚相变。该构造涵盖了阿贝尔和非阿贝尔理论，包括D(S₃)、SU(2)_k Chern-Simons理论，并推广至异常任意子情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚任意子引发的相变在一般拓扑序（包括非阿贝尔拓扑序）中缺乏系统性的构造方法，需要统一的框架来描述和实现这类相变过程。

Method: 以对称拓扑场论（SymTFT）为核心工具，对于给定的拓扑序，从由可凝聚任意子生成的透明线中识别相关对称性，从而以三明治结构实现该拓扑序；相变通过在SymTFT的对称边界上纯粹地耦合标量场至任意子来实现。

Result: 该构造成功应用于阿贝尔理论、非阿贝尔理论（如D(S₃)和SU(2)_k Chern-Simons理论），并进一步扩展到异常任意子的情形，验证了框架的一般性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种基于SymTFT的通用方法，能够系统地构造二维拓扑序中任意子凝聚相变，覆盖包括非阿贝尔和异常任意子在内的广泛情形。

Abstract: We construct phase transitions that proliferate condensable anyons in general 2+1d topological orders, including non-abelian ones. The central tool that provides a systematic approach to this question is the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT). For a given topological order, we identify the relevant symmetry from the transparent lines generated by the condensable anyons, and thereby realize the topological order in terms of a 3+1d SymTFT sandwich. The proliferation phase transition is realized by coupling scalar fields to the anyons purely on the symmetry boundary of the SymTFT. We illustrate the construction for abelian theories, as well as non-abelian ones, $D(S_3)$ and $SU(2)_k$ Chern-Simons theories, and extend it to anomalous anyons.

</details>


### [161] [Light-induced effective magnetic fields in Landau quantized graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11751)
*Hiroki Ueda,Alexej Pashkin,Ece Uykur,Kryštof Kašša,Filip Chudoba,Jan Kunc,Manfred Helm,Milan Orlita,Stephan Winnerl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过圆偏振光脉冲选择性激发石墨烯中非等距朗道量子态（Landau quantized states），演示了光诱导有效磁场（light-induced effective magnetic field）的产生机制。通过磁性调谐朗道能级跃迁共振，识别了光诱导磁信号的微观起源，建立了电子起源的逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）的清晰微观图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振辐射驱动的超快磁性是未来光自旋电子学和磁子学等技术的基础，但其动力学过程常因电子、自旋、声子、等离子激元、拓扑和晶格等多子系统之间在超快时间尺度上的多体量子耦合而变得复杂交织，亟需建立清晰的微观物理图像。

Method: 以石墨烯（graphene）这一狄拉克材料（Dirac material）为原型，使用圆偏振光脉冲对非等距朗道量子态进行选择性激发；通过磁调谐朗道能级跃迁共振至远离其他低能激发的区域，从而获得洁净的电学可调控平台，并测量瞬态法拉第旋转（Faraday rotation）信号。

Result: 研究发现不同朗道能级携带不同的光学霍尔电导（optical Hall conductivity），通过光激发直接改变其占据数会产生具有色散性磁场依赖关系的瞬态法拉第旋转信号，其线形与静态磁光光谱一致；所诱导的有效磁场经泵浦电场归一化后超过典型电子起源逆法拉第效应的报道值。

Conclusion: 实验结果建立了电子起源逆法拉第效应的清晰微观图像，表明当朗道能级跃迁与狄拉克系统中其他低能激发的能量重合时，可触发相关子晶格之间的层级动力学（hierarchical dynamics），为超快自旋控制提供了新的物理基础。

Abstract: Ultrafast magnetism triggered by circularly polarized radiation underpins ultrafast spin control, relevant to future technologies, e.g., opto-spintronics and magnonics. The dynamics are often complicated and intertwined among correlated subsystems, such as electrons, spins, phonons, plasmons, topology, and lattice, due to many-body quantum coupling at ultrafast timescales. Here, we demonstrate light-induced effective magnetic fields generated by selective excitation between non-equidistant Landau quantized states in graphene, a prototypical Dirac material, using circularly polarized pulses. By magnetically tuning the Landau-level transition resonance away from other low-energy excitations, we obtain a clean electrostatically controllable platform and identify the microscopic origin of the light-induced magnetic signals, independent of sublattice coupling. Because different Landau levels carry distinct optical Hall conductivities, direct modification of their occupancies via optical excitations creates transient Faraday rotation signals with dispersive magnetic-field dependence, mirroring the static magneto-optical lineshape. The induced effective magnetic field normalized by the pump electric field exceeds typical reported values for the inverse Faraday effect of electronic origin. Our results establish a clear microscopic picture of the inverse Faraday effect of electronic origin, which can trigger hierarchical dynamics among correlated sublattices once Landau-level transitions are magnetically tuned to coincide with other low-energy excitations in Dirac systems and related materials.

</details>


### [162] [Spin nematic liquid crystal and scalar spin chirality in tetragonal lattice YbMnBi$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11776)
*Yaofeng Xie,Sijie Xu,Yu Pan,Taekoo Oh,Tingjun Zhang,Masaaki Matsuda,Zhaoyu Liu,Zehao Wang,Yiheng Wang,Siyu Pan,Avishek Maity,Sylwia Pawledzio,Xiaoping Wang,Songxue Chi,Feng Ye,Yiqing Hao,Huibo Cao,Barry L. Winn,Melissa K. Graves-Brook,Shuai Wu,Fan Li,Xiaoyuan Zhou,Claudia Felser,Naoto Nagaosa,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过极化中子散射实验，在YbMnBi₂中观测到动态自旋向列相（dynamic spin nematic phase），并在外加面内磁场下，该相与Yb³⁺磁矩相互作用诱导出非零标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality），从而在该材料的顺磁态中产生反常霍尔效应（AHE）和反常能斯特效应（ANE）；对称性分析支持这一机制，为无磁有序的室温自旋电子学开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋向列相（spin nematic order）虽已在阻挫磁体和方晶格铱氧化物中被提出，但其对磁输运性质（magnetotransport）的影响此前完全未知；该研究旨在探究自旋向列相能否通过诱导标量自旋手性产生反常霍尔效应等磁输运现象。

Method: 采用极化中子散射（polarized neutron scattering）测量四方结构AMnBi₂（A=Ca, Yb）的磁结构和低能自旋激发；结合对称性基础的Ginzburg-Landau分析，考察自旋向列序与标量自旋手性之间的耦合项在外加磁场下的允许性。

Result: YbMnBi₂和CaMnBi₂均为严格c轴对齐的共线反铁磁体（C型），奈尔温度（T_N）分别约为290 K和270 K。YbMnBi₂在冷却至约400 K时形成各向异性的动态自旋向列相，而CaMnBi₂无此相；面内磁场下仅YbMnBi₂出现反常霍尔效应和反常能斯特效应；Ginzburg-Landau分析确认磁场下向列序与标量自旋手性的耦合可行。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供了动态标量自旋手性诱导反常霍尔效应和反常能斯特效应的有力证据，发生在补偿型共线反铁磁体的顺磁相中；这一发现为复合自旋序物理及无磁有序的室温自旋电子学（room-temperature spintronics）开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A spin nematic order, analogous to the nematic liquid crystal, characterizes the spontaneous breaking of spin-space rotational symmetry while preserving time-reversal ($T$) symmetry. In contrast, scalar spin chirality (SSC), a composite three-spin order, breaks $T$ symmetry and is known to induce an anomalous Hall effect (AHE). Although a spin nematic phase has been suggested in frustrated magnets and the square-lattice iridate, how it might affect magnetotransport properties is unknown. Here we use polarized neutron scattering to show that tetragonal $A$MnBi$_2$ ($A$ = Ca, Yb) is a strictly $c$-axis-aligned collinear antiferromagnet (C-type), with $T_N \approx 270$ K and 290 K, respectively. On cooling from 450 K to $T_N$, low-energy spin excitations in YbMnBi$_2$ spontaneously change from isotropic to anisotropic in spin space within the tetragonal plane, forming a dynamic spin nematic phase around 400 K due to heavy Yb-induced spin-orbit coupling, before gapping out below $T_N$. Similar measurements on CaMnBi$_2$ reveal isotropic paramagnetic scattering without a spin nematic phase above $T_N$. Under an in-plane magnetic field, the Yb$^{3+}$ moments may interact with the dynamic spin nematic phase to induce nonzero SSC, giving rise to AHE and an anomalous Nernst effect (ANE) in YbMnBi$_2$ that are absent in CaMnBi$_2$ above $T_N$. A symmetry-based Ginzburg-Landau analysis shows that coupling terms between the nematic order and SSC are allowed under an external magnetic field, which could explain the rapid increase of AHE with field in YbMnBi$_2$. Our results provide compelling evidence for dynamic SSC-induced AHE and ANE in the paramagnetic phase of a compensated collinear antiferromagnet, opening a new avenue for the physics of composite spin orders and room-temperature spintronics without magnetic order.

</details>


### [163] [Phonon anomalies and critical scaling in the spin-$1/2$ trimer chain Na$_2$Cu$_3$Ge$_4$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12061)
*P. Srikanth Patnaik,A. K. Bera,Srishti Bhardwaj,Tulika Maitra,Buddhananda Banerjee,Anushree Roy,S. M. Yusuf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过80至400 K的温度依赖拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）研究，揭示了低维量子磁体Na₂Cu₃Ge₄O₁₂（NCGO）中自旋-晶格耦合（spin-lattice coupling）诱导的准粒子激发与声子动力学临界标度行为，发现了T_c=167±1 K、临界指数β=0.24±0.02的声子重整化幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维量子磁体是探索自旋-晶格耦合介导的量子关联及涌现准粒子激发（emergent quasiparticle excitations）的理想平台，而NCGO中铜离子的反铁磁耦合自旋-1/2三聚体链具有与晶格振动能量尺度重叠的高能自旋激发，为研究自旋-声子协同效应提供了独特条件。

Method: 研究采用80至400 K温度范围的系统性温度依赖拉曼光谱实验，通过分析宽谱背景提取动态自旋磁化率（dynamic spin susceptibility），并结合声子峰的积分拉曼磁化率进行幂律拟合；此外，对80 K下记录的200个光谱进行矩阵范数（matrix-norm）和功率检验（power test）分析，量化声子能量本征值间的统计相关性。

Result: 研究发现：准粒子激发在170 K以下涌现；材料从正常顺磁态向关联量子磁态转变时呈现异常声子动力学交叉；声子模式的积分拉曼磁化率遵循I~|T-T_c|^β的幂律关系，T_c=167±1 K，β=0.24±0.02；200个光谱的统计相关性分析揭示了声子模式间意外的线性关联，表明集体晶格响应由自旋关联主导。

Conclusion: 综合宽谱背景与锐利声子峰的结果表明，交叉区域观察到的声子重整化（phonon renormalization）由动态自旋态驱动，确立了NCGO作为研究低维磁性材料中协同自旋-晶格耦合与声子动力学临界标度行为的模型系统。

Abstract: Low-dimensional quantum magnets provide an ideal platform to explore spin-lattice coupling-mediated quantum correlations, which give rise to emergent quasiparticle excitations. The antiferromagnetically coupled spin-1/2 trimer chain of copper ions in Na$_2$Cu$_3$Ge$_4$O$_{12}$ (NCGO) hosts high-energy spin excitations of different species, whose energy scales overlap with those of lattice vibrations. Here, we report a comprehensive temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopic study performed between 80 and 400 K. The dynamic spin susceptibility, as obtained from the analysis of the broad spectral background, reveals the emergence of quasiparticle excitations below 170 K. We further identify an unusual crossover of phonon dynamics when the material transits from a normal paramagnetic state to a correlated quantum magnetic state. A power law dependence of the integrated Raman susceptibility of the phonon modes, $I_{χ^{\prime\prime}}^{i}\sim|T-T_{c}|^β$, is observed with the critical temperature $T_c$=167$\pm$1 K, and critical exponent $β= 0.24\pm 0.02$. The combined results obtained from the broad spectral background and sharp phonon peaks further indicate that the phonon renormalization observed across the crossover is driven by dynamic spin states. Additionally, statistical correlations among phonon energy eigenvalues, quantified through matrix-norm and power-test analyses of 200 spectra recorded at 80 K, reveal an unexpected linear correlation among phonon modes, also indicating that the collective lattice response is governed by spin correlations. These findings establish NCGO as a model system for investigating cooperative spin-lattice coupling and critical scaling behavior of phonon dynamics in low-dimensional magnetic materials.

</details>


### [164] [Two routes to quantum anomalous Hall states in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12124)
*Makoto Naka,Shuntaro Sumita,Yukitoshi Motome,Hitoshi Seo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上提出在交错磁材料（altermagnetic materials）中实现量子反常霍尔态（quantum anomalous Hall state）的两条可行路径，分别是引入交错势场（staggered potential）和施加垂直磁场，分别产生Chern数C=1与C=2的拓扑交错磁相，并伴随布里渊区边界的能隙闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁（altermagnetism）作为一种新兴的磁性类型，同时具备零净磁化与自旋劈裂能带的特性，但在其中实现量子反常霍尔态（quantum anomalous Hall effect）的可能性尚未被系统探索。该工作旨在理论层面提出将拓扑平庸的交错磁态转化为拓扑非平庸态的具体机制与实验可行路径。

Method: 采用最小化正方晶格Hubbard模型（square-lattice Hubbard model），引入与正交晶体结构相关的反对称自旋轨道耦合（antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling）以支持拓扑平庸的交错磁态。在此基础上分别引入Rashba型自旋轨道耦合（Rashba-type spin-orbit coupling）并结合两类外部扰动——交错势场与垂直于二维平面的磁场——实现对拓扑相变的驱动；通过能隙闭合分析、Chern数计算以及条带几何（ribbon geometry）下的手性边缘态计算验证拓扑性质。

Result: 第一条路径由交错势场驱动，产生量化的霍尔电导|σxy|=e²/h、Chern数C=1的拓扑交错磁基态；第二条路径由垂直磁场实现，在磁滞回线（magnetic hysteresis loop）中形成Chern数C=2的亚稳态，其霍尔电导为|σxy|=2e²/h。两种拓扑转变均伴随布里渊区边界处特征性的能隙闭合，且能隙闭合点的数目决定了Chern数。条带几何计算显示手性边缘态与体拓扑不变量一致，且C=1与C=2态具有不同的自旋极化特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为在交错磁体中实现量化的反常霍尔响应提供了两条实验上可达成的路径，确立了从拓扑平庸交错磁态到拓扑非平庸态的完整理论方案。

Abstract: We theoretically propose two possible routes to realizing quantum anomalous Hall states in altermagnetic materials. We consider a minimal square-lattice Hubbard model with antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling associated with an orthorhombic crystal structure, which supports a topologically trivial altermagnetic state. By incorporating Rashba-type spin-orbit coupling and external perturbations, we demonstrate that this trivial state can be turned into topological altermagnetic phases in two distinct ways. The first route is driven by a staggered potential that breaks the symmetry connecting crystallographically equivalent sublattices, leading to a topological altermagnetic ground state characterized by a quantized Hall conductivity $\left| σ_{xy} \right|=e^2/h$ and a Chern number $C=1$. The second route is realized by applying a magnetic field perpendicular to the two-dimensional plane. The resulting topological state appears as a metastable state in the magnetic hysteresis loop, exhibiting a quantized Hall conductivity $\left| σ_{xy} \right|=2e^2/h$ associated with a Chern number $C=2$. We show that these topological transitions are accompanied by characteristic gap closings at the Brillouin-zone boundary, with the number of gap-closing points determining the Chern number. Ribbon-geometry calculations reveal chiral edge states consistent with the bulk topological invariants and demonstrate distinct spin polarizations between the $C=1$ and $C=2$ states. Our results establish experimentally accessible routes to quantized anomalous Hall responses in altermagnets.

</details>


### [165] [Probing Impurity Quantum Criticality with Entanglement Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12317)
*Mateo Cárdenes Wuttig,Andrew J. Millis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group）和数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）计算，证明可测量的自旋和电荷涨落可作为双杂质近藤模型（two-impurity Kondo model）量子临界性的纠缠见证（entanglement witness），建立了纠缠图像与实验可观测动力学响应函数之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠是量子力学的核心特征，但其与量子临界性（quantum criticality）的关系是当前研究的重要课题。作者希望通过双杂质近藤模型这一具有实验实现背景的系统，探索量子临界纠缠能否通过可测量的关联函数被检测，从而为理解重费米子（heavy-fermion）物理提供纠缠视角的洞察。

Method: 作者使用密度矩阵重整化群和数值重整化群计算方法，解析双杂质近藤模型中分离两个独立近藤屏蔽杂质与本间单态（inter-impurity singlet）的非费米液体临界点（non-Fermi-liquid critical point）。通过分析杂质-热浴纠缠和杂质间磁化率（inter-impurity susceptibility）的奇异响应，以及计算集体自旋和电荷算符在零温和有限温度下的量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information），建立纠缠图像与实验可观测量之间的对应关系。

Result: 研究表明，量子临界点对应每个局域矩主导纠缠伴侶的变化：从局域矩与扩展导带电子自由度的纠缠转变为与另一个杂质的纠缠。这一纠缠重组伴随着杂质-热浴纠缠和杂质间磁化率的奇异响应。量子Fisher信息中编码了相同的结构，从而将纠缠图像连接到实验上可访问的动力学响应函数。

Conclusion: 杂质系统为量子临界纠缠的可测量检测提供了受控的实验平台，可通过可测量的关联函数探测量子临界性。该项工作进一步为从纠缠角度理解重费米子物理提供了新的可能性和更深层的见解。

Abstract: Entanglement is a defining feature of quantum mechanics, and its relation to quantum criticality is of considerable current interest. Here we show that measurable spin and charge fluctuations provide an entanglement witness of quantum criticality in the two-impurity Kondo model, which has experimental realizations in terms of coupled quantum dots and magnetic impurities added to surfaces. We use density-matrix renormalization group and numerical renormalization group calculations to resolve the non-Fermi-liquid critical point separating two independently Kondo-screened impurities from an inter-impurity singlet and interpret it as a change in the dominant entanglement partner of each local moment: from entanglement of the local moment with an extended set of conduction-electron degrees of freedom to entanglement with the other impurity. This reorganization is accompanied by a singular response of the impurity-bath entanglement and the inter-impurity susceptibility. We show how the same structure is encoded in the quantum Fisher information of collective spin and charge operators at zero and finite temperatures, connecting the entanglement picture to experimentally accessible dynamical response functions. Our results establish impurity systems as controlled settings in which quantum-critical entanglement can be detected through measurable correlations, and provide further insight into the possibility of understanding heavy-fermion physics in terms of entanglement.

</details>


### [166] [Emergent heavy fermion and superconductivity near Mott transition in twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12319)
*Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在投影活动带极限（projective active-band limit）内提出了一个用于扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）整数填充的有效重费米子（heavy fermion）框架，通过混价莫特（mixed-valence Mott）物理统一描述费米速度与准粒子权重消失、Kondo屏蔽转变以及超导穹顶等涌现现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽调制的莫特转变（Mott transition）附近金属的费米速度 $v_F$ 与准粒子权重 $Z$ 往往趋于消失，作者希望探明类似现象能否在扭转双层石墨烯整数填充下自然涌现，并建立统一的物理描述框架。

Method: 在投影活动带极限内构造有效重费米子模型：电荷扇区中活动带 $c(\mathbf{k})$ 与涌现的正交费米子 $\psi(\mathbf{k})$ 杂化以打开莫特能隙和二次能带接触半金属；局部矩 $\psi'$ 与 $\psi$ 之间由涌现的 Kondo 耦合 $J_K \sim U$ 刻画，以扭转角 $\theta$ 作为莫特转变的调控参数；并引入反 Hund 耦合 $J_A$ 探讨配对对称性。

Result: 在 $\nu=0$ 时系统呈现二次能带接触半金属；偏离魔角后发展出具有消失准粒子权重 $Z$ 的 Kondo 屏蔽重半金属；反 Hund 耦合 $J_A$ 导致莫特边界附近的 s 波全能隙或节点型超导穹顶。在 $\nu=\pm1,\pm2$ 填充下，增大带宽首先将小能隙莫特态驱动至二次能带接触半金属，再进入具有大费米面的重费米液体。

Conclusion: 该框架为同时包含巡游载流子与来自 $f$ 轨道的局域矩的涌现重费米子物理提供了统一描述，将莫特转变、Kondo 屏蔽转变与超导统摄于同一理论图景之下。

Abstract: Near a bandwidth-tuned Mott transition, the Fermi velocity $v_F$ and quasiparticle residue $Z$ of a metal often vanish. Here, we show that analogous phenomena emerge in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) at integer fillings and can be captured by an emergent heavy-fermion framework within a projective active-band limit. Unlike models incorporating remote bands, our effective heavy-fermion description arises from \textit{mixed-valence Mott} physics via decoupled charge and local-moment sectors. In the charge sector, active bands $c(\mathbf{k})$ hybridize with an emergent \emph{orthogonal fermion} $ψ(\mathbf{k})$ to open a large Mott gap at $|\mathbf{k}| > k_*$ ($k_*$ sets the momentum-patch size) and a quadratic band-touching semimetal near $\mathbf{k}=0$ at neutrality ($ν=0$). The orthogonal fermion is a linear combination of the doublon and holon excitations and may be written as $ψ_i \sim (δn^f_i+\frac{1}{2})^{-1} f_i$. An emergent Kondo coupling $J_K \sim U$ ($U$ is the local Hubbard interaction) between $ψ$ and local moments $ψ'$ frames the Mott transition as a Kondo screening transition, tuned by the twist angle $θ$. Away from the magic angle, a Kondo-screened heavy semimetal develops below $T_K$ (the Kondo temperature) with vanishing $Z$. Introducing anti-Hund's coupling $J_A$ generates an s-wave fully gapped or nematic, nodally gapped superconducting dome near the Mott boundary even at $ν=0$. At other integer fillings $ν= \pm 1, \pm 2$, increasing bandwidth first drives the small-gap Mott state into an intermediate quadratic band-touching semimetal before entering a heavy Fermi liquid with large Fermi surfaces. Our results establish a unified framework for emergent heavy fermion physics with both itinerant carriers and local moments from the $f$ orbital.

</details>

### [167] [OpenMP Fortran programs for rotating dipolar Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11278)
*Denis Mujo,Dušan Vudragović,Paulsamy Muruganandam,Sadhan K. Adhikari*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提供了一套基于OpenMP并行框架的Fortran 90/95程序，用于求解二维和三维旋转偶极玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）的Gross-Pitaevskii方程，是作者先前发布的非旋转偶极BEC程序的升级版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期L. Klaus等人发表于Nature Physics的实验研究（2022）首次实现了旋转偶极BEC的观测，理论研究者亟需相应的数值工具来开展相关理论研究，因此需要对已有程序进行扩展以支持旋转情形。

Method: 算法采用分裂步半隐式Crank-Nicolson格式（split-step semi-implicit Crank-Nicolson scheme），分别通过虚时间传播（imaginary-time propagation）和实时间传播（real-time propagation）来获取稳态解和BEC动力学演化。程序基于OpenMP并行实现Fortran 90/95代码。

Result: 提供了可在二维和三维空间中求解旋转偶极BEC Gross-Pitaevskii方程的OpenMP并行Fortran 90/95程序，相较于先前版本新增了对旋转项的数值处理能力。

Conclusion: 该程序可作为旋转偶极BEC相关理论研究的实用工具，响应了近期实验进展对数值模拟工具的需求。

Abstract: In this paper we present Open Multi-Processing (OpenMP) Fortran 90/95 programs to solve the Gross-Pitaevskii equation for a rotating dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) in two and three dimensions, which is a new version of our previous published programs for a dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate without rotation. After the recent experimental study of a rotating dipolar BEC [L. Klaus et al., Nature Phys. 18, 1453 (2022)], the present programs will be useful tools for related theoretical investigation. The algorithm used is the split-step semi-implicit Crank-Nicolson scheme for imaginary- and real-time propagation to obtain stationary states and BEC dynamics, respectively, as in the previous version [L. E. Young-S. et al., Comput. Phys. Commun. 286 (2023) 108669].

</details>


### [168] [Fluctuation Spectra and Response Function of Coupled Atomic and Molecular BECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11642)
*Avinaba Mukherjee,Raka Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了原子-分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）通过费什巴赫共振（Feshbach resonance）耦合的非平衡性质，其中费什巴赫耦合和失谐受高斯白噪声（Gaussian white noise）影响。利用玻色约瑟夫森结和布洛赫球框架，发现耦合噪声与失谐噪声产生不同波动谱，且在共振处原子-分子杂化使线宽最大、有效温度和相位差最小，实现功率利用和品质因子的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索原子-分子BEC系统在噪声和周期驱动下的非平衡动力学特性，理解失谐、相干性和噪声如何共同调控系统行为，并评估系统的能量利用与品质因子表现。

Method: 采用玻色-约瑟夫森结（bosonic Josephson junction）理论框架和布洛赫球（Bloch sphere）描述，对含高斯白噪声的费什巴赫耦合与失谐进行建模，分析系统的波动谱、色散与吸收响应，并考察周期驱动下的系统行为。

Result: 耦合噪声和失谐噪声产生不同的波动谱，同时包含费什巴赫共振峰和对称的离共振峰；在费什巴赫共振处，原子-分子杂化使线宽最大化、有效温度与驱动场-系统相位差最小化，从而获得优化的功率利用和品质因子。

Conclusion: 原子-分子杂化在费什巴赫共振处对系统响应起到关键调控作用，耦合噪声与失谐噪声行为可区分，共振条件可同时优化线宽、有效温度与相位差，有利于提升系统的能量效率。

Abstract: We investigate out-of-equilibrium properties of atomic molecular Bose Einstein condensates coupled through a Feshbach resonance, with the Feshbach coupling and detuning subject to Gaussian white noise. Using a bosonic Josephson junction framework and a Bloch sphere description, we examine the interplay of detuning, coherence, and noise governing the system dynamics. Coupling and detuning noise produce distinct fluctuation spectra, featuring both Feshbach resonant and symmetric off resonant peaks. We characterize the dispersive and absorptive response of the atom dimer system under periodic driving. The atom molecule hybridization at the Feshbach resonance maximizes the linewidth and minimizes both the effective temperature and the phase difference between the driving field and the system. This leads to an optimized power utilization and quality factor.

</details>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Process-fracture mapping of a DLP-printed photopolymer using Bayesian active learning and surrogate-based sensitivity analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11594)
*Ethan Blackwell,Yogesh C. Chandrashekar,Guoqiang Li,Kshitiz Upadhyay*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的基于贝叶斯主动学习（Bayesian active learning）的框架，用于构建数字光处理（DLP）打印光聚合物的"工艺-断裂"映射关系，仅通过28组工艺条件（每组三次重复）的稀疏实验即可获得高精度代理模型和可靠的全局灵敏度分析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLP可快速制造聚合物结构，但其断裂性能取决于多个相互作用的工艺变量，穷举实验表征不可行，亟需数据高效的实验规划与映射方法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯主动学习框架结合高斯过程回归（Gaussian process regression, GPR）与修正的上置信界（upper confidence bound, UCB）类采集函数逐步选取实验条件；断裂阻力通过三点弯曲试验结合数字图像相关（digital image correlation, DIC）技术提取临界J积分（critical J-integral, J_c）量化；基于代理模型开展一次性扰动分析和全局Sobol灵敏度分析。

Result: 最终GPR代理模型训练拟合R²=0.99，留一交叉验证R²=0.63、Pearson系数r=0.81；Sobol分析表明UV曝光时间为主导工艺变量（一阶与总阶指数分别为0.6780和0.7581），各参数按总阶影响排序为UV曝光时间、层角度、打印温度、层高；一阶Sobol指数之和为0.8058，说明参数间存在不可忽略的高阶交互效应。

Conclusion: 该工作证明由贝叶斯主动学习引导的实验策略可从稀疏实验方案中高效恢复工艺-断裂关系及参数交互效应，为DLP打印材料性能的快速优化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Digital light processing (DLP) enables rapid fabrication of polymer structures, but fracture performance depends on multiple interacting processing variables, making exhaustive experimental characterization impractical. This work presents a data-efficient framework for process-fracture mapping of a DLP-printed photopolymer using Bayesian active learning and digital image correlation (DIC)-assisted Mode I fracture experiments. Four processing parameters were considered: layer angle, UV exposure time, layer height, and print temperature. Fracture resistance was quantified by the critical J-integral, $J_c$, obtained from three-point-bending tests with DIC-based evaluation of crack-mouth opening displacement and hinge-point kinematics. Beginning with two randomly selected conditions, Gaussian process regression (GPR) and a modified upper confidence bound (UCB)-style acquisition function selected 26 additional experiments, yielding 28 processing conditions with three replicates each. The final GPR surrogate reproduced the training data with $R^2=0.99$ and achieved leave-one-out cross-validation performance of $R^2=0.63$ and Pearson $r=0.81$. Surrogate-based sensitivity analysis quantified parameter effects and global contributions. One-at-a-time response curves revealed nonlinear conditional trends, while global Sobol analysis identified UV exposure time as the dominant processing variable, with first-order and total-order indices of 0.6780 and 0.7581, respectively. Based on total-order influence, the parameters ranked as UV exposure time, layer angle, print temperature, and layer height. The first-order Sobol indices summed to 0.8058, indicating non-negligible interaction and higher-order effects. These results demonstrate that Bayesian-active-learning-guided experimentation can efficiently recover process-fracture relationships and parameter interactions from a sparse experimental campaign.

</details>

### [170] [A determination of the backscattering probability of low-energy antiprotons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11795)
*GBAR Collaboration,K. Park,E. Perez,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,P. Cladé,M. Chung,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,A. Douillet,S. Geffroy,S. Guellati-Khelifa,P. Guichard,P. -A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,J. -P. Karr,B. Kim,S. Kim,E. -S. Kim,N. Kuroda,B. Lee,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,V. Martimort,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,N. Paul,P. Pérez,C. Regenfus,C. Roumegou,J. -Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt-Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. -P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文测量了GBAR实验中4和6 keV反质子（antiproton）在微通道板探测器表面的背散射概率，未发现背散射证据，并在68%置信水平下将其上限定为14%，同时讨论了背散射对反氢原子产生截面测定的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般认为反质子轰击材料表面后会立即与材料原子核湮灭，但在几个keV动能下，反质子可能像低能质子、电子或正电子一样在湮灭前发生大角度库仑散射，表现为"背散射"现象。这个背散射比例在很大程度上是未知的，而它是GBAR实验中测定反氢原子产生截面（production cross-section）的关键输入参数。

Method: 利用GBAR实验中用于束流成像的微通道板（Micro-Channel Plate，MCP）探测器，对4 keV和6 keV能量的反质子轰击其表面的背散射概率进行了测定。

Result: 实验中未发现反质子背散射的证据，并在68%置信水平下将背散射概率的上限定为14%。

Conclusion: 该上限值表明背散射效应对GBAR实验中参与反氢原子产生的反质子数量的影响有限，论文还具体讨论了背散射对确定参与反氢产生的反质子数目的影响。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that antiprotons impinging on a material surface annihilate promptly with the nuclei of the material. However, at kinetic energies of a few keV, this assumption may not hold. As with low-energy protons, electrons or positrons that can be reflected from a target, they may undergo large-angle Coulomb scattering before annihilation occurs, thereby appearing to be "backscattered" from the material surface. This backscattering fraction, largely unknown, is a crucial ingredient to the determination of the production cross-section of antihydrogen atoms in the GBAR experiment. This paper presents a determination of the probability that 4 and 6 keV antiprotons backscatter on the surface of a Micro-Channel Plate detector used for beam imaging at GBAR. No evidence for backscattering has been found and an upper limit of 14% at 68% confidence level has been set on this probability. The impact of backscattering on the determination of the number of antiprotons that participate in antihydrogen production in GBAR is also addressed.

</details>


### [171] [Toward a Measurement of the Higgs Boson Mass with Natural-Width Precision at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12071)
*Jan Eysermans,Ang Li,Gregorio Bernardi,Christoph Paus,Emmanuel Perez,Michele Selvaggi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文表明，利用轻子ZH反冲（recoil）道，未来环形对撞机(FCC-ee)可将希格斯玻色子质量测量精度提升至4 MeV（含统计与系统不确定性），从而以优于1%的精度确定希格斯耦合，并支持通过共振希格斯玻色子产生来探测电子汤川耦合（electron Yukawa coupling）。研究还识别了实现该精度所需的探测器与加速器性能条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子质量的亚10 MeV精度测量能够实现亚百分级的希格斯耦合测定，并避免希格斯质量成为电弱拟合中的限制输入因素。同时，通过共振希格斯产生探测电子汤川耦合需要与希格斯玻色子自然宽度（约4 MeV）相当的测量精度，因此需要探索可以达到这一精度的对撞机方案与实验条件。

Method: 利用轻子ZH反冲道（leptonic ZH recoil channels）进行希格斯玻色子质量测量，研究中包含统计误差与系统误差的综合评估，并进一步识别达到4 MeV精度所需的探测器与加速器性能要求。

Result: 研究显示FCC-ee可通过轻子ZH反冲道达到4 MeV的希格斯玻色子质量测量精度（包括统计与系统不确定性），该精度可与希格斯玻色子约4 MeV的自然宽度相匹配。

Conclusion: 4 MeV级希格斯质量测量精度可以实现电子汤川耦合的共振探测，使希格斯质量不再成为电弱精确拟合的限制输入；同时本文明确了达成该目标的探测器与加速器性能基准。

Abstract: Higgs boson mass measurements with sub-10 MeV precision enable sub-percent determinations of Higgs boson couplings and prevent the Higgs boson mass from becoming a limiting input to electroweak fits. Probing the electron Yukawa coupling through resonant Higgs boson production requires a precision comparable to the Higgs boson natural width of approximately 4 MeV. Using the leptonic ZH recoil channels, we show that FCC-ee can reach a Higgs boson mass precision of 4 MeV, including statistical and systematic uncertainties, thereby enabling this unique measurement. We identify the detector and accelerator performance required to reach this precision.

</details>


### [172] [Laboratory constraints on peV-scale mass splitting between ordinary and sterile neutron states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12173)
*N. J. Ayres,Z. Berezhiani,G. Bison,K. Bodek,V. Bondar,P. -J. Chiu,M. Daum,C. B. Doorenbos,S. Emmenegger,K. Kirch,V. Kletzl,J. Krempel,B. Lauss,D. Pais,I. Rienäcker,D. Ries,D. Rozpędzik,P. Schmidt-Wellenburg,K. S. Tanaka,J. Zejma,N. Ziehl,G. Zsigmond*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子-镜中子（mirror neutron）振荡的参数空间，通过调节外加磁场在5–360 μT范围内匹配有限质量劈裂的共振条件，推导出中子-镜中子振荡时间常数的排除极限（约20秒），覆盖质量差范围0.3–22 peV，且在部分参数区间比中子星冷却天体物理界限更为严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行“镜”扇区（mirror sector）所代表的惰性物质态可能贡献于宇宙中观测到的暗物质。此前对该场景的研究未充分考虑镜扇区内部相互作用以及两个态非质量简并的情况，因此需要系统考察相关参数空间。

Method: 考虑了镜扇区内的相互作用，并假设中子与镜中子两个态不必然质量简并。通过将外加磁场强度在5 μT < B < 360 μT范围内调节至与有限质量劈裂对应的共振条件，从而对n-n'振荡现象进行实验约束。

Result: 推导出n-n'振荡时间常数的排除极限可达约20秒，覆盖质量差范围0.3–22 peV。在该参数范围的某些部分，所获得的极限优于模型依赖的中子星冷却界限（neutron-star-cooling bound）。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在此场景下提供了比中子星冷却天体物理估计更严格的实验约束，对验证镜扇区暗物质假说具有重要实验意义。

Abstract: Sterile states of matter, represented by a parallel ``mirror'' sector, may contribute to the observed dark matter in the Universe. We investigated the parameter space of neutron $(n)$ to mirror-neutron $(n')$ oscillations, in the case where the two states are not necessarily mass-degenerate, taking into account interactions in the mirror sector. By tuning the magnitude of an applied magnetic-field in the range $5~μ\mathrm{T} < B < 360~μ\mathrm{T}$ to corresponding resonance conditions for finite mass splitting, we derive exclusion limits for the $n-n'$ oscillation time constant reaching about $20~\text{s}$ over the mass-difference range $0.3 - 22~\text{peV}$. In parts of this parameter range, our limits exceed the model-dependent neutron-star-cooling bound, providing the first experimental constraints in this scenario that are more stringent than this astrophysical estimate.

</details>

Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb实验在质心能量7、8和13 TeV下记录的质子-质子对撞数据（总积分亮度8.4 fb⁻¹），利用勒让德多项式（Legendre polynomials）构造B⁰→K*⁰(→K⁺π⁻)μ⁺μ⁻衰变振幅，在q²区间1.1–8.0 GeV²/c⁴内提取CP平均观测量，发现若干观测量偏离标准模型（Standard Model）预期，并可将这种偏差解释为有效Wilson系数（Wilson coefficient）C₉的移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要检验标准模型在稀有衰变中的预言，并区分强子效应与超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的新物理贡献。传统的唯象分析在q²分箱选择上缺乏灵活性，因此需要一种模型无关（model-independent）的分析方法。

Method: 利用勒让德多项式构造B⁰→K*⁰μ⁺μ⁻的衰变振幅，在μ⁺μ⁻不变质量平方区间1.1<q²<8.0 GeV²/c⁴内展开。从振幅中提取CP平均观测量，并在模型无关框架下将这些观测量与标准模型预言对比，以测试理论预言并启发对Wilson系数偏移的解释。

Result: 部分CP平均观测量相对于标准模型出现偏差。该偏差可被解释为有效Wilson系数C₉的移动，且具体显著性依赖于q²分箱方案，范围从4.3σ到4.8σ不等。

Conclusion: 这种模型无关的方法允许在全局分析中灵活选择q²分箱，有助于区分强子效应与新物理贡献。Wilson系数C₉相对于标准模型预期的显著偏离（最高达4.8σ）为可能的新物理信号提供了证据支持。

Abstract: An analysis of the decay amplitudes of $B^0 \to K^{*0}(\to K^+π^-)μ^+μ^-$ is presented, using proton-proton collision data recorded by the LHCb experiment at centre-of-mass energies of 7, 8, and 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 8.4 fb$^{-1}$. The amplitudes are constructed from Legendre polynomials in the $μ^+μ^-$ invariant mass squared region $1.1<q^2<8.0$ GeV$^2/c^4$. $C\!P$-averaged observables are obtained from the amplitudes. Some of these observables present deviations with respect to the Standard Model, which can be interpreted as shifts in the effective Wilson coefficients. This model-independent approach enables tests of theoretical predictions that can help disentangle hadronic effects from potential contributions from physics beyond the Standard Model. This allows flexibility in the choice of $q^2$ binning for global analyses. Depending on the binning scheme, the deviation of the Wilson coefficient $C_9$ from its Standard Model expectation varies from $4.3σ$ to $4.8σ$.

</details>


### [174] [Measurement of the $\bar ν_μ-$Hydrogen Charged-Current Quasi-Elastic Cross Section using the NOvA Near Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12293)
*The NOvA Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在NOvA近探测器中利用NuMI束流1.2×10²¹质子打靶样本，对μ子反中微子在氢上的带电电流准弹性散射（$\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n$）总截面进行的高统计量测量，得到平均能量1.9 GeV下截面为0.538 × 10⁻³⁸ cm²，精度为迄今最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前（反）中微子-氢（hydrogen）相互作用的实验数据缺乏高统计量和高精度测量，需要精确的截面数据来检验理论并约束反中微子通量（flux）。

Method: 利用NOvA近探测器对氢含量为10.8%的目标进行基于拓扑（topological）和运动学（kinematic）约束的事件选择，获得35,509个信号事件；并使用专门的数据控制样本来约束重核上的（反）中微子相互作用背景，以减小相关系统不确定度。

Result: 测量得到总截面 $σ(\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n) = 0.538 \pm 0.009\ ({\rm stat}) \pm 0.010\ ({\rm syst}) \pm 0.055\ ({\rm flux}) \times 10^{-38}$ cm²（平均能量1.9 GeV），为迄今最精确的该过程总截面测量；事件统计量为此类（反）中微子-氢相互作用测量中的最高纪录。

Conclusion: 统计与非线性系统不确定度之和比通量不确定度小四倍以上，表明该测量未来可用于约束绝对反中微子通量。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the total cross section for muon antineutrino charged-current quasi-elastic scattering on hydrogen, $\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n$, in the NOvA near detector using a $1.2\times10^{21}$ proton-on-target exposure in the NuMI beam. A selection based on topological and kinematic constraints yields 35,509 signal events in the hydrogen-rich ($10.8\%$) detector, providing the highest statistics of (anti)neutrino--hydrogen interactions measured to date. Backgrounds from (anti)neutrino interactions on heavier nuclei are constrained using dedicated data control samples, significantly reducing the related systematic uncertainties. We obtain a value $σ(\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n) = 0.538 \pm 0.009 ({\rm stat}) \pm 0.010 ({\rm syst}) \pm0.055 ({\rm flux}) \times 10^{-38}$ cm$^2$ for the total cross section at an average energy of 1.9 GeV, the most precise total cross-section measurement of this process to date. The combined statistical and non-flux systematic uncertainty is more than four times smaller than the flux uncertainty, allowing a future use of this measurement to constrain the absolute $\bar ν_μ$ flux.

</details>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Temperature-Dependent Performance of NaI(Tl) Crystal with Dual-Channel SiPM Readout for Low-Mass Dark Matter Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11303)
*W. K. Kim,H. Y. Lee,K. W. Kim,H. S. Lee*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文首次报道了由两个硅光电倍增管（SiPM）直接耦合至晶体两端读取的NaI(Tl)晶体的温度依赖性表征。在94–293 K范围内，光产额在238 K时达到17.7 ± 1.1 光电子/keV，较室温提升34.5%；结合双通道符合触发有效抑制随机热噪声，为暗物质和中微子相干弹性散射搜索降低能量阈值提供了关键路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温运行是降低暗物质和中微子相干弹性散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering）搜索能量阈值的潜在途径，但此前缺乏对SiPM直接耦合NaI(Tl)晶体在宽温度范围内的系统表征，因此需要建立温度依赖性的实验数据。

Method: 将一块6 mm × 6 mm × 13 mm的NaI(Tl)晶体两端直接耦合两个硅光电倍增管，在液氮冷却低温恒温器中于94–293 K温度范围内进行表征；使用241Am源的γ射线峰测量光产额、能量分辨率和闪烁衰减时间，并对光学串扰（optical crosstalk）贡献进行修正。

Result: 经光学串扰修正后，光产额在238 K时达到17.7 ± 1.1 光电子/keV，较室温（293 K）提升34.5%。双通道配置通过符合触发（coincidence trigger）有效抑制随机热噪声。

Conclusion: 低温下光产额提升与双通道符合触发抑制噪声相结合，为暗物质（dark matter）和中微子相干弹性散射搜索降低能量阈值提供了关键途径。

Abstract: We report the first temperature-dependent characterization of a NaI(Tl) crystal readout by two silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs) directly coupled to opposite ends of the crystal for rare-event searches. A $6 \text{ mm} \times 6 \text{ mm} \times 13 \text{ mm}$ NaI(Tl) crystal was directly coupled to two SiPMs and characterized in a liquid nitrogen-cooled cryostat over a temperature range of 94$-$293 K. The light yield, energy resolution, and scintillation decay time were measured using $γ$-ray peak from a $^{241}$Am source. After correcting for optical crosstalk contributions, the light yield increased, reaching $17.7 \pm 1.1$ photoelectrons/keV at 238 K, corresponding to a 34.5% enhancement relative to room temperature (293 K). Furthermore, dual-channel configuration effectively suppresses random thermal noise via coincidence triggers, which together with the observed increase in light yield, provides a critical pathway toward lowering the energy threshold for dark matter and coherent elastic neutrino$-$nucleus scattering searches.

</details>


### [176] [Development and Initial Performance of an Upgraded NaI(Tl) Crystal Encapsulation for COSINE-100U](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11678)
*Doohyeok Lee,Jae Young Cho,Chang Hyon Ha,Eunju Jeon,Hongjoo Kim,Jinyoung Kim,Kyungwon Kim,SungHyun Kim,Sun Kee Kim,Won Kyung Kim,Yeongduk Kim,Young Ju Ko,Hyunseok Lee,Hyun Su Lee,In Soo Lee,Jaison Lee,Seo Hyun Lee,Seung Mok Lee,Reina H. Maruyama,Jong-Chul Park,Kangsoon Park,Kihong Park,Se Dong Park,Kyungmin Seo,Min Ki Son,Gyun Ho Yu*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文介绍了COSINE-100U实验的晶体封装（crystal-encapsulation）升级方案。该方案取消了石英光学窗口，通过2毫米厚硅胶光学垫片将光电倍增管（PMT）直接耦合到晶体端面，并通过晶边倒角提升光收集效率。基于2462小时（102.6天）室温数据，所有八根晶体的光产额均高于COSINE-100，范围在15.8至27.7光电子/千电子伏（p.e./keV），六根超过20 p.e./keV；表面α事例率显著降低，并恢复了此前因光学性能不佳而被排除的两根晶体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COSINE-100实验旨在通过低本底NaI(Tl)探测器检验DAMA/LIBRA的年调制信号（annual-modulation claim）主张。为实现升级以提升对低质量暗物质（low-mass dark matter）的灵敏度，需要在不损害长期探测器稳定性的前提下提高光的收集效率并降低表面相关本底。

Method: 设计了一种新的晶体封装系统：移除COSINE-100中使用的石英光学窗口，通过2毫米厚硅胶光学垫片将PMT直接耦合至晶体端面以减少光学界面数量；对较大晶体进行晶边倒角以引导闪烁光更高效地进入3英寸高量子效率PMT。使用2462小时（102.6天）室温运行数据以及2023年3月末698小时（29.1天）的COSINE-100参考数据进行性能与本底对比研究。

Result: 所有八根晶体的光产额均高于COSINE-100，数值介于15.8至27.7 p.e./keV，其中六根超过20 p.e./keV。体α（bulk-α）事例率低于COSINE-100测量值，与内部²¹⁰Pb的预期时间演化一致；1–2 MeV表面α（surface-α）事例率大幅降低。升级同时恢复了两根此前因光学性能差而从COSINE-100物理分析中排除的晶体。

Conclusion: 独立的验证测试表明，该封装在低温下长期浸没于液体闪烁体（liquid scintillator）中仍保持机械稳健和光学稳定。本文报告了在Yemilab设施实施的封装设计、室温探测器性能以及表面相关本底的降低效果，为提升低质量暗物质探测灵敏度提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: The COSINE-100 experiment was designed to test the DAMA/LIBRA annual-modulation claim using low-background NaI(Tl) detectors. For the COSINE-100U upgrade, we developed a new crystal-encapsulation system to increase light-collection efficiency while preserving long-term detector stability, thereby improving sensitivity to low-mass dark matter. The upgraded design eliminates the quartz optical windows used in COSINE-100 and directly couples the photomultiplier tubes (PMTs) to the crystal end faces through 2-mm-thick silicone optical pads, thereby reducing the number of optical interfaces. For the larger crystals, the crystal edges were beveled to guide scintillation light more efficiently onto 3-inch high-quantum-efficiency PMTs. The performance study uses 2462~h (102.6~days) of room-temperature COSINE-100U data and, for direct background comparisons, reference COSINE-100 data acquired near the end of operation. 698~h (29.1~days) of COSINE-100 data acquired near the end of operation in March 2023. All eight crystals showed higher light yields than in COSINE-100, with values ranging from 15.8 to 27.7~p.e./keV; six crystals exceeded 20~p.e./keV. The measured bulk-$α$ rates were lower than the COSINE-100 values and consistent with the expected time evolution of internal $^{210}$Pb, while the 1--2-MeV surface-$α$ rates were substantially reduced. The upgrade also restored two crystals that had previously been excluded from the COSINE-100 physics analysis because of poor optical performance. Independent validation tests demonstrated that the encapsulation remains mechanically robust and optically stable during long-term immersion in liquid scintillator at low temperature. This paper presents the encapsulation design, the room-temperature detector performance, and the reduction in surface-related backgrounds achieved at the Yemilab facility.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Predicting Mechanical Properties of Lignin-Containing Polyurethane Rigid Foams from Microstructure Using Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11447)
*Ilige S. Hage,Charbel Y. Seif,Jose Enrico Q. Quinsaat,Daniel J. van de Pas,Richard Vendamme,Walter Eeversd,Karolien Vanbroekhovend,Elias Feghalid*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习方法，通过扫描电子显微镜（SEM）图像训练卷积神经网络，实现含木质素刚性聚氨酯泡沫力学性能的无损预测，R²值达0.850–0.91，并通过Grad-CAM可视化确认模型聚焦于物理上有意义的微观结构区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物基刚性泡沫材料因可持续性和竞争性性能成为传统泡沫的良好替代品，但其制造过程复杂且破坏性测试常不可行，因此需要探寻微观结构与力学性能之间的关联方法。

Method: 以不同种类和比例的木质素基多元醇部分替代传统多元醇，制备含木质素的刚性聚氨酯泡沫；采集SEM图像与对应力学压缩数据，训练自定义双头卷积神经网络（CNN），以加权多输出损失函数优化，分别预测密度、比压缩模量、比屈服强度和比压缩强度。

Result: CNN在预测密度、比压缩模量、比屈服强度和比压缩强度方面表现优异，R²值介于0.850至0.91之间，相关系数高于0.92，平均绝对误差百分比低于9%；Grad-CAM可视化显示网络关注细胞壁和支柱节点等具有物理意义的微观结构区域。

Conclusion: 所提出的CNN可视为一种可解释、无损且数据驱动的框架，能够预测和理解生物基聚氨酯泡沫的力学行为，从而减少耗时制造和破坏性测试带来的不便。

Abstract: Bio-based alternatives for conventional rigid foams have proven to be good substituents owing to their enhanced sustainability and competitive performance. However, because their manufacturing processes are complex and destructive testing is often impractical, this study investigates whether microstructural features can be correlated with mechanical properties in lignin-containing rigid polyurethane (PU) foams using machine-learning approaches. Various types and percentages of lignin-based polyols were investigated as a partial polyol replacement. Scanning electron microscopy (SEM) images and corresponding mechanical compression data were used to train a custom state-of-art dual-head convolutional neural network (CNN) targeting the specific prediction of density, specific compression modulus, specific yield strength, and specific compression strength. The CNN was optimized with a weighted multi-output loss function, achieving strong predictive performance R2 values ranging from 0.850 to 0.91 and correlation coefficients above 0.92 while maintaining mean absolute error percentages below 9%. This proves the trained network capability to predict and capture morphological features governing load bearing responses. On the other hand, Grad-CAM visualization revealed that the network focused its predictions on physically meaningful microstructural regions such as cell walls and strut junctions, which confirm that the proposed network can be classified as an interpretable, non-destructive and data-driven framework for predicting and understanding bio-based PU foams mechanical behavior, hence reducing the inconvenience caused by time consuming manufacturing and destructive testing.

</details>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Quantitative Unique Continuation on Simplex and $\mathbb{Z}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11602)
*Shihe Liu,Yunfeng Shi,Zhifei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对任意维度 $n \geq 3$ 的 $n$ 维单纯形晶格（simplex lattice）$\Delta^{(n)}_{N}$ 以及整数格 $\mathbb{Z}^n$ 建立了定量唯一延拓（quantitative unique continuation）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散格点上研究定量唯一延拓性质，这是唯一延拓问题从连续型偏微分方程向离散图结构推广的重要方向，此前结果可能仅覆盖低维或特定格点类型。

Method: 针对 $n$-维单纯形晶格 $\Delta^{(n)}_{N}$ 与整数格 $\mathbb{Z}^n$，对任意 $n \geq 3$ 建立定量唯一延拓结果。

Result: 成功在任意维度 $n \geq 3$ 下，为单纯形晶格 $\Delta^{(n)}_{N}$ 和整数格 $\mathbb{Z}^n$ 这两种格点结构建立了定量唯一延拓性质。

Conclusion: 定量唯一延拓性质在两类离散格点上获得了任意高维的推广，为该性质在离散分析中的进一步应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, we establish the quantitative unique continuation on both $n$-dimensional simplex lattice $Δ^{(n)}_{N}$ and $\mathbb{Z}^n$ for arbitrary $n\geq 3$.

</details>


### [179] [Sharp instability of planar isotropic--nematic interfaces in the Landau--de Gennes model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12131)
*Wei Wang,Qin Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Landau–de Gennes模型中带各向异性弹性常数L的一维平面各向同性–向列相界面的稳定性。作者去除了先前工作中证明L<0时不稳定所需的额外条件，证明在整个 -3/2<L<0 范围内，对角平面类（diagonal planar class）中任意非负约化能量极小值子在一般一维扰动下均不稳定，从而确立了L=0是从负L侧看不稳定范围的尖锐端点。此外，文章定义了最优稳定性指标，并修正了先前界面轮廓中因子二归一化不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究仅在附加条件下证明了Landau–de Gennes模型中L<0时一维平面各向同性–向列相界面的不稳定性。本文的动机是移除这一多余条件，彻底确定负L范围内界面不稳定的完整区间，并澄清L=0作为临界端点的地位。

Method: 作者在Landau–de Gennes模型中，于对角平面类约束下考虑约化能量（reduced energy），对任意非负极小值子建立一般一维扰动下的不稳定性分析。通过直接分析临界算子（critical operator）在L=0处的非负性，并结合整个负L区间的不稳定性论证，作者还引入了最优稳定性指标（optimal stability index）来刻画稳定性边界，同时校正了此前界面轮廓公式中的因子二归一化误差。

Result: 作者去除了先前的附加条件，证明在整个 -3/2<L<0 范围内，对角平面类中的任何非负约化能量极小值子在一般一维扰动下均不稳定。该结果确立了L=0是从负L侧看不稳定区间的尖锐端点（sharp endpoint）：L=0处临界算子非负，而约化问题在整个负L范围内均不稳定。此外，作者定义了最优稳定性指标，并修正了先前界面轮廓中因子二归一化不一致的问题。

Conclusion: 研究工作确定了Landau–de Gennes模型中界面不稳定性的精确边界L=0，填补了此前在负L区间证明的空白，并提供了更清晰的最优稳定性刻画。同时，文中对先前界面轮廓归一化的修正进一步完善了该模型的数学表述。

Abstract: We study the stability of one-dimensional planar isotropic--nematic interfaces in the Landau--de Gennes model with anisotropic elastic constant $L$. Earlier work proved the instability for $L<0$ only under an extra condition. We remove this condition and prove that any non-negative minimizer of the reduced energy within the diagonal planar class is unstable under general one-dimensional perturbations throughout $-\frac{3}{2}<L<0$. This result shows that $L=0$ is the sharp endpoint of the instability range from the negative-$L$ side: the critical operator is non-negative at $L=0$, while instability holds over the full negative-$L$ range of the reduced problem. We also define the optimal stability index and correct a factor-of-two normalization inconsistency in a previously stated interface profile.

</details>
